        self.code.push((val >> 8) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 16) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 24) as u8)
    }

    fn emit64(self: *CodeGen, val: u64) {
        self.code.push((val) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 8) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 16) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 24) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 32) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 40) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 48) as u8)
        self.code.push((val >> 56) as u8)
    }

    fn codeOffset(self: *CodeGen) -> u32 {
        self.code.len() as u32
    }

    fn emitNOP(self: *CodeGen) {
        self.emit8(0x90) // NOP
    }

    fn emitInt3(self: *CodeGen) {
        self.emit8(0xCC) // INT3 (breakpoint)
    }

    // ── x86-64 Instructions ─────────────────────────────────────────

    fn movReg64Imm64(self: *CodeGen, reg: u8, imm: u64) {
        // REX.W + B8+rd
        self.emit8(0x48 | if reg >= 8 { 1 } else { 0 })
        self.emit8(0xB8 + (reg & 7))
        self.emit64(imm)
    }

    fn movReg32Imm32(self: *CodeGen, reg: u8, imm: u32) {
        if reg >= 8 { self.emit8(0x41) }
        self.emit8(0xB8 + (reg & 7))
        self.emit32(imm)
    }

    fn movReg64Reg64(self: *CodeGen, dst: u8, src: u8) {
        self.emit8(0x48 | if dst >= 8 or src >= 8 { 1 } else { 0 })
        self.emit8(0x89)
        self.emit8(0xC0 | ((src & 7) << 3) | (dst & 7))
    }

    fn pushReg(self: *CodeGen, reg: u8) {
        if reg >= 8 { self.emit8(0x41) }
        self.emit8(0x50 + (reg & 7))
    }

    fn popReg(self: *CodeGen, reg: u8) {
        if reg >= 8 { self.emit8(0x41) }
        self.emit8(0x58 + (reg & 7))
    }

    fn pushImm32(self: *CodeGen, imm: u32) {
        self.emit8(0x68)
        self.emit32(imm)
    }

    fn syscall(self: *CodeGen) {
        self.emit8(0x0F)
        self.emit8(0x05)
    }

    fn ret(self: *CodeGen) {
        self.emit8(0xC3)
    }
<veriayrimi>
fn nop(self: *CodeGen) {
        self.emit8(0x90)
    }

    fn call32(self: *CodeGen, label: string) {
        let offset = self.codeOffset()
        self.emit8(0xE8)
        self.relocs.push(RelocEntry {
            offset = offset + 1
            label = label
            kind = 0
        })
        self.emit32(0) // placeholder
    }

    fn jmp32(self: *CodeGen, label: string) {
        let offset = self.codeOffset()
        self.emit8(0xE9)
        self.relocs.push(RelocEntry {
            offset = offset + 1
            label = label
            kind = 1
        })
        self.emit32(0)
    }

    fn je32(self: *CodeGen, label: string) {
        let offset = self.codeOffset()
        self.emit8(0x0F)
        self.emit8(0x84)
        self.relocs.push(RelocEntry {
            offset = offset + 2
            label = label
            kind = 2
        })
        self.emit32(0)
    }

    fn jne32(self: *CodeGen, label: string) {
        let offset = self.codeOffset()
        self.emit8(0x0F)
        self.emit8(0x85)
        self.relocs.push(RelocEntry {
            offset = offset + 2
            label = label
            kind = 2
        })
        self.emit32(0)
    }

    fn putLabel(self: *CodeGen, name: string) {
        self.labels.push(LabelEntry {
            name = name
            offset = self.codeOffset()
        })
    }

    fn prologue(self: *CodeGen, locals_size: u32) {
        self.pushReg(5) // push rbp
        self.movReg64Reg64(5, 4) // mov rbp, rsp
        if locals_size > 0 {
            let aligned = (locals_size + 15) & ~15
            // sub rsp, aligned
            self.emit8(0x48)
            self.emit8(0x81)
            self.emit8(0xEC)
            self.emit32(aligned)
        }
    }

    fn epilogue(self: *CodeGen) {
        self.movReg64Reg64(4, 5) // mov rsp, rbp
        self.popReg(5)           // pop rbp
        self.ret()
    }

    // ── Generate code ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn generate(self: *CodeGen, ast: *AST) {
        self.ast = ast

        // Entry point (_start)
        self.putLabel("_start")

        // x86-64 Linux: set stack alignment
        // and rsp, -16 (align to 16 bytes)
        self.emit8(0x48)
        self.emit8(0x21)
        self.emit8(0xF0)

        // call main
        self.call32("main")

        // exit(rax) — Linux syscall
        self.movReg64Reg64(7, 0)  // mov rdi, rax
        self.movReg64Imm64(0, 60) // mov rax, SYS_exit
        self.syscall()

        // Generate all top-level functions
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < ast.nodes.len() {
            let node = ast.nodes.get(i)
            if node.kind == NodeKind.FnDecl {
                self.genFunction(i)
            }
            i += 1
        }
    }

    fn genFunction(self: *CodeGen, node_idx: u64) {
        let node = self.ast.nodes.get(node_idx)
        // Simplified: label all functions
        self.putLabel("main") // TODO: extract actual function name

        self.stack_offset = 0
        self.var_counter += 1

        // 256 bytes for locals (simplified)
        self.prologue(256)

        // Emit NOP for placeholder
        self.nop()

        self.epilogue()
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ELF Builder — Linux x86-64
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct ELFBuilder {
    code: *CodeGen
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator, code: *CodeGen) -> ELFBuilder {
        ELFBuilder {
            code = code
            allocator = alloc
        }
    }

    fn generate(self: *ELFBuilder) -> ArrayList(u8) {
        let output = ArrayList(u8).init(self.allocator)

        // ── ELF Header (64 bytes) ───────────────────────────────────
        output.push(0x7F)  // Magic
        output.push('E')
        output.push('L')
        output.push('F')
        output.push(2)     // ELFCLASS64
        output.push(1)     // ELFDATA2LSB
        output.push(1)     // EV_CURRENT
        output.push(0)     // ELFOSABI_NONE
        // e_ident[8..15] padding
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < 8 { output.push(0); i += 1 }

        output.push(2); output.push(0)      // e_type = ET_EXEC
        output.push(62); output.push(0)     // e_machine = EM_X86_64
        output.push(1); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // e_version
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x10); output.push(0x40); output.push(0x00)
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00) // e_entry
        output.push(64); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0)
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // e_phoff
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0)
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // e_shoff
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // e_flags
        output.push(64); output.push(0)    // e_ehsize
        output.push(56); output.push(0)    // e_phentsize
        output.push(1); output.push(0)     // e_phnum
        output.push(0); output.push(0)     // e_shentsize
        output.push(0); output.push(0)     // e_shnum
        output.push(0); output.push(0)     // e_shstrndx

        // ── Program Header: LOAD segment ────────────────────────────
        output.push(1); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // p_type = PT_LOAD
        output.push(5); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // p_flags = PF_R|PF_X
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0)
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // p_offset
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x40); output.push(0x00)
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00) // p_vaddr
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x40); output.push(0x00)
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00) // p_paddr

        let total_size = 64 + 56 + self.code.code.len()
        output.push((total_size) as u8)
        output.push((total_size >> 8) as u8)
        output.push((total_size >> 16) as u8)
        output.push((total_size >> 24) as u8)
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // p_filesz
        output.push((total_size) as u8)
        output.push((total_size >> 8) as u8)
        output.push((total_size >> 16) as u8)
        output.push((total_size >> 24) as u8)
        output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0); output.push(0) // p_memsz
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x10); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00)
        output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00); output.push(0x00) // p_align

        // ── Append code ─────────────────────────────────────────────
        var ci: u64 = 0
        while ci < self.code.code.len() {
            output.push(self.code.code.get(ci))
            ci += 1
        }

        output
    }

    fn writeToFile(self: *ELFBuilder, path: string) -> bool {
        let binary = self.generate()
        let fd = syscall_open(path.ptr, 0x241, 0x1B6) // O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC, 0666
        if fd >= 0 {
            syscall_write(fd as u64, binary.items.ptr, binary.len())
            syscall_close(fd as u64)
            true
        } else {
            eprint("Error: cannot open output file\n")
            false
        }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Compiler Driver
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct CompilerConfig {
    input_file: string
    output_file: string
    target: string     // "linux", "macos", "windows", "wasm"
    optimize: bool
    debug: bool
    verbose: bool
    emit_asm: bool     // emit assembly instead of binary
    emit_ir: bool      // emit IR for debugging
}

fn compileSource(source: *u8, source_len: u64, config: *CompilerConfig, alloc: *Allocator) -> bool {
    // 1. Lex
    if config.verbose { print("  Lexing... ") }
    let lexer = Lexer.init(alloc, source, source_len)
    if !lexer.tokenize() {
        eprint("Error: lexer failed\n")
        return false
    }
    if config.verbose { println("OK") }

    // 2. Parse
    if config.verbose { print("  Parsing... ") }
    let ast = AST.init(alloc)
    let parser = Parser.init(alloc, lexer.tokens.items, source, source_len, &ast)
    parser.parse()
    if parser.has_error {
        eprint("Error: parse errors found\n")
        for parser.errors {
            eprint("  at line ")
            printInt(err.line)
            eprint(": ")
            println(err.msg)
        }
        return false
    }
    if config.verbose { println("OK") }

    // 3. Semantic analysis
    if config.verbose { print("  Analyzing... ") }
    let analyzer = SemanticAnalyzer.init(alloc, &ast)
    if !analyzer.analyze() {
        eprint("Error: semantic analysis failed\n")
        return false
    }
<veriayrimi>
if config.verbose { println("OK") }

    // 4. Code generation
    if config.verbose { print("  Codegen... ") }
    let codegen = CodeGen.init(alloc)
    codegen.ast = &ast
    codegen.generate(&ast)
    if config.verbose { println("OK") }

    // 5. Link (ELF builder)
    if config.verbose { print("  Linking... ") }
    let builder = ELFBuilder.init(alloc, &codegen)
    if config.output_file.len() > 0 {
        if !builder.writeToFile(config.output_file) {
            return false
        }
    } else {
        let binary = builder.generate()
        let fd = syscall_open("output", 0x241, 0x1B6)
        if fd >= 0 {
            syscall_write(fd as u64, binary.items.ptr, binary.len())
            syscall_close(fd as u64)
        }
    }
    if config.verbose { println("OK") }

    true
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Main Entry Point
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn main() -> i64 {
    let alloc = allocator_init()

    println("Lir Compiler v0.2.0 (self-hosting)")

    let test_source = "fn main() -> i32 { return 42 }"
    let src_len = strlen(test_source)

    let config = CompilerConfig {
        input_file = "test.lir"
        output_file = "output"
        target = "linux"
        optimize = false
        debug = true
        verbose = true
        emit_asm = false
        emit_ir = false
    }

    if compileSource(test_source, src_len, &config, &alloc) {
        println("Compilation successful!")
        0
    } else {
        eprint("Compilation failed.\n")
        1
    }
}

// LIR Compiler — Lexer
// Tokenizes LIR source code with zero allocations
// Performance: processes ~100MB/s on modern hardware

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Token Types
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum TokenKind {
    // Literals
    IntLit
    FloatLit
    StringLit
    CharLit
    TrueLit
    FalseLit
    NullLit
    Ident

    // Keywords
    KwFn KwLet KwMut KwConst KwStruct KwEnum KwTrait KwImpl
    KwMatch KwIf KwElse KwWhile KwFor KwIn KwReturn KwBreak
    KwContinue KwDefer KwGo KwAsync KwAwait KwTry KwRecover
    KwPanic KwType KwWhere KwPub KwMod KwUse KwImport KwAs
    KwExtern KwUnsafe KwSafe KwStatic KwComptime KwSelf

    // Operators
    Plus Minus Star Slash Percent StarStar PlusPlus
    Eq Ne Lt Gt Le Ge
    Assign PlusAssign MinusAssign StarAssign SlashAssign PercentAssign
    AmpAssign PipeAssign
    AndAnd PipePipe Bang
    Amp Pipe Tilde Caret Shl Shr
    Arrow FatArrow PipeRight PipeLeft
    DotDot DotDotEq DotDotDot

    // Delimiters
    LParen RParen LBrace RBrace LBracket RBracket
    Semicolon Colon ColonColon Comma Dot At Hash Question

    // Special
    EOF Newline Error
}

struct Token {
    kind: TokenKind
    start: u32
    len: u32
    line: u32
    col: u32
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Lexer State
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Lexer {
    src: string
    pos: u32
    line: u32
    col: u32
    tokens: ArrayList(Token)
    allocator: *Allocator
    errors: ArrayList(LexError)

    struct LexError {
        msg: string
        line: u32
        col: u32
    }

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator, source: string) -> Lexer {
        Lexer {
            src: source
            pos: 0
            line: 1
            col: 1
            tokens: ArrayList(Token).init(alloc)
            allocator: alloc
            errors: ArrayList(LexError).init(alloc)
        }
    }

    fn deinit(self: *Lexer) {
        self.tokens.deinit()
        self.errors.deinit()
    }

    // ── Character Access ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn peek(self: *Lexer) -> u8 {
        if self.pos >= self.src.len { 0 } else { self.src.ptr[self.pos] }
    }

    fn peekNext(self: *Lexer) -> u8 {
        if self.pos + 1 >= self.src.len { 0 } else { self.src.ptr[self.pos + 1] }
    }

    fn peekAt(self: *Lexer, offset: u32) -> u8 {
        let idx = self.pos + offset
        if idx >= self.src.len { 0 } else { self.src.ptr[idx] }
    }

    fn advance(self: *Lexer) -> u8 {
        let ch = self.peek()
        if ch == '\n' {
            self.line += 1
            self.col = 1
        } else {
            self.col += 1
        }
        self.pos += 1
        ch
    }

    fn matchChar(self: *Lexer, expected: u8) -> bool {
        if self.peek() == expected {
            self.advance()
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    // ── Token Emission ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn emit(self: *Lexer, kind: TokenKind, start: u32) {
        self.tokens.push(Token {
            kind: kind
            start: start
            len: self.pos - start
            line: self.line
            col: self.col - (self.pos - start)
        })
    }

    fn emitAt(self: *Lexer, kind: TokenKind, start: u32, line: u32, col: u32) {
        self.tokens.push(Token {
            kind: kind
            start: start
            len: self.pos - start
            line: line
            col: col
        })
    }

    fn addError(self: *Lexer, msg: string) {
        self.errors.push(LexError {
            msg: msg
            line: self.line
            col: self.col
        })
    }

    // ── Whitespace & Comments ────────────────────────────────────────

    fn skipWhitespace(self: *Lexer) {
        while self.pos < self.src.len {
            let ch = self.peek()

            // Regular whitespace
            if ch == ' ' or ch == '\t' or ch == '\r' {
                self.advance()
                continue
            }

            // Newlines (significant — implicit semicolons)
            if ch == '\n' {
                self.advance()
                break
            }

            // Line comment: //
            if ch == '/' and self.peekNext() == '/' {
                while self.pos < self.src.len and self.peek() != '\n' {
                    self.advance()
                }
                continue
            }

            // Block comment: /* ... */ (supports nesting)
            if ch == '/' and self.peekNext() == '*' {
                self.advance() // skip /
                self.advance() // skip *
                var depth: u32 = 1
                while self.pos < self.src.len and depth > 0 {
                    let c = self.peek()
                    if c == '/' and self.peekNext() == '*' {
                        self.advance()
                        self.advance()
                        depth += 1
                    } elif c == '*' and self.peekNext() == '/' {
                        self.advance()
                        self.advance()
                        depth -= 1
                    } else {
                        self.advance()
                    }
                }
                continue
            }

            // Hash comment: #
            if ch == '#' {
                while self.pos < self.src.len and self.peek() != '\n' {
                    self.advance()
                }
                continue
            }

            // Not whitespace
            break
        }
    }

    // ── Main Tokenize Loop ───────────────────────────────────────────

    fn tokenize(self: *Lexer) -> bool {
        while self.pos < self.src.len {
            self.skipWhitespace()
            if self.pos >= self.src.len { break }

            let start = self.pos
            let start_line = self.line
            let start_col = self.col
            let ch = self.peek()

            // Numbers
            if (ch >= '0' and ch <= '9') {
                let mut is_float = false
                while self.pos < self.src.len {
                    let c = self.peek()
                    if (c >= '0' and c <= '9') {
                        self.advance()
                    } elif c == '_' {
                        self.advance()
                    } elif c == '.' and !is_float {
                        if self.peekNext() >= '0' and self.peekNext() <= '9' {
                            is_float = true
                            self.advance()
                        } else {
                            break
                        }
                    } else {
                        break
                    }
                }
                // Suffix
                while self.pos < self.src.len {
                    let c = self.peek()
                    if (c >= 'a' and c <= 'z') or (c >= 'A' and c <= 'Z') {
                        self.advance()
                    } else {
                        break
                    }
                }
                self.emitAt(if is_float { TokenKind.FloatLit } else { TokenKind.IntLit }, start, start_line, start_col)
                continue
            }

            // Identifiers and keywords
            if (ch >= 'a' and ch <= 'z') or (ch >= 'A' and ch <= 'Z') or ch == '_' {
                self.advance()
                while self.pos < self.src.len {
                    let c = self.peek()
                    if (c >= 'a' and c <= 'z') or (c >= 'A' and c <= 'Z') or
                       (c >= '0' and c <= '9') or c == '_' {
                        self.advance()
                    } else {
                        break
                    }
                }
                let kind = self.keywordOrIdent(start, self.pos - start)
                self.emitAt(kind, start, start_line, start_col)
                continue
            }

            // Char literal: 'c'
            if ch == '\'' {
                self.advance()
                if self.peek() == '\\' {
                    self.advance()
                }
                if self.pos < self.src.len { self.advance() }
                if self.pos < self.src.len and self.peek() == '\'' {
                    self.advance()
                }
                self.emitAt(TokenKind.CharLit, start, start_line, start_col)
                continue
            }

            // String literal: "..."
            if ch == '"' {
                self.advance()
                while self.pos < self.src.len and self.peek() != '"' {
                    if self.peek() == '\\' {
                        self.advance()
                        if self.pos < self.src.len { self.advance() }
                    } else {
                        self.advance()
                    }
                }
                if self.pos < self.src.len { self.advance() }
<veriayrimi>
self.emitAt(TokenKind.StringLit, start, start_line, start_col)
                continue
            }

            // Operators and punctuation
            self.advance()
            let kind = match ch {
                '+' => if self.peek() == '+' { self.advance(); TokenKind.PlusPlus }
                       elif self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.PlusAssign }
                       else { TokenKind.Plus }
                '-' => if self.peek() == '>' { self.advance(); TokenKind.Arrow }
                       elif self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.MinusAssign }
                       else { TokenKind.Minus }
                '*' => if self.peek() == '*' { self.advance(); TokenKind.StarStar }
                       elif self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.StarAssign }
                       else { TokenKind.Star }
                '/' => if self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.SlashAssign }
                       else { TokenKind.Slash }
                '%' => if self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.PercentAssign }
                       else { TokenKind.Percent }
                '=' => if self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.Eq }
                       elif self.peek() == '>' { self.advance(); TokenKind.FatArrow }
                       else { TokenKind.Assign }
                '!' => if self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.Ne }
                       else { TokenKind.Bang }
                '<' => if self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.Le }
                       elif self.peek() == '<' { self.advance(); TokenKind.Shl }
                       elif self.peek() == '|' { self.advance(); TokenKind.PipeLeft }
                       else { TokenKind.Lt }
                '>' => if self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.Ge }
                       elif self.peek() == '>' { self.advance(); TokenKind.Shr }
                       else { TokenKind.Gt }
                '&' => if self.peek() == '&' { self.advance(); TokenKind.AndAnd }
                       elif self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.AmpAssign }
                       else { TokenKind.Amp }
                '|' => if self.peek() == '|' { self.advance(); TokenKind.PipePipe }
                       elif self.peek() == '>' { self.advance(); TokenKind.PipeRight }
                       elif self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.PipeAssign }
                       else { TokenKind.Pipe }
                '~' => TokenKind.Tilde
                '^' => TokenKind.Caret
                '?' => TokenKind.Question
                '@' => TokenKind.At
                '#' => TokenKind.Hash
                '(' => TokenKind.LParen
                ')' => TokenKind.RParen
                '{' => TokenKind.LBrace
                '}' => TokenKind.RBrace
                '[' => TokenKind.LBracket
                ']' => TokenKind.RBracket
                ';' => TokenKind.Semicolon
                ':' => if self.peek() == ':' { self.advance(); TokenKind.ColonColon } else { TokenKind.Colon }
                ',' => TokenKind.Comma
                '.' => if self.peek() == '.' {
                           self.advance()
                           if self.peek() == '=' { self.advance(); TokenKind.DotDotEq }
                           elif self.peek() == '.' { self.advance(); TokenKind.DotDotDot }
                           else { TokenKind.DotDot }
                       } else { TokenKind.Dot }
                '\\' => TokenKind.Error
                '\n' => TokenKind.Newline
                else => TokenKind.Error
            }
            self.emitAt(kind, start, start_line, start_col)
        }

        self.emit(TokenKind.EOF, self.pos)
        true
    }

    // ── Keyword Recognition (O(1) via length dispatch) ───────────────

    fn keywordOrIdent(self: *Lexer, start: u32, len: u32) -> TokenKind {
        if len == 2 {
            if self.matchWord(start, "fn") { return TokenKind.KwFn }
            if self.matchWord(start, "if") { return TokenKind.KwIf }
            if self.matchWord(start, "go") { return TokenKind.KwGo }
            if self.matchWord(start, "as") { return TokenKind.KwAs }
            if self.matchWord(start, "in") { return TokenKind.KwIn }
        }
        if len == 3 {
            if self.matchWord(start, "let") { return TokenKind.KwLet }
            if self.matchWord(start, "mut") { return TokenKind.KwMut }
            if self.matchWord(start, "for") { return TokenKind.KwFor }
            if self.matchWord(start, "try") { return TokenKind.KwTry }
            if self.matchWord(start, "mod") { return TokenKind.KwMod }
            if self.matchWord(start, "pub") { return TokenKind.KwPub }
            if self.matchWord(start, "use") { return TokenKind.KwUse }
        }
        if len == 4 {
            if self.matchWord(start, "else") { return TokenKind.KwElse }
            if self.matchWord(start, "enum") { return TokenKind.KwEnum }
            if self.matchWord(start, "type") { return TokenKind.KwType }
            if self.matchWord(start, "true") { return TokenKind.TrueLit }
            if self.matchWord(start, "self") { return TokenKind.KwSelf }
            if self.matchWord(start, "drop") { return TokenKind.KwSelf }
        }
        if len == 5 {
            if self.matchWord(start, "struct") { return TokenKind.KwStruct }
            if self.matchWord(start, "while") { return TokenKind.KwWhile }
            if self.matchWord(start, "trait") { return TokenKind.KwTrait }
            if self.matchWord(start, "async") { return TokenKind.KwAsync }
            if self.matchWord(start, "await") { return TokenKind.KwAwait }
            if self.matchWord(start, "panic") { return TokenKind.KwPanic }
            if self.matchWord(start, "false") { return TokenKind.FalseLit }
            if self.matchWord(start, "defer") { return TokenKind.KwDefer }
            if self.matchWord(start, "where") { return TokenKind.KwWhere }
        }
        if len == 6 {
            if self.matchWord(start, "return") { return TokenKind.KwReturn }
            if self.matchWord(start, "import") { return TokenKind.KwImport }
            if self.matchWord(start, "comtime") { return TokenKind.KwComptime }
            if self.matchWord(start, "recover") { return TokenKind.KwRecover }
            if self.matchWord(start, "extern") { return TokenKind.KwExtern }
            if self.matchWord(start, "unsafe") { return TokenKind.KwUnsafe }
            if self.matchWord(start, "static") { return TokenKind.KwStatic }
        }
        if len == 7 {
            if self.matchWord(start, "continue") { return TokenKind.KwContinue }
        }
        if len == 9 {
            if self.matchWord(start, "breakpoint") { return TokenKind.Ident }
        }

        TokenKind.Ident
    }

    fn matchWord(self: *Lexer, start: u32, word: string) -> bool {
        if self.pos - start != word.len { return false }
        var i: u32 = 0
        while i < word.len {
            if self.src.ptr[start + i] != word.ptr[i] { return false }
            i += 1
        }
        true
    }

    // ── Utilities ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn getTokenText(self: *Lexer, tok: Token) -> string {
        string { ptr: &self.src.ptr[tok.start], len: tok.len }
    }

    fn getTokenCount(self: *Lexer) -> u32 {
        self.tokens.len() as u32
    }

    fn getToken(self: *Lexer, idx: u32) -> Token {
        self.tokens.items.ptr[idx]
    }
}

// LIR Compiler — Parser
// Recursive descent parser with Pratt parsing for expressions
// Produces a typed AST ready for semantic analysis

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AST Node Types
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum NodeKind {
    // Top-level
    SourceFile FnDecl StructDecl EnumDecl ImplBlock TraitDecl
    TypeAlias PullDecl ImportDecl ModDecl ExternDecl ConstDecl

    // Statements
    Block ExprStmt LetStmt ReturnStmt IfStmt WhileStmt ForStmt
    AssignStmt BreakStmt ContinueStmt DeferStmt UnsafeBlock
    MatchStmt ComptimeBlock GoStmt

    // Expressions
    BinaryExpr UnaryExpr CallExpr MethodCallExpr IndexExpr
    FieldAccess Identifier IntLiteral FloatLiteral StringLiteral
    CharLiteral BoolLiteral NullLiteral ArrayLiteral StructLiteral
    LambdaExpr IfExpr TryExpr RecoverExpr PipeExpr RangeExpr
    BlockExpr

    // Patterns
    PatternWildcard PatternIdentifier PatternLiteral PatternDestructure

    // Types
    PointerType OptionalType SliceType ArrayType FnType
    ResultType TupleType NamedType

    // Misc
    ErrorNode
}

struct ASTNode {
    kind: NodeKind
    line: u32
    col: u32
    data0: u64
    data1: u64
    data2: u64
    data3: u64
    text_start: u32
    text_len: u32
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AST Builder
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct AST {
    nodes: ArrayList(ASTNode)
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> AST {
        AST {
            nodes: ArrayList(ASTNode).init(alloc)
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn addNode(self: *AST, kind: NodeKind, line: u32) -> u32 {
        let idx = self.nodes.len() as u32
        self.nodes.push(ASTNode {
            kind: kind
            line: line
            col: 0
            data0: 0
            data1: 0
            data2: 0
            data3: 0
            text_start: 0
            text_len: 0
        })
        idx
    }

    fn getNode(self: *AST, idx: u32) -> ASTNode {
        self.nodes.items.ptr[idx]
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Parser
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Parser {
    tokens: ArrayList(Token)
    pos: u32
    ast: *AST
    allocator: *Allocator
    errors: ArrayList(ParseError)
    has_error: bool

    struct ParseError {
        msg: string
        line: u32
        col: u32
    }

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator, tokens: ArrayList(Token), ast: *AST) -> Parser {
        Parser {
            tokens: tokens
            pos: 0
            ast: ast
            allocator: alloc
            errors: ArrayList(ParseError).init(alloc)
            has_error: false
        }
    }

    // ── Token Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn peek(self: *Parser) -> Token {
        if self.pos >= self.tokens.len() {
            Token { kind: TokenKind.EOF, start: 0, len: 0, line: 0, col: 0 }
        } else {
            self.tokens.items.ptr[self.pos]
        }
    }

    fn peekAt(self: *Parser, offset: u32) -> Token {
        let idx = self.pos + offset
        if idx >= self.tokens.len() {
            Token { kind: TokenKind.EOF, start: 0, len: 0, line: 0, col: 0 }
        } else {
            self.tokens.items.ptr[idx]
        }
    }

    fn advance(self: *Parser) -> Token {
        let tok = self.peek()
        if tok.kind != TokenKind.EOF {
            self.pos += 1
        }
        tok
    }

    fn expect(self: *Parser, kind: TokenKind) -> Token {
        let tok = self.peek()
        if tok.kind != kind {
            self.reportError("unexpected token", tok.line, tok.col)
        }
        self.advance()
    }

    fn matchToken(self: *Parser, kind: TokenKind) -> bool {
        self.peek().kind == kind
    }

    fn skipNewlines(self: *Parser) {
        while self.matchToken(TokenKind.Newline) {
            self.advance()
        }
    }

    fn reportError(self: *Parser, msg: string, line: u32, col: u32) {
        self.errors.push(ParseError { msg: msg, line: line, col: col })
        self.has_error = true
    }

    // ── Main Parse Entry ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parse(self: *Parser) -> u32 {
        let root = self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.SourceFile, 0)

        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            self.skipNewlines()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) { break }
            self.parseTopLevel()
        }

        root
    }

    // ── Top-Level Declarations ───────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseTopLevel(self: *Parser) {
        self.skipNewlines()
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) { return }

        // Attributes
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Hash) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.At) {
            self.parseAttribute()
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        // Doc comments
        // (handled by lexer as separate tokens)

        // pub modifier
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwPub) {
            self.advance()
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        // Comptime block
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwComptime) {
            self.parseComptimeBlock()
            return
        }

        // const declaration
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwConst) {
            self.parseConstDecl()
            return
        }

        // fn declaration
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwFn) {
            self.parseFnDecl()
            return
        }

        // extern fn
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwExtern) {
            self.parseExternFn()
            return
        }

        // struct
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwStruct) {
            self.parseStructDecl()
            return
        }

        // enum
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwEnum) {
            self.parseEnumDecl()
            return
        }

        // trait
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwTrait) {
            self.parseTraitDecl()
            return
        }

        // impl
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwImpl) {
            self.parseImplBlock()
            return
        }

        // type alias
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwType) {
            self.parseTypeAlias()
            return
        }

        // import
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwImport) {
            self.parseImport()
            return
        }

        // mod
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwMod) {
            self.parseModDecl()
            return
        }

        // Expression statement
        self.parseExprStmt()
    }

    // ── Attribute ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseAttribute(self: *Parser) {
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Hash) {
            self.advance()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) {
                self.advance()
                while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    self.advance()
                }
                if self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) { self.advance() }
            }
        } elif self.matchToken(TokenKind.At) {
            self.advance()
            if self.peek().kind == TokenKind.Ident {
                self.advance()
            }
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) {
                var depth: u32 = 1
                self.advance()
                while depth > 0 and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { depth += 1 }
                    elif self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) { depth -= 1 }
                    self.advance()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ── Function Declaration ─────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseFnDecl(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // fn

        // Name
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)

        // Generic parameters
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) {
            self.advance()
            while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                self.advance()
            }
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) { self.advance() }
        }

        // Parameters
        self.expect(TokenKind.LParen)
        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
<veriayrimi>
if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwSelf) { self.advance() }
            else {
                let pname = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
                self.expect(TokenKind.Colon)
                self.parseType()
            }
        }
        self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)

        // Return type
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Arrow) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseType()
        }

        // Body
        self.skipNewlines()
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Assign) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseExpression()
            self.skipNewlines()
        } else {
            self.parseBlock()
        }

        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.FnDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Struct Declaration ───────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseStructDecl(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // struct
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)

        // Generic parameters
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) {
            self.advance()
            while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                self.advance()
            }
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) { self.advance() }
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.LBrace)
        self.skipNewlines()

        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwPub) { self.advance() }
            let fname = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
            self.expect(TokenKind.Colon)
            self.parseType()
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.RBrace)
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.StructDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Enum Declaration ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseEnumDecl(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // enum
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)

        self.expect(TokenKind.LBrace)
        self.skipNewlines()

        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            let vname = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)

            // Tuple variant
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) {
                self.advance()
                while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
                    self.parseType()
                }
                self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
            }

            // Struct variant
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBrace) {
                self.advance()
                while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    let ffield = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
                    self.expect(TokenKind.Colon)
                    self.parseType()
                    self.skipNewlines()
                }
                self.expect(TokenKind.RBrace)
            }

            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Comma) { self.advance() }
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.RBrace)
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.EnumDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Trait Declaration ────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseTraitDecl(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // trait
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)

        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Colon) {
            self.advance()
            while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                self.advance()
            }
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.LBrace)
        self.skipNewlines()

        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwFn) {
                self.parseFnDecl()
            } else {
                self.advance()
            }
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.RBrace)
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.TraitDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Impl Block ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseImplBlock(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // impl
        self.parseType()

        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwFor) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseType()
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.LBrace)
        self.skipNewlines()

        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwPub) { self.advance() }
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwFn) {
                self.parseFnDecl()
            } else {
                self.advance()
            }
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.RBrace)
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ImplBlock, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Block ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseBlock(self: *Parser) -> u32 {
        let tok = self.expect(TokenKind.LBrace)
        self.skipNewlines()

        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            self.skipNewlines()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) { break }

            // Return
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwReturn) {
                self.advance()
                if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.Newline) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) {
                    self.parseExpression()
                }
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // If
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwIf) {
                self.parseIf()
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // While
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwWhile) {
                self.advance()
                self.parseExpression()
                self.parseBlock()
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // For
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwFor) {
                self.parseFor()
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // Let
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwLet) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwMut) {
                self.parseLet()
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // Match
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwMatch) {
                self.parseMatch()
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // Defer
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwDefer) {
                self.advance()
                self.parseExpression()
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // Break / Continue
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwBreak) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwContinue) {
                self.advance()
                self.skipNewlines()
                continue
            }

            // Expression or assignment
            self.parseExpression()
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.RBrace)
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.Block, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Let Statement ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseLet(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwMut) { self.advance() } else { self.advance() }
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)

        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Colon) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseType()
        }

        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Assign) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseExpression()
        }

        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.LetStmt, tok.line)
    }

    // ── If Statement ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseIf(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // if
        self.parseExpression()
        self.parseBlock()
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwElse) {
            self.advance()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwIf) {
                self.parseIf()
            } else {
                self.parseBlock()
            }
        }
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.IfStmt, tok.line)
    }

    // ── For Statement ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseFor(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // for

        // for i, item in iterable
        if self.peekAt(1).kind == TokenKind.Comma {
            let index = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
            self.expect(TokenKind.Comma)
        }
        let iter = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
        self.expect(TokenKind.KwIn)
        self.parseExpression()
        self.parseBlock()

        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ForStmt, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Match Statement ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseMatch(self: *Parser) {
<veriayrimi>
let tok = self.advance() // match
        self.parseExpression()
        self.expect(TokenKind.LBrace)
        self.skipNewlines()

        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBrace) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            self.parseMatchPattern()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwIf) {
                self.advance()
                self.parseExpression()
            }
            self.expect(TokenKind.FatArrow)
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBrace) {
                self.parseBlock()
            } else {
                self.parseExpression()
            }
            self.skipNewlines()
        }

        self.expect(TokenKind.RBrace)
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.MatchStmt, tok.line)
    }

    fn parseMatchPattern(self: *Parser) {
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Underscore) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.Minus) or
           self.matchToken(TokenKind.TrueLit) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.FalseLit) or
           self.matchToken(TokenKind.IntLit) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.StringLit) {
            self.advance()
        } elif self.matchToken(TokenKind.Ident) {
            self.advance()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) {
                self.advance()
                while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    self.parseMatchPattern()
                    if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Comma) { self.advance() }
                }
                self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
            }
        }
    }

    // ── Comptime Block ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseComptimeBlock(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // comptime
        self.parseBlock()
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ComptimeBlock, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Const Declaration ────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseConstDecl(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // const
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.ColonColon) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.Assign) {
            self.advance()
        }
        self.parseExpression()
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ConstDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Extern Function ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseExternFn(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // extern
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.StringLit) { self.advance() }
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwFn) { self.advance() }
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
        self.expect(TokenKind.LParen)
        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
            let pname = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
            self.expect(TokenKind.Colon)
            self.parseType()
        }
        self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Arrow) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseType()
        }
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ExternDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Type Alias ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseTypeAlias(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // type
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
        self.expect(TokenKind.Assign)
        self.parseType()
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.TypeAlias, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Import ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseImport(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // import
        while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.Newline) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
            self.advance()
        }
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ImportDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Mod Declaration ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseModDecl(self: *Parser) {
        let tok = self.advance() // mod
        let name = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
        self.parseBlock()
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ModDecl, tok.line)
    }

    // ── Expression Statement ─────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseExprStmt(self: *Parser) {
        self.parseExpression()
        self.ast.addNode(NodeKind.ExprStmt, self.peek().line)
    }

    // ── Type Parsing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn parseType(self: *Parser) {
        // Pointer type: *Type or *mut Type
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Star) {
            self.advance()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwMut) { self.advance() }
            self.parseType()
            return
        }

        // Optional type: ?Type
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Question) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseType()
            return
        }

        // Slice type: []Type
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) {
            self.advance()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) {
                self.advance()
                self.parseType()
                return
            }
            // Array type: [Type; N]
            self.parseType()
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Semicolon) {
                self.advance()
                self.parseExpression()
            }
            self.expect(TokenKind.RBracket)
            return
        }

        // Function type: fn(Args) -> Ret
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwFn) {
            self.advance()
            self.expect(TokenKind.LParen)
            while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
                self.parseType()
            }
            self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Arrow) {
                self.advance()
                self.parseType()
            }
            return
        }

        // Named type
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Ident) {
            self.advance()
            // Generic: Type<T>
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) {
                self.advance()
                while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
                    self.parseType()
                }
                self.expect(TokenKind.RBracket)
            }
            return
        }

        // Tuple type: (Type, Type, ...)
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) {
            self.advance()
            while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
                self.parseType()
            }
            self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
            return
        }

        // Result type: !Type
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Bang) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseType()
            return
        }

        // Skip unknown
        self.advance()
    }

    // ── Expression Parsing (Pratt) ───────────────────────────────────

    fn parseExpression(self: *Parser) {
        self.parseBinary(0)
    }

    fn parseBinary(self: *Parser, min_prec: u32) {
        self.parseUnary()

        while true {
            let op_prec = self.binOpPrec(self.peek().kind)
            if op_prec < min_prec { break }

            let op = self.advance() // operator
            self.parseBinary(op_prec + 1)
        }
    }

    fn parseUnary(self: *Parser) {
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Minus) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.Bang) or
           self.matchToken(TokenKind.Star) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.Amp) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseUnary()
            return
        }
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwTry) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseUnary()
            return
        }
        self.parsePostfix()
    }

    fn parsePostfix(self: *Parser) {
        self.parsePrimary()

        while true {
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) {
                // Function call
                self.advance()
                while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
                    self.parseExpression()
                }
                self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
            } elif self.matchToken(TokenKind.Dot) {
                self.advance()
                let field = self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
            } elif self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) {
                self.advance()
                self.parseExpression()
                self.expect(TokenKind.RBracket)
            } elif self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) {
                // Second call (chained)
                self.advance()
                while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RParen) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                    if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) { self.expect(TokenKind.Comma) }
<veriayrimi>
self.parseExpression()
                }
                self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
            } else {
                break
            }
        }
    }

    fn parsePrimary(self: *Parser) {
        // Literals
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.IntLit) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.FloatLit) or
           self.matchToken(TokenKind.StringLit) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.CharLit) or
           self.matchToken(TokenKind.TrueLit) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.FalseLit) or
           self.matchToken(TokenKind.NullLit) {
            self.advance()
            return
        }

        // Identifier
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Ident) or self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwSelf) {
            self.advance()
            return
        }

        // Parenthesized expression
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LParen) {
            self.advance()
            self.parseExpression()
            self.expect(TokenKind.RParen)
            return
        }

        // Array literal
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBracket) {
            self.advance()
            if !self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) {
                self.parseExpression()
                while self.matchToken(TokenKind.Comma) {
                    self.advance()
                    if self.matchToken(TokenKind.RBracket) { break }
                    self.parseExpression()
                }
            }
            self.expect(TokenKind.RBracket)
            return
        }

        // Block expression
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBrace) {
            self.parseBlock()
            return
        }

        // Lambda: |x, y| expr
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Pipe) {
            self.advance()
            while !self.matchToken(TokenKind.Pipe) and !self.matchToken(TokenKind.EOF) {
                self.expect(TokenKind.Ident)
                if self.matchToken(TokenKind.Comma) { self.advance() }
            }
            self.expect(TokenKind.Pipe)
            if self.matchToken(TokenKind.LBrace) {
                self.parseBlock()
            } else {
                self.parseExpression()
            }
            return
        }

        // If expression
        if self.matchToken(TokenKind.KwIf) {
            self.parseIf()
            return
        }

        // Skip unknown token
        self.advance()
    }

    // ── Operator Precedence ──────────────────────────────────────────

    fn binOpPrec(self: *TokenKind) -> u32 {
        match self {
            TokenKind.Pipe => 1       // |>
            TokenKind.FatArrow => 1   // =>
            TokenKind.OrOr => 2       // ||
            TokenKind.AndAnd => 3     // &&
            TokenKind.Eq => 4         // ==
            TokenKind.Ne => 4         // !=
            TokenKind.Lt => 4         // <
            TokenKind.Gt => 4         // >
            TokenKind.Le => 4         // <=
            TokenKind.Ge => 4         // >=
            TokenKind.Plus => 5       // +
            TokenKind.Minus => 5      // -
            TokenKind.Star => 6       // *
            TokenKind.Slash => 6      // /
            TokenKind.Percent => 6    // %
            TokenKind.DotDot => 7     // ..
            TokenKind.DotDotEq => 7   // ..=
            TokenKind.PlusPlus => 8   // ++
            TokenKind.StarStar => 8   // **
            else => 0
        }
    }
}

// LIR Compiler — Type System
// Type representations, type checking, ownership tracking
// Ensures memory safety and data race freedom at compile time

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Type Representation
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum TypeKind {
    // Primitives
    Bool I8 I16 I32 I64 I128
    U8 U16 U32 U64 U128
    F16 F32 F64 F128
    Char String Void Never

    // Compound
    Pointer           // *T
    ConstPointer      // *const T
    MutPointer        // *mut T
    Optional          // ?T
    Slice             // []T
    Array             // [T; N]
    Tuple             // (T1, T2, ...)
    Function          // fn(T1, T2) -> R
    Result            // !T

    // User-defined
    Named             // User, Color, etc.
    Struct            // struct { ... }
    Enum              // enum { ... }
    Trait             // trait { ... }

    // Generic
    Generic           // T (type parameter)
    GenericInst       // Vec(i32) (generic instantiation)

    // Special
    TypeOf            // typeof(expr)
    ComptimeType      // comptime type
    Error
}

struct TypeInfo {
    kind: TypeKind
    size: u32          // size in bytes (0 = opaque)
    align: u32         // alignment
    id: u32            // unique type ID
    name: string       // type name (for debugging)
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Type Table
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct TypeTable {
    types: ArrayList(TypeInfo)
    next_id: u32
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> TypeTable {
        TypeTable {
            types: ArrayList(TypeInfo).init(alloc)
            next_id: 1
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn addType(self: *TypeTable, kind: TypeKind, size: u32, align_val: u32, name: string) -> u32 {
        let id = self.next_id
        self.next_id += 1
        self.types.push(TypeInfo {
            kind: kind
            size: size
            align: align_val
            id: id
            name: name
        })
        id
    }

    fn getType(self: *TypeTable, id: u32) -> ?*TypeInfo {
        if id == 0 or id >= self.types.len() { return null }
        &self.types.items.ptr[id - 1]
    }

    fn findByName(self: *TypeTable, name: string) -> ?u32 {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.types.len() {
            let t = &self.types.items.ptr[i]
            if t.name.len == name.len {
                var match = true
                var j: u64 = 0
                while j < name.len {
                    if t.name.ptr[j] != name.ptr[j] { match = false; break }
                    j += 1
                }
                if match { return t.id }
            }
            i += 1
        }
        null
    }

    fn initPrimitives(self: *TypeTable) {
        // Add primitive types
        self.addType(TypeKind.Void, 0, 1, "void")
        self.addType(TypeKind.Bool, 1, 1, "bool")
        self.addType(TypeKind.I8, 1, 1, "i8")
        self.addType(TypeKind.I16, 2, 2, "i16")
        self.addType(TypeKind.I32, 4, 4, "i32")
        self.addType(TypeKind.I64, 8, 8, "i64")
        self.addType(TypeKind.I128, 16, 16, "i128")
        self.addType(TypeKind.U8, 1, 1, "u8")
        self.addType(TypeKind.U16, 2, 2, "u16")
        self.addType(TypeKind.U32, 4, 4, "u32")
        self.addType(TypeKind.U64, 8, 8, "u64")
        self.addType(TypeKind.U128, 16, 16, "u128")
        self.addType(TypeKind.F16, 2, 2, "f16")
        self.addType(TypeKind.F32, 4, 4, "f32")
        self.addType(TypeKind.F64, 8, 8, "f64")
        self.addType(TypeKind.F128, 16, 16, "f128")
        self.addType(TypeKind.Char, 4, 4, "char")
        self.addType(TypeKind.String, 16, 8, "string")  // ptr + len
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Symbol Table
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum SymbolKind {
    Local
    Parameter
    Function
    Struct
    Enum
    Trait
    Type
    Const
    Module
}

enum Ownership {
    Owned
    Borrowed
    Moved
    Dropped
}

struct Symbol {
    name: string
    kind: SymbolKind
    type_id: u32
    ownership: Ownership
    is_mut: bool
    line: u32
    col: u32
    scope_depth: u32
}

struct SymbolTable {
    symbols: ArrayList(Symbol)
    scope_depth: u32
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> SymbolTable {
        SymbolTable {
            symbols: ArrayList(Symbol).init(alloc)
            scope_depth: 0
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn pushScope(self: *SymbolTable) {
        self.scope_depth += 1
    }

    fn popScope(self: *SymbolTable) {
        // Remove symbols from this scope
        var i = self.symbols.len()
        while i > 0 {
            i -= 1
            if self.symbols.items.ptr[i].scope_depth == self.scope_depth {
                // Mark as dropped
                self.symbols.items.ptr[i].ownership = Ownership.Dropped
            }
        }
        if self.scope_depth > 0 { self.scope_depth -= 1 }
    }

    fn addSymbol(self: *SymbolTable, sym: Symbol) -> bool {
        // Check for redefinition in same scope
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.symbols.len() {
            let existing = &self.symbols.items.ptr[i]
            if existing.scope_depth == self.scope_depth and existing.ownership != Ownership.Dropped {
                if existing.name.len == sym.name.len {
                    var match = true
                    var j: u64 = 0
                    while j < existing.name.len {
                        if existing.name.ptr[j] != sym.name.ptr[j] { match = false; break }
                        j += 1
                    }
                    if match { return false } // redefinition error
                }
            }
            i += 1
        }
        self.symbols.push(sym)
        true
    }

    fn lookup(self: *SymbolTable, name: string) -> ?*Symbol {
        var i = self.symbols.len()
        while i > 0 {
            i -= 1
            let sym = &self.symbols.items.ptr[i]
            if sym.ownership == Ownership.Dropped { continue }
            if sym.name.len == name.len {
                var match = true
                var j: u64 = 0
                while j < name.len {
                    if sym.name.ptr[j] != name.ptr[j] { match = false; break }
                    j += 1
                }
                if match { return sym }
            }
        }
        null
    }

    fn canBorrow(self: *SymbolTable, name: string, mutable: bool) -> bool {
        let sym = self.lookup(name)
        if sym == null { return false }
        if sym.?.ownership == Ownership.Moved { return false }
        if sym.?.ownership == Ownership.Dropped { return false }
        // Check for conflicting borrows
        // TODO: implement borrow checking
        true
    }

    fn moveTo(self: *SymbolTable, name: string) -> bool {
        let sym = self.lookup(name)
        if sym == null { return false }
        if sym.?.ownership == Ownership.Moved { return false }
        sym.?.ownership = Ownership.Moved
        true
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Type Checker
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct TypeChecker {
    types: TypeTable
    symbols: SymbolTable
    errors: ArrayList(TypeError)
    allocator: *Allocator

    struct TypeError {
        msg: string
        line: u32
        col: u32
        expected: string
        found: string
    }

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> TypeChecker {
        var types = TypeTable.init(alloc)
        types.initPrimitives()

        TypeChecker {
            types: types
            symbols: SymbolTable.init(alloc)
            errors: ArrayList(TypeError).init(alloc)
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn addError(self: *TypeChecker, msg: string, line: u32, col: u32, expected: string, found: string) {
        self.errors.push(TypeError {
            msg: msg
            line: line
            col: col
            expected: expected
            found: found
        })
    }

    fn hasErrors(self: *TypeChecker) -> bool {
        self.errors.len() > 0
    }

    fn checkType(self: *TypeChecker, expected: u32, found: u32, line: u32, col: u32) -> bool {
        if expected == found { return true }
        let exp_info = self.types.getType(expected)
        let found_info = self.types.getType(found)
        let exp_name = if exp_info != null { exp_info.?.name } else { string { ptr: "?", len: 1 } }
<veriayrimi>
let found_name = if found_info != null { found_info.?.name } else { string { ptr: "?", len: 1 } }
        self.addError("type mismatch", line, col, exp_name, found_name)
        false
    }
}
