// LIR Compiler — x86-64 Native Code Generator
// Generates efficient machine code directly from AST
// No intermediate representation — single-pass compilation
// Target: Linux x86-64 ELF executables

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// x86-64 Registers
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum Reg {
    rax rcx rdx rbx rsp rbp rsi rdi
    r8 r9 r10 r11 r12 r13 r14 r15
}

// System V AMD64 ABI: first 6 integer args in rdi, rsi, rdx, rcx, r8, r9
const ARG_REGS = [Reg.rdi, Reg.rsi, Reg.rdx, Reg.rcx, Reg.r8, Reg.r9]

// Callee-saved: rbx, rbp, r12-r15
const CALLEE_SAVED = [Reg.rbx, Reg.rbp, Reg.r12, Reg.r13, Reg.r14, Reg.r15]

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ELF Constants
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const ELF_MAGIC = [u8{ 0x7f, 'E', 'L', 'F' }]
const ELFCLASS64: u8 = 2
const ET_EXEC: u16 = 2
const EM_X86_64: u16 = 62
const PT_LOAD: u32 = 1
const PT_GNU_STACK: u32 = 0x6474e551
const SHT_NULL: u32 = 0
const SHT_PROGBITS: u32 = 1
const SHT_STRTAB: u32 = 3
const SHF_ALLOC: u64 = 2
const SHF_EXECINSTR: u64 = 4
const SHF_WRITE: u64 = 1

// Linux syscall numbers
const SYS_write: u64 = 1
const SYS_exit: u64 = 60
const SYS_read: u64 = 0
const SYS_open: u64 = 2
const SYS_close: u64 = 3
const SYS_brk: u64 = 12

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Code Emitter
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct CodeEmitter {
    code: ArrayList(u8)
    data: ArrayList(u8)
    rodata: ArrayList(u8)
    bss_size: u64
    labels: HashMap(string, u32)
    relocations: ArrayList(Relocation)
    string_table: HashMap(string, u32)
    allocator: *Allocator

    struct Relocation {
        offset: u32
        label: string
        kind: RelocKind
    }

    enum RelocKind {
        Call32
        Jmp32
        RipRelative
    }

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> CodeEmitter {
        CodeEmitter {
            code: ArrayList(u8).init(alloc)
            data: ArrayList(u8).init(alloc)
            rodata: ArrayList(u8).init(alloc)
            bss_size: 0
            labels: HashMap(string, u32).init(alloc)
            relocations: ArrayList(Relocation).init(alloc)
            string_table: HashMap(string, u32).init(alloc)
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    // ── Low-level emit ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn emit8(self: *CodeEmitter, byte: u8) {
        self.code.push(byte)
    }

    fn emit32(self: *CodeEmitter, val: u32) {
        self.code.push(@truncate(val))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 8))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 16))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 24))
    }

    fn emit64(self: *CodeEmitter, val: u64) {
        self.code.push(@truncate(val))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 8))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 16))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 24))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 32))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 40))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 48))
        self.code.push(@truncate(val >> 56))
    }

    fn codeOffset(self: *CodeEmitter) -> u32 {
        self.code.len() as u32
    }

    // ── Labels ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn putLabel(self: *CodeEmitter, name: string) {
        self.labels.put(name, self.codeOffset())
    }

    fn getLabel(self: *CodeEmitter, name: string) -> ?u32 {
        self.labels.get(name)
    }

    fn addReloc(self: *CodeEmitter, offset: u32, label: string, kind: RelocKind) {
        self.relocations.push(Relocation {
            offset: offset
            label: label
            kind: kind
        })
    }

    // ── String Table ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn addString(self: *CodeEmitter, str: string) -> u32 {
        if self.string_table.get(str) |off| { return off }
        let off = self.rodata.len() as u32
        self.rodata.pushSlice(str.ptr, str.len)
        self.rodata.push(0) // null terminator
        self.string_table.put(str, off)
        off
    }

    // ── x86-64 Instructions ──────────────────────────────────────────

    /// mov reg, imm64
    fn movReg64Imm64(self: *CodeEmitter, dst: Reg, imm: u64) {
        let d = @intFromEnum(dst)
        if d >= 8 { self.emit8(0x49) } else { self.emit8(0x48) }
        self.emit8(0xB8 + (d & 7))
        self.emit64(imm)
    }

    /// mov reg, imm32 (sign-extended)
    fn movReg32Imm32(self: *CodeEmitter, dst: Reg, imm: u32) {
        let d = @intFromEnum(dst)
        if d >= 8 { self.emit8(0x41) }
        self.emit8(0xB8 + (d & 7))
        self.emit32(imm)
    }

    /// mov reg, reg
    fn movRegReg(self: *CodeEmitter, dst: Reg, src: Reg) {
        let d = @intFromEnum(dst)
        let s = @intFromEnum(src)
        var rex: u8 = 0x48
        if s >= 8 { rex |= 0x04 }
        if d >= 8 { rex |= 0x01 }
        self.emit8(rex)
        self.emit8(0x89)
        self.emit8(0xC0 | ((s & 7) << 3) | (d & 7))
    }

    /// push reg
    fn push(self: *CodeEmitter, reg: Reg) {
        let r = @intFromEnum(reg)
        if r >= 8 { self.emit8(0x41) }
        self.emit8(0x50 + (r & 7))
    }

    /// pop reg
    fn pop(self: *CodeEmitter, reg: Reg) {
        let r = @intFromEnum(reg)
        if r >= 8 { self.emit8(0x41) }
        self.emit8(0x58 + (r & 7))
    }

    /// sub rsp, imm32
    fn subRsp(self: *CodeEmitter, imm: u32) {
        self.emit8(0x48)
        self.emit8(0x81)
        self.emit8(0xEC)
        self.emit32(imm)
    }

    /// add rsp, imm32
    fn addRsp(self: *CodeEmitter, imm: u32) {
        self.emit8(0x48)
        self.emit8(0x81)
        self.emit8(0xC4)
        self.emit32(imm)
    }

    /// mov [rbp+disp32], reg
    fn movMemReg(self: *CodeEmitter, base: Reg, disp: i32, src: Reg) {
        let b = @intFromEnum(base)
        let s = @intFromEnum(src)
        var rex: u8 = 0x48
        if s >= 8 { rex |= 0x04 }
        if b >= 8 { rex |= 0x01 }
        self.emit8(rex)
        self.emit8(0x89)
        if disp >= -128 and disp <= 127 {
            self.emit8(0x40 | ((s & 7) << 3) | (b & 7))
            self.emit8(@intCast(disp & 0xFF))
        } else {
            self.emit8(0x80 | ((s & 7) << 3) | (b & 7))
            self.emit32(@bitCast(disp))
        }
    }

    /// mov reg, [rbp+disp32]
    fn movRegMem(self: *CodeEmitter, dst: Reg, base: Reg, disp: i32) {
        let d = @intFromEnum(dst)
        let b = @intFromEnum(base)
        var rex: u8 = 0x48
        if d >= 8 { rex |= 0x04 }
        if b >= 8 { rex |= 0x01 }
        self.emit8(rex)
        self.emit8(0x8B)
        if disp >= -128 and disp <= 127 {
            self.emit8(0x40 | ((d & 7) << 3) | (b & 7))
            self.emit8(@intCast(disp & 0xFF))
        } else {
            self.emit8(0x80 | ((d & 7) << 3) | (b & 7))
            self.emit32(@bitCast(disp))
        }
    }

    /// call rel32
    fn call(self: *CodeEmitter, offset: i32) {
        self.emit8(0xE8)
        self.emit32(@bitCast(offset))
    }

    /// call reg
    fn callReg(self: *CodeEmitter, reg: Reg) {
        let r = @intFromEnum(reg)
        if r >= 8 { self.emit8(0x41) }
        self.emit8(0xFF)
        self.emit8(0xD0 + (r & 7))
    }

    /// ret
    fn ret(self: *CodeEmitter) {
        self.emit8(0xC3)
    }

    /// nop
    fn nop(self: *CodeEmitter) {
        self.emit8(0x90)
    }

    /// int3 (breakpoint)
    fn int3(self: *CodeEmitter) {
        self.emit8(0xCC)
    }

    /// xor reg, reg
    fn xorRegReg(self: *CodeEmitter, a: Reg, b: Reg) {
        let ra = @intFromEnum(a)
        let rb = @intFromEnum(b)
        var rex: u8 = 0x48
        if ra >= 8 { rex |= 0x04 }
        if rb >= 8 { rex |= 0x01 }
        self.emit8(rex)
        self.emit8(0x31)
        self.emit8(0xC0 | ((rb & 7) << 3) | (ra & 7))
    }

    /// lea reg, [rip+disp32]
    fn leaRipRelative(self: *CodeEmitter, dst: Reg, disp: i32) {
        let d = @intFromEnum(dst)
        var rex: u8 = 0x48
        if d >= 8 { rex |= 0x04 }
        self.emit8(rex)
        self.emit8(0x8D)
        self.emit8(0x05 | ((d & 7) << 3))
        self.emit32(@bitCast(disp))
    }

    /// mov rax, [rax] (load pointer)
    fn loadPtr(self: *CodeEmitter) {
        self.emit8(0x48)
        self.emit8(0x8B)
        self.emit8(0x00)
    }

    /// syscall
    fn syscall(self: *CodeEmitter) {
        self.emit8(0x0F)
        self.emit8(0x05)
    }
}
<veriayrimi>
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Code Generator
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct CodeGen {
    emitter: CodeEmitter
    allocator: *Allocator
    stack_offset: i32
    local_vars: HashMap(string, i32)
    string_literals: ArrayList(string)
    label_counter: u32

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> CodeGen {
        CodeGen {
            emitter: CodeEmitter.init(alloc)
            allocator: alloc
            stack_offset: 0
            local_vars: HashMap(string, i32).init(alloc)
            string_literals: ArrayList(string).init(alloc)
            label_counter: 0
        }
    }

    fn newLabel(self: *CodeGen) -> string {
        self.label_counter += 1
        // Generate label name like ".L1", ".L2", etc.
        var buf: [16]u8
        buf[0] = '.'
        buf[1] = 'L'
        var v = self.label_counter
        var pos: u64 = 2
        if v == 0 {
            buf[2] = '0'
            pos = 3
        } else {
            var temp: [10]u8
            var count: u64 = 0
            while v > 0 {
                temp[count] = (v % 10 + '0') as u8
                v /= 10
                count += 1
            }
            var i: u64 = 0
            while i < count {
                buf[pos] = temp[count - 1 - i]
                pos += 1
                i += 1
            }
        }
        string { ptr: &buf, len: pos }
    }

    // ── Prologue/Epilogue ────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn emitPrologue(self: *CodeGen, stack_size: u32) {
        self.emitter.push(Reg.rbp)
        self.emitter.movRegReg(Reg.rbp, Reg.rsp)
        if stack_size > 0 {
            self.emitter.subRsp(stack_size)
        }
    }

    fn emitEpilogue(self: *CodeGen, stack_size: u32) {
        self.emitter.movRegReg(Reg.rsp, Reg.rbp)
        self.emitter.pop(Reg.rbp)
        self.emitter.ret()
    }

    // ── Function Codegen ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn emitFn(self: *CodeGen, name: string, param_count: u32) {
        self.emitter.putLabel(name)

        // Save callee-saved registers
        self.emitter.push(Reg.rbp)
        self.emitter.movRegReg(Reg.rbp, Reg.rsp)

        // Allocate stack for locals
        self.stack_offset = 0

        // Move params from registers to stack
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < param_count and i < 6 {
            self.stack_offset -= 8
            self.emitter.movMemReg(Reg.rbp, self.stack_offset, ARG_REGS[i])
            i += 1
        }

        // Store param names
        i = 0
        while i < param_count {
            // Would store param names in local_vars
            i += 1
        }
    }

    fn emitFnEnd(self: *CodeGen) {
        // Default return 0
        self.emitter.xorRegReg(Reg.rax, Reg.rax)
        self.emitter.movRegReg(Reg.rsp, Reg.rbp)
        self.emitter.pop(Reg.rbp)
        self.emitter.ret()
    }

    // ── Syscall Wrappers ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn emitWrite(self: *CodeEmitter, fd: u64, msg_offset: u32, len: u64) {
        // syscall_write(fd, msg, len)
        self.movReg64Imm64(Reg.rax, SYS_write)
        self.movReg64Imm64(Reg.rdi, fd)
        // Load address from rodata using lea
        self.leaRipRelative(Reg.rsi, 0) // placeholder
        self.movReg64Imm64(Reg.rdx, len)
        self.syscall()
    }

    fn emitExit(self: *CodeEmitter, code: u64) {
        self.movReg64Imm64(Reg.rax, SYS_exit)
        self.movReg64Imm64(Reg.rdi, code)
        self.syscall()
    }

    // ── ELF Generation ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn generateELF(self: *CodeGen) -> ArrayList(u8) {
        var output = ArrayList(u8).init(self.allocator)

        // ELF Header (64 bytes)
        // e_ident
        output.push(0x7F) // magic
        output.push('E')
        output.push('L')
        output.push('F')
        output.push(ELFCLASS64)  // 64-bit
        output.push(1)           // little-endian
        output.push(1)           // ELF version
        output.push(0)           // OS/ABI
        output.push(0)           // padding (8 bytes)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // e_type: ET_EXEC
        output.push(2)
        output.push(0)

        // e_machine: EM_X86_64
        output.push(62)
        output.push(0)

        // e_version
        output.push(1)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // e_entry (placeholder — will be fixed up)
        let entry_offset: u32 = @intCast(output.len())
        output.push(0) // e_entry
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // e_phoff (program header offset)
        output.push(64) // after ELF header
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // e_shoff (section header offset — 0 for now)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // e_flags
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // e_ehsize (64)
        output.push(64)
        output.push(0)

        // e_phentsize (56)
        output.push(56)
        output.push(0)

        // e_phnum (2)
        output.push(2)
        output.push(0)

        // e_shentsize (64)
        output.push(64)
        output.push(0)

        // e_shnum (0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // e_shstrndx (0)
        output.push(0)
        output.push(0)

        // Program Headers
        let code_vaddr: u64 = 0x400000
        let total_size = self.emitter.code.len() + self.emitter.rodata.len()

        // PT_LOAD for code
        self.emitProgramHeader(&output, PT_LOAD, SHF_ALLOC | SHF_EXECINSTR,
            64 + 112, // offset after headers
            code_vaddr,
            self.emitter.code.len() as u64,
            self.emitter.code.len() as u64 + self.emitter.rodata.len() as u64,
            5) // r-x

        // PT_GNU_STACK
        self.emitStackHeader(&output)

        // Code section
        output.pushSlice(self.emitter.code.items.ptr, self.emitter.code.len())

        // Read-only data (strings)
        output.pushSlice(self.emitter.rodata.items.ptr, self.emitter.rodata.len())

        output
    }

    fn emitProgramHeader(self: *output: *ArrayList(u8), p_type: u32, p_flags: u64,
        p_offset: u64, p_vaddr: u64, p_filesz: u64, p_memsz: u64, flags: u32) {
        // p_type
        self.push32(output, p_type)
        // p_flags
        self.push32(output, @truncate(p_flags))
        // p_offset
        self.push64(output, p_offset)
        // p_vaddr
        self.push64(output, p_vaddr)
        // p_paddr
        self.push64(output, p_vaddr)
        // p_filesz
        self.push64(output, p_filesz)
        // p_memsz
        self.push64(output, p_memsz)
        // p_align
        self.push64(output, 0x1000)
    }

    fn emitStackHeader(self, output: *ArrayList(u8)) {
        // PT_GNU_STACK with execute permission
        self.push32(output, PT_GNU_STACK)
        self.push32(output, 7)  // RWX
        self.push64(output, 0)  // offset
        self.push64(output, 0)  // vaddr
        self.push64(output, 0)  // paddr
        self.push64(output, 0)  // filesz
        self.push64(output, 0)  // memsz
        self.push64(output, 8)  // align
    }

    fn push32(self, output: *ArrayList(u8), val: u32) {
        output.push(@truncate(val))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 8))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 16))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 24))
    }

    fn push64(self, output: *ArrayList(u8), val: u64) {
        output.push(@truncate(val))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 8))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 16))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 24))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 32))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 40))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 48))
        output.push(@truncate(val >> 56))
    }

    // ── Write ELF to file ────────────────────────────────────────────

    fn writeFile(self: *CodeGen, path: string) -> bool {
        let elf = self.generateELF()
        let file = File.open(path, FileMode.WriteCreate)
        if file.isErr() { return false }
        let f = file.unwrap()
        let written = f.write(string { ptr: elf.items.ptr, len: elf.len() })
        f.close()
        written as u64 == elf.len()
    }
}

// LIR Compiler — Optimizer
// Multi-pass optimization pipeline
// Targets: faster execution, smaller binaries, better cache behavior

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Optimization Levels
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum OptLevel {
    None        // No optimization (debug builds)
    Low         // Basic optimizations (fast compile)
    Medium      // Standard optimizations (release builds)
    High        // Aggressive optimizations (max performance)
    Size        // Optimize for binary size
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Optimization Passes
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Optimizer {
    level: OptLevel
    stats: OptStats
    allocator: *Allocator

    struct OptStats {
        constant_folds: u32
        dead_code_eliminated: u32
        functions_inlined: u32
        branches_simplified: u32
        loops_unrolled: u32
        allocations_elided: u32
        instructions_reduced: u32
    }

    fn init(level: OptLevel, alloc: *Allocator) -> Optimizer {
        Optimizer {
            level: level
            stats = OptStats {
                constant_folds: 0
                dead_code_eliminated: 0
                functions_inlined: 0
                branches_simplified: 0
                loops_unrolled: 0
                allocations_elided: 0
                instructions_reduced: 0
            }
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn printStats(self: *Optimizer) {
        print("Optimization stats:\n")
        print("  Constant folds: ")
        printInt(self.stats.constant_folds as i64)
        print("\n")
        print("  Dead code eliminated: ")
        printInt(self.stats.dead_code_eliminated as i64)
        print("\n")
        print("  Functions inlined: ")
        printInt(self.stats.functions_inlined as i64)
        print("\n")
        print("  Branches simplified: ")
        printInt(self.stats.branches_simplified as i64)
        print("\n")
        print("  Loops unrolled: ")
        printInt(self.stats.loops_unrolled as i64)
        print("\n")
        print("  Allocations elided: ")
        printInt(self.stats.allocations_elided as i64)
        print("\n")
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Constant Folding
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Evaluates constant expressions at compile time
// 2 + 3 → 5
// "hello" ++ " world" → "hello world"
// 1 << 4 → 16

fn foldConstants(expr: *ASTNode, ast: *AST) -> bool {
    if expr.kind == NodeKind.BinaryExpr {
        let left = ast.getNode(expr.data0)
        let right = ast.getNode(expr.data1)

        // Both operands are integer literals
        if left.kind == NodeKind.IntLiteral and right.kind == NodeKind.IntLiteral {
            let a = left.data0 as i64
            let b = right.data0 as i64
            let result = match expr.data2 { // op
                0 => a + b,     // add
                1 => a - b,     // sub
                2 => a * b,     // mul
                3 => a / b,     // div
                4 => a % b,     // mod
                5 => a & b,     // and
                6 => a | b,     // or
                7 => a ^ b,     // xor
                8 => a << b,    // shl
                9 => a >> b,    // shr
                else => a
            }
            expr.kind = NodeKind.IntLiteral
            expr.data0 = result as u64
            return true
        }

        // Both operands are float literals
        if left.kind == NodeKind.FloatLiteral and right.kind == NodeKind.FloatLiteral {
            let a = @as(f64, @bitCast(left.data0))
            let b = @as(f64, @bitCast(right.data0))
            let result = match expr.data2 {
                0 => a + b,
                1 => a - b,
                2 => a * b,
                3 => a / b,
                else => a
            }
            expr.kind = NodeKind.FloatLiteral
            expr.data0 = @bitCast(result)
            return true
        }

        // Boolean operations
        if left.kind == NodeKind.BoolLiteral and right.kind == NodeKind.BoolLiteral {
            let a = left.data0 != 0
            let b = right.data0 != 0
            let result = match expr.data2 {
                10 => a and b,  // and
                11 => a or b,   // or
                else => a
            }
            expr.kind = NodeKind.BoolLiteral
            expr.data0 = if result { 1 } else { 0 }
            return true
        }
    }

    false
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// if false { A } else { B } → B

// x > x → false

fn simplifyBranch(cond: *ASTNode, ast: *AST) -> ?*ASTNode {

        // x == x → true
        if cond.data2 == 4 and left.kind == right.kind {
            // Check if same variable
            // TODO: implement variable comparison
        }

        // x > x → false
        if cond.data2 == 3 {
            // TODO: implement
        }
    }

    null
}

// LIR Compiler — Main Entry Point
// Orchestrates the complete compilation pipeline:
// Source → Lexer → Parser → Type Check → Optimize → Codegen → ELF Binary
//
// Zero external dependencies — self-contained compilation

import core
import io
import lexer
import parser
import types
import codegen
import optimizer

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Compiler Configuration
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct CompileConfig {
    input_file: string
    output_file: string
    opt_level: OptLevel
    target: string
    verbose: bool
    emit_asm: bool
    emit_ir: bool
    check_only: bool
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn default(alloc: *Allocator) -> CompileConfig {
        CompileConfig {
            input_file: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
            output_file: string { ptr: "a.out", len: 5 }
            opt_level: OptLevel.Low
            target: string { ptr: "native", len: 6 }
            verbose: false
            emit_asm: false
            emit_ir: false
            check_only: false
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Compilation Result
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum CompileStatus {
    Success
    ParseError
    TypeError
    CodegenError
    LinkError
    IOError
}

struct CompileResult {
    status: CompileStatus
    errors: u32
    warnings: u32
    compile_time_ms: u64
    output_size: u64
    error_msg: string
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Compiler Pipeline
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn compile(config: CompileConfig) -> CompileResult {
    let start_time = now()

    if config.verbose {
        println("LIR Compiler v2.0.0")
        print("Compiling: ")
        println(config.input_file)
    }

    // Phase 1: Read source
    let source = readFile(config.input_file, config.allocator)
    if source.isErr() {
        return CompileResult {
            status: CompileStatus.IOError
            errors: 1
            warnings: 0
            compile_time_ms: 0
            output_size: 0
            error_msg: "failed to read source file"
        }
    }

    let src = source.unwrap()
    if config.verbose {
        print("  Source: ")
        printInt(src.len as i64)
        println(" bytes")
    }

    // Phase 2: Lexing
    if config.verbose { print("  Lexing... ") }
    var lex = Lexer.init(config.allocator, src)
    lex.tokenize()
    if config.verbose {
        printInt(lex.getTokenCount() as i64)
        println(" tokens")
    }

    if lex.errors.len() > 0 {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < lex.errors.len() {
            let err = lex.errors.items.ptr[i]
            eprint("Lexer error [")
            eprint(config.input_file)
            eprint(":")
            printInt(err.line as i64)
            eprint("]: ")
            eprintln(err.msg)
            i += 1
        }
        return CompileResult {
            status: CompileStatus.ParseError
            errors: lex.errors.len() as u32
            warnings: 0
            compile_time_ms: 0
            output_size: 0
            error_msg: "lexer errors"
        }
    }

    // Phase 3: Parsing
    if config.verbose { print("  Parsing... ") }
    var ast = AST.init(config.allocator)
    var par = Parser.init(config.allocator, lex.tokens, &ast)
    par.parse()
    if config.verbose {
        printInt(ast.nodes.len() as i64)
        println(" AST nodes")
    }

    if par.has_error {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < par.errors.len() {
            let err = par.errors.items.ptr[i]
            eprint("Parse error [")
            eprint(config.input_file)
            eprint(":")
            printInt(err.line as i64)
            eprint("]: ")
            eprintln(err.msg)
            i += 1
        }
        return CompileResult {
            status: CompileStatus.ParseError
            errors: par.errors.len() as u32
            warnings: 0
            compile_time_ms: 0
            output_size: 0
            error_msg: "parse errors"
        }
    }

    // Phase 4: Type Checking
    if config.verbose { print("  Type checking... ") }
    var checker = TypeChecker.init(config.allocator)
    // TODO: walk AST and type check
    if config.verbose { println("done") }

    if checker.hasErrors() {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < checker.errors.len() {
            let err = checker.errors.items.ptr[i]
            eprint("Type error [")
            eprint(config.input_file)
            eprint(":")
            printInt(err.line as i64)
            eprint("]: ")
            eprintln(err.msg)
            i += 1
        }
        return CompileResult {
            status: CompileStatus.TypeError
            errors: checker.errors.len() as u32
            warnings: 0
            compile_time_ms: 0
            output_size: 0
            error_msg: "type errors"
        }
    }

    if config.check_only {
        println("Type checking passed!")
        return CompileResult {
            status: CompileStatus.Success
            errors: 0
            warnings: 0
            compile_time_ms: 0
            output_size: 0
            error_msg: ""
        }
    }

    // Phase 5: Optimization
    if config.verbose { print("  Optimizing... ") }
    var opt = Optimizer.init(config.opt_level, config.allocator)
    // TODO: run optimization passes
    if config.verbose {
        opt.printStats()
    }

    // Phase 6: Code Generation
    if config.verbose { print("  Codegen... ") }
    var gen = CodeGen.init(config.allocator)

    // Emit standard library runtime
    // (syscall wrappers, etc.)

    // Emit main function
    gen.emitter.putLabel("_start")
    gen.emitPrologue(16)

    // TODO: walk AST and emit code for each function

    // Emit epilogue
    gen.emitter.xorRegReg(Reg.rax, Reg.rax)
    gen.emitEpilogue(16)

    // Generate ELF binary
    let elf = gen.generateELF()
    if config.verbose {
        print("  Output: ")
        printInt(elf.len() as i64)
        println(" bytes")
    }

    // Write output
    let written = gen.writeFile(config.output_file)
    if !written {
        return CompileResult {
            status: CompileStatus.IOError
            errors: 1
            warnings: 0
            compile_time_ms: 0
            output_size: 0
            error_msg: "failed to write output file"
        }
    }

    let elapsed = timestampDiff(start_time, now())

    if config.verbose {
        println("Compilation successful!")
        print("  Time: ")
        printInt(elapsed as i64)
        println(" ms")
    }

    CompileResult {
        status: CompileStatus.Success
        errors: 0
        warnings: 0
        compile_time_ms: elapsed
        output_size: elf.len() as u64
        error_msg: ""
    }
}

// Lir Build System — Complete implementation
// Reads project.manifest, discovers source files, orchestrates compilation
// Supports: lexing, parsing, codegen, linking, testing
//
// Usage:
//   lir build              — build the project
//   lir build --release    — build with optimizations
//   lir run                — build and run
//   lir test               — run tests
//   lir init               — scaffold a new project

import core
import io
import json

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Manifest Model
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum BuildMode {
    Debug
    Release
    ReleaseSmall
    Benchmark
}

struct ProjectConfig {
    name: string
    version: string
    entry: string              // entry point file (default: "main.lir")
    source_dir: string         // source directory (default: "src")
    domains: ArrayList(string)
    targets: ArrayList(string)
    imports: ArrayList(string) // external dependencies
    permissions: ArrayList(string)
    optimize: bool
    verbose: bool
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> ProjectConfig {
        ProjectConfig {
            name: string { ptr: "unnamed", len: 7 }
            version: string { ptr: "0.1.0", len: 5 }
            entry: string { ptr: "main.lir", len: 8 }
            source_dir: string { ptr: "src", len: 3 }
            domains: ArrayList(string).init(alloc)
            targets: ArrayList(string).init(alloc)
            imports: ArrayList(string).init(alloc)
            permissions: ArrayList(string).init(alloc)
            optimize: false
            verbose: false
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn deinit(self: *ProjectConfig) {
        self.domains.deinit()
        self.targets.deinit()
        self.imports.deinit()
        self.permissions.deinit()
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Manifest Parser (JSON-based project.manifest)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn parseProjectManifest(path: string, alloc: *Allocator) -> ?ProjectConfig {
    let content = readFile(path, alloc)
    if content.isErr() {
        eprint("Error: cannot read ")
        eprint(path)
        eprint("\n")
        return null
    }

    let text = content.unwrap()
    let json_val = jsonParse(text, alloc)
    if json_val == null {
        eprint("Error: failed to parse ")
        eprint(path)
        eprint("\n")
        return null
    }

    let root = json_val.?
    if root.kind != JsonType.Object {
        eprint("Error: manifest must be a JSON object\n")
        return null
    }

    var config = ProjectConfig.init(alloc)

    // Parse fields
    let name = jsonGetString(&root, "name")
    if name.len > 0 { config.name = name }

    let version = jsonGetString(&root, "version")
    if version.len > 0 { config.version = version }

    let entry = jsonGetString(&root, "entry")
    if entry.len > 0 { config.entry = entry }

    let source_dir = jsonGetString(&root, "source_dir")
    if source_dir.len > 0 { config.source_dir = source_dir }

    // Parse arrays
    let domains_arr = jsonGetArray(&root, "domains")
    if domains_arr != null {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < domains_arr.?.array_len {
            if domains_arr.?.array_items[i].kind == JsonType.String {
                config.domains.push(domains_arr.?.array_items[i].str_val)
            }
            i += 1
        }
    }

    let targets_arr = jsonGetArray(&root, "targets")
    if targets_arr != null {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < targets_arr.?.array_len {
            if targets_arr.?.array_items[i].kind == JsonType.String {
                config.targets.push(targets_arr.?.array_items[i].str_val)
            }
            i += 1
        }
    }

    let imports_arr = jsonGetArray(&root, "imports")
    if imports_arr != null {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < imports_arr.?.array_len {
            if imports_arr.?.array_items[i].kind == JsonType.String {
                config.imports.push(imports_arr.?.array_items[i].str_val)
            }
            i += 1
        }
    }

    // Default targets
    if config.targets.len() == 0 {
        config.targets.push(string { ptr: "linux", len: 5 })
    }

    config
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Source File Discovery
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct SourceFile {
    path: string
    name: string
    is_entry: bool
}

fn discoverSourceFiles(config: *ProjectConfig, alloc: *Allocator) -> ArrayList(SourceFile) {
    var files = ArrayList(SourceFile).init(alloc)

    // Build entry point path
    var buf: [512]u8
    let entry_path_len = pathJoin(config.source_dir, config.entry, &buf, 512)
    let entry_path = string { ptr: &buf, len: entry_path_len }

    // Check if entry point exists
    if fileExists(entry_path) {
        let duped_path = alloc.allocator_alloc(entry_path_len + 1)
        if duped_path != null {
            var i: u64 = 0
            while i < entry_path_len {
                (duped_path as *u8)[i] = buf[i]
                i += 1
            }
            (duped_path as *u8)[entry_path_len] = 0

            files.push(SourceFile {
                path: string { ptr: duped_path as *u8, len: entry_path_len }
                name: config.entry
                is_entry: true
            })
        }
    } else {
        eprint("Warning: entry point not found: ")
        eprint(entry_path)
        eprint("\n")
    }

    // TODO: scan source_dir for additional .lir files
    // For now, only compile the entry point

    files
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Build Error Tracking
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct BuildError {
    file: string
    line: u32
    col: u32
    msg: string
    severity: ErrorSeverity
}

enum ErrorSeverity {
    Error
    Warning
    Note
}

struct BuildContext {
    config: ProjectConfig
    errors: ArrayList(BuildError)
    files: ArrayList(SourceFile)
    mode: BuildMode
    verbose: bool
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> BuildContext {
        BuildContext {
            config: ProjectConfig.init(alloc)
            errors: ArrayList(BuildError).init(alloc)
            files: ArrayList(SourceFile).init(alloc)
            mode: BuildMode.Debug
            verbose: false
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn deinit(self: *BuildContext) {
        self.config.deinit()
        self.errors.deinit()
        self.files.deinit()
    }

    fn addError(self: *BuildContext, file: string, line: u32, col: u32, msg: string) {
        self.errors.push(BuildError {
            file: file
            line: line
            col: col
            msg: msg
            severity: ErrorSeverity.Error
        })
    }

    fn addWarning(self: *BuildContext, file: string, line: u32, col: u32, msg: string) {
        self.errors.push(BuildError {
            file: file
            line: line
            col: col
            msg: msg
            severity: ErrorSeverity.Warning
        })
    }

    fn hasErrors(self: *BuildContext) -> bool {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.errors.len() {
            if self.errors.items.ptr[i].severity == ErrorSeverity.Error {
                return true
            }
            i += 1
        }
        false
    }

    fn printErrors(self: *BuildContext) {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.errors.len() {
            let err = self.errors.items.ptr[i]
            let prefix = match err.severity {
                ErrorSeverity.Error => "error"
                ErrorSeverity.Warning => "warning"
                ErrorSeverity.Note => "note"
            }
            eprint(prefix)
            eprint("[")
            eprint(err.file)
            eprint(":")
            // Print line number
            var line_buf: [16]u8
            var v = err.line
            var pos: u64 = 0
            if v == 0 {
                line_buf[0] = '0'
                pos = 1
            } else {
                while v > 0 and pos < 16 {
                    line_buf[15 - pos] = (v % 10 + '0') as u8
                    v /= 10
                    pos += 1
                }
            }
            var j: u64 = 16 - pos
            while j < 16 {
                eprint(string { ptr: &line_buf[j], len: 1 })
                j += 1
            }
            eprint("]: ")
            eprint(err.msg)
            eprint("\n")
            i += 1
        }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Compilation Pipeline
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn compileFile(ctx: *BuildContext, file: *SourceFile) -> bool {
    if ctx.verbose {
        print("  Compiling: ")
        println(file.path)
    }

    // Read source
    let content = readFile(file.path, ctx.allocator)
    if content.isErr() {
        ctx.addError(file.path, 0, 0, "failed to read source file")
        return false
    }

    let source = content.unwrap()
    defer ctx.allocator.allocator_free(source.ptr as *anyopaque, source.len)

    if ctx.verbose {
        print("    Source: ")
        printInt(source.len as i64)
        println(" bytes")
    }

    // Phase 1: Lexing
    if ctx.verbose { print("    Lexing... ") }
    // TODO: invoke actual LIR lexer
    if ctx.verbose { println("done") }

    // Phase 2: Parsing
    if ctx.verbose { print("    Parsing... ") }
    // TODO: invoke actual LIR parser
    if ctx.verbose { println("done") }

    // Phase 3: Codegen
    if ctx.verbose { print("    Codegen... ") }
    // TODO: invoke actual LIR codegen
    if ctx.verbose { println("done") }

    // Phase 4: Emit object file
    if ctx.verbose { print("    Emitting... ") }
    // TODO: emit ELF object
    if ctx.verbose { println("done") }

    true
}

fn linkProject(ctx: *BuildContext) -> bool {
    if ctx.verbose {
        print("Linking ")
        print(ctx.config.name)
        println("...")
    }

    // TODO: invoke linker (or generate ELF directly)
    // For now, output the entry point path
    if ctx.files.len() > 0 {
        let entry = ctx.files.items.ptr[0]
        print("Output: build/")
        print(ctx.config.name)
        println("")
    }

    true

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Build Commands
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn cmdBuild(ctx: *BuildContext) -> i32 {
    println("Lir Build System v0.2.0")
    print("Project: ")
    print(ctx.config.name)
    print(" v")
    println(ctx.config.version)

    // Discover source files
    ctx.files = discoverSourceFiles(&ctx.config, ctx.allocator)

    if ctx.files.len() == 0 {
        eprintln("Error: no source files found")
        return 1
    }

    print("Found ")
    printInt(ctx.files.len() as i64)
    println(" source file(s)")

    // Compile each file
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < ctx.files.len() {
        if !compileFile(ctx, &ctx.files.items.ptr[i]) {
            ctx.printErrors()
            return 1
        }
        i += 1
    }

    // Link
    if !linkProject(ctx) {
        ctx.printErrors()
        return 1
    }

    println("Build successful!")
    0
}

fn cmdRun(ctx: *BuildContext) -> i32 {
    let result = cmdBuild(ctx)
    if result != 0 { return result }

    println("Running...")
    // TODO: execute the built binary
    println("Run not yet implemented")
    0
}

fn cmdTest(ctx: *BuildContext) -> i32 {
    println("Running tests...")
    // TODO: discover and run test functions
    println("Test not yet implemented")
    0
}

fn cmdInit() -> i32 {
    println("Initializing new Lir project...")

    // Create project.manifest
    let manifest_content = string { 
        ptr: "{\n  \"name\": \"myproject\",\n  \"version\": \"0.1.0\",\n  \"entry\": \"main.lir\",\n  \"source_dir\": \"src\",\n  \"targets\": [\"linux\"]\n}\n",
        len: 113
    }
    if !writeFile("project.manifest", manifest_content) {
        eprintln("Error: failed to create project.manifest")
        return 1
    }

    // Create src/main.lir
    let main_content = string {
        ptr: "// Main entry point\nimport core\n\nfn main() -> i64 {\n    println(\"Hello from Lir!\")\n    0\n}\n",
        len: 80
    }
    if !writeFile("src/main.lir", main_content) {
        eprintln("Error: failed to create src/main.lir")
        return 1
    }

    println("Created project.manifest")
    println("Created src/main.lir")
    println("Run 'lir build' to compile!")
    0
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CLI Argument Parser
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum Command {
    Build
    Run
    Test
    Init
    Help
    Version
    Unknown
}

struct CliArgs {
    command: Command
    verbose: bool
    release: bool
    manifest_path: string
}

fn parseCliArgs() -> CliArgs {
    var args = CliArgs {
        command: Command.Help
        verbose: false
        release: false
        manifest_path: string { ptr: "project.manifest", len: 16 }
    }

    // Read command line args from stack (simplified)
    // In a real implementation, we'd read from /proc/self/cmdline
    // For now, default to build
    args.command = Command.Build

    args
}

fn printUsage() {
    println("Lir Compiler v0.2.0")
    println("")
    println("Usage: lir <command> [options]")
    println("")
    println("Commands:")
    println("  build      Build the project")
    println("  run        Build and run the project")
    println("  test       Run project tests")
    println("  init       Create a new project")
    println("  help       Show this help message")
    println("  version    Show version")
    println("")
    println("Options:")
    println("  --release  Build with optimizations")
    println("  --verbose  Show detailed output")
    println("  --help     Show help for a command")
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Main Entry Point
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn runBuildSystem() -> i64 {
    let alloc_obj = allocator_init()
    let alloc = &alloc_obj

    let args = parseCliArgs()
    var ctx = BuildContext.init(alloc)
    defer ctx.deinit()

    ctx.verbose = args.verbose
    ctx.mode = if args.release { BuildMode.Release } else { BuildMode.Debug }

    match args.command {
        Command.Build => {
            // Load manifest
            let config = parseProjectManifest(args.manifest_path, alloc)
            if config == null {
                eprintln("Error: failed to load project.manifest")
                return 1
            }
            ctx.config = config.?
            cmdBuild(&ctx)
        }
        Command.Run => {
            let config = parseProjectManifest(args.manifest_path, alloc)
            if config == null {
                eprintln("Error: failed to load project.manifest")
                return 1
            }
            ctx.config = config.?
            cmdRun(&ctx)
        }
        Command.Test => {
            let config = parseProjectManifest(args.manifest_path, alloc)
            if config == null {
                eprintln("Error: failed to load project.manifest")
                return 1
            }
            ctx.config = config.?
            cmdTest(&ctx)
        }
        Command.Init => cmdInit()
        Command.Version => {
            println("Lir Compiler v0.2.0")
            0
        }
        Command.Help => {
            printUsage()
            0
        }
        Command.Unknown => {
            eprintln("Unknown command. Run 'lir help' for usage.")
            1
        }
    }
}

// Lir Compiler — CLI Entry Point
// This is the main module that handles lir build / run / test / init commands
//
// Compile with: zig build-exe main.zig -cflags -- (or use the bootstrap compiler)
// The actual entry is in src/toolchain/cli.zig which dispatches to this

import core
import io
import json
import os

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Version
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const VERSION_MAJOR: u32 = 0
const VERSION_MINOR: u32 = 2
const VERSION_PATCH: u32 = 0

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CLI State
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct CliState {
    command: string
    verbose: bool
    release: bool
    manifest_path: string
    project_dir: string
    allocator: *Allocator
}

fn initState(alloc: *Allocator) -> CliState {
    CliState {
        command: string { ptr: "help", len: 4 }
        verbose: false
        release: false
        manifest_path: string { ptr: "project.manifest", len: 16 }
        project_dir: string { ptr: ".", len: 1 }
        allocator: alloc
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Command Dispatch
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn dispatchCommand(state: *CliState) -> i32 {
    if state.command.len == 4 and strCompare(state.command, "build") {
        return cmdBuild(state)
    }
    if state.command.len == 3 and strCompare(state.command, "run") {
        return cmdRun(state)
    }
    if state.command.len == 4 and strCompare(state.command, "test") {
        return cmdTest(state)
    }
    if state.command.len == 4 and strCompare(state.command, "init") {
        return cmdInitProject(state)
    }
    if state.command.len == 4 and strCompare(state.command, "help") {
        printHelp()
        return 0
    }
    if state.command.len == 7 and strCompare(state.command, "version") {
        printVersion()
        return 0
    }
    if state.command.len == 7 and strCompare(state.command, "clean") {
        return cmdClean(state)
    }

    eprint("Unknown command: '")
    eprint(state.command)
    eprint("'. Run 'lir help' for usage.\n")
    1
}

fn strCompare(a: string, b: string) -> bool {
    if a.len != b.len { return false }
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < a.len {
        if a.ptr[i] != b.ptr[i] { return false }
        i += 1
    }
    true
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Build Command
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn cmdBuild(state: *CliState) -> i32 {
    println("Lir Compiler v0.2.0")
    println("Building...")

    // Load manifest
    let manifest = parseProjectManifest(state.manifest_path, state.allocator)
    if manifest == null {
        eprintln("Error: project.manifest not found or invalid")
        eprintln("Run 'lir init' to create a new project")
        return 1
    }

    let config = manifest.?
    print("Project: ")
    print(config.name)
    print(" v")
    println(config.version)

    // Discover files
    var buf: [512]u8
    let entry_len = pathJoin(config.source_dir, config.entry, &buf, 512)
    let entry_path = string { ptr: &buf, len: entry_len }

    if !fileExists(entry_path) {
        eprint("Error: entry point not found: ")
        eprint(entry_path)
        eprint("\n")
        return 1
    }

    // Read entry point
    let source = readFile(entry_path, state.allocator)
    if source.isErr() {
        eprintln("Error: failed to read source file")
        return 1
    }

    let src = source.unwrap()
    print("Source: ")
    printInt(src.len as i64)
    println(" bytes")

    // TODO: Full compilation pipeline
    // For now, report success
    println("Build successful!")
    0
}

fn cmdRun(state: *CliState) -> i32 {
    let result = cmdBuild(state)
    if result != 0 { return result }
    println("Running...")
    println("(run not yet implemented)")
    0
}

fn cmdTest(state: *CliState) -> i32 {
    println("Running tests...")
    println("(test not yet implemented)")
    0
}

fn cmdClean(state: *CliState) -> i32 {
    println("Cleaning build artifacts...")
    // TODO: remove build/ directory
    println("Clean complete")
    0
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Init Command — Scaffold a new project
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn cmdInitProject(state: *CliState) -> i32 {
    println("Initializing new Lir project...")

    // Create project.manifest
    let manifest = string {
        ptr: "{\n  \"name\": \"myproject\",\n  \"version\": \"0.1.0\",\n  \"entry\": \"main.lir\",\n  \"source_dir\": \"src\",\n  \"targets\": [\"linux\"],\n  \"domains\": [],\n  \"imports\": []\n}\n",
        len: 143
    }
    if !writeFile("project.manifest", manifest) {
        eprintln("Error: could not create project.manifest")
        return 1
    }

    // Create src/ directory marker
    let main_lir = string {
        ptr: "// Main entry point for myproject\n// Lir compiler v0.2.0\n\nimport core\n\nfn main() -> i64 {\n    println(\"Hello from Lir!\")\n    0\n}\n",
        len: 99
    }
    if !writeFile("src/main.lir", main_lir) {
        eprintln("Error: could not create src/main.lir")
        return 1
    }

    // Create test file
    let test_lir = string {
        ptr: "// Tests for myproject\n\nimport core\nimport testing\n\n#[test]\nfn testHello() {\n    assert(true, \"basic assertion\")\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn testArithmetic() {\n    assertEq(2 + 2, 4)\n}\n",
        len: 148
    }
    if !writeFile("src/tests.lir", test_lir) {
        eprintln("Error: could not create src/tests.lir")
        return 1
    }

    println("Created project.manifest")
    println("Created src/main.lir")
    println("Created src/tests.lir")
    println("")
    println("Next steps:")
    println("  1. Edit src/main.lir")
    println("  2. Run 'lir build'")
    println("  3. Run 'lir test'")
    0
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Help & Version
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn printHelp() {
    println("Lir Compiler v0.2.0")
    println("")
    println("A self-contained programming language with built-in build system.")
    println("Write, build, and run projects without any external dependencies.")
    println("")
    println("Usage: lir <command> [options]")
    println("")
    println("Commands:")
    println("  build      Compile the project")
    println("  run        Compile and execute the project")
    println("  test       Run project tests")
    println("  init       Create a new project with boilerplate")
    println("  clean      Remove build artifacts")
    println("  help       Show this help message")
    println("  version    Show compiler version")
    println("")
    println("Options:")
    println("  --release      Optimize for production")
    println("  --verbose, -v  Show detailed compilation output")
    println("  --manifest     Path to manifest file (default: project.manifest)")
    println("")
    println("Project Structure:")
    println("  project.manifest    Project configuration (JSON)")
    println("  src/                Source directory")
    println("    main.lir          Entry point")
    println("    tests.lir         Test files")
    println("")
    println("Examples:")
    println("  lir init            Create a new project")
    println("  lir build           Build the project")
    println("  lir build --release Build with optimizations")
    println("  lir run             Build and run")
    println("  lir test            Run tests")
}

fn printVersion() {
    print("lir ")
    printInt(VERSION_MAJOR as i64)
    print(".")
    printInt(VERSION_MINOR as i64)
    print(".")
    printlnInt(VERSION_PATCH as i64)
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Main Entry Point
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn main() -> i64 {
    let alloc_obj = allocator_init()
    let alloc = &alloc_obj

    var state = initState(alloc)

    // Parse command from args (simplified — reads first arg)
    // In a full implementation, this would parse argc/argv
    // For now, default to build
    state.command = string { ptr: "build", len: 5 }

    dispatchCommand(&state)
}
