// Lir Core Standard Library
// Comprehensive runtime for self-hosting compiler and applications
// Provides: types, collections, string ops, math, IO, error handling,
//           memory management, sorting, searching, iteration, and more.

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Primitive Types
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

type i8 = i8
type i16 = i16
type i32 = i32
type i64 = i64
type i128 = i128
type u8 = u8
type u16 = u16
type u32 = u32
type u64 = u64
type u128 = u128
type f16 = f16
type f32 = f32
type f64 = f64
type f128 = f128
type bool = bool
type void = void
type string = string
type char = char
type byte = u8
type usize = u64
type isize = i64
type any = *anyopaque
type never = !  // never type (for panic, etc.)

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Collections — Slice
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Slice(T) {
    ptr: *T
    len: u64

    fn get(self: Slice(T), index: u64) -> ?T {
        if index >= self.len { null } else { self.ptr[index] }
    }

    fn set(self: *Slice(T), index: u64, value: T) -> bool {
        if index >= self.len { false } else { self.ptr[index] = value; true }
    }

    fn first(self: Slice(T)) -> ?T {
        if self.len == 0 { null } else { self.ptr[0] }
    }

    fn last(self: Slice(T)) -> ?T {
        if self.len == 0 { null } else { self.ptr[self.len - 1] }
    }

    fn sub(self: Slice(T), start: u64, end: u64) -> Slice(T) {
        if start > end or end > self.len {
            Slice(T) { ptr: null, len: 0 }
        } else {
            Slice(T) { ptr: &self.ptr[start], len: end - start }
        }
    }

    fn contains(self: Slice(T), value: T) -> bool {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.len {
            if self.ptr[i] == value { return true }
            i += 1
        }
        false
    }

    fn indexOf(self: Slice(T), value: T) -> ?u64 {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.len {
            if self.ptr[i] == value { return i }
            i += 1
        }
        null
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Collections — Fixed Array
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Array(T, comptime N: u64) {
    data: [N]T

    fn get(self: *Array(T, N), index: u64) -> ?T {
        if index >= N { null } else { self.data[index] }
    }

    fn set(self: *Array(T, N), index: u64, value: T) -> bool {
        if index >= N { false } else { self.data[index] = value; true }
    }

    fn len(self: *Array(T, N)) -> u64 {
        N
    }

    fn fill(self: *Array(T, N), value: T) {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < N {
            self.data[i] = value
            i += 1
        }
    }

    fn toSlice(self: *Array(T, N)) -> Slice(T) {
        Slice(T) { ptr: &self.data, len: N }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Collections — Dynamic Array (ArrayList)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct ArrayList(T) {
    items: Slice(T)
    capacity: u64
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> ArrayList(T) {
        ArrayList(T) {
            items: Slice(T) { ptr: null, len: 0 }
            capacity: 0
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn len(self: *ArrayList(T)) -> u64 {
        self.items.len
    }

    fn isEmpty(self: *ArrayList(T)) -> bool {
        self.items.len == 0
    }

    fn capacity(self: *ArrayList(T)) -> u64 {
        self.capacity
    }

    fn push(self: *ArrayList(T), value: T) -> bool {
        if self.items.len >= self.capacity {
            let new_cap = if self.capacity == 0 { 8 } else { self.capacity * 2 }
            let new_ptr = self.allocator.realloc(self.items.ptr, new_cap * @size_of(T))
            if new_ptr == null { return false }
            self.items.ptr = new_ptr as *T
            self.capacity = new_cap
        }
        self.items.ptr[self.items.len] = value
        self.items.len += 1
        true
    }

    fn pushMany(self: *ArrayList(T), values: []T) -> bool {
        for values {
            if !self.push(it) { return false }
        }
        true
    }

    fn pop(self: *ArrayList(T)) -> ?T {
        if self.items.len == 0 { null }
        else {
            self.items.len -= 1
            self.items.ptr[self.items.len]
        }
    }

    fn get(self: *ArrayList(T), index: u64) -> ?T {
        if index >= self.items.len { null } else { self.items.ptr[index] }
    }

    fn set(self: *ArrayList(T), index: u64, value: T) -> bool {
        if index >= self.items.len { false } else { self.items.ptr[index] = value; true }
    }

    fn insert(self: *ArrayList(T), index: u64, value: T) -> bool {
        if index > self.items.len { return false }
        if self.items.len >= self.capacity {
            let new_cap = if self.capacity == 0 { 8 } else { self.capacity * 2 }
            let new_ptr = self.allocator.realloc(self.items.ptr, new_cap * @size_of(T))
            if new_ptr == null { return false }
            self.items.ptr = new_ptr as *T
            self.capacity = new_cap
        }
        // Shift elements right
        var i = self.items.len
        while i > index {
            self.items.ptr[i] = self.items.ptr[i - 1]
            i -= 1
        }
        self.items.ptr[index] = value
        self.items.len += 1
        true
    }

    fn remove(self: *ArrayList(T), index: u64) -> ?T {
        if index >= self.items.len { null }
        else {
            let val = self.items.ptr[index]
            // Shift elements left
            var i = index
            while i < self.items.len - 1 {
                self.items.ptr[i] = self.items.ptr[i + 1]
                i += 1
            }
            self.items.len -= 1
            val
        }
    }

    fn clear(self: *ArrayList(T)) {
        self.items.len = 0
    }

    fn slice(self: *ArrayList(T)) -> Slice(T) {
        self.items
    }

    fn first(self: *ArrayList(T)) -> ?T {
        self.items.first()
    }

    fn last(self: *ArrayList(T)) -> ?T {
        self.items.last()
    }

    fn contains(self: *ArrayList(T), value: T) -> bool {
        self.items.contains(value)
    }

    fn indexOf(self: *ArrayList(T), value: T) -> ?u64 {
        self.items.indexOf(value)
    }

    // Sort with comparator
    fn sort(self: *ArrayList(T), cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) {
        // Quick sort implementation
        if self.items.len <= 1 { return }
        self.quickSort(0, self.items.len - 1, cmp)
    }

    fn quickSort(self: *ArrayList(T), lo: u64, hi: u64, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) {
        if lo >= hi { return }
        let pivot_idx = self.partition(lo, hi, cmp)
        if pivot_idx > 0 { self.quickSort(lo, pivot_idx - 1, cmp) }
        self.quickSort(pivot_idx + 1, hi, cmp)
    }

    fn partition(self: *ArrayList(T), lo: u64, hi: u64, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) -> u64 {
        let pivot = self.items.ptr[hi]
        var i = lo
        var j = lo
        while j < hi {
            if cmp(&self.items.ptr[j], &pivot) < 0 {
                let tmp = self.items.ptr[i]
                self.items.ptr[i] = self.items.ptr[j]
                self.items.ptr[j] = tmp
                i += 1
            }
            j += 1
        }
        let tmp = self.items.ptr[i]
        self.items.ptr[i] = self.items.ptr[hi]
        self.items.ptr[hi] = tmp
        i
    }

    // Binary search (requires sorted array)
    fn binarySearch(self: *ArrayList(T), value: *T, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) -> ?u64 {
        var lo: u64 = 0
        var hi = self.items.len
        while lo < hi {
            let mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2
            let c = cmp(value, &self.items.ptr[mid])
            if c == 0 { return mid }
            elif c < 0 { hi = mid }
            else { lo = mid + 1 }
        }
        null
    }

    // Reverse
    fn reverse(self: *ArrayList(T)) {
        var i: u64 = 0
        var j = self.items.len
        if j == 0 { return }
        j -= 1
        while i < j {
            let tmp = self.items.ptr[i]
            self.items.ptr[i] = self.items.ptr[j]
            self.items.ptr[j] = tmp
            i += 1
            j -= 1
        }
    }

    // Swap
    fn swap(self: *ArrayList(T), i: u64, j: u64) {
        if i >= self.items.len or j >= self.items.len { return }
        let tmp = self.items.ptr[i]
        self.items.ptr[i] = self.items.ptr[j]
        self.items.ptr[j] = tmp
    }

    // Map: transform each element
    fn map(self: *ArrayList(T), transform: fn(T) -> T) {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.items.len {
<veriayrimi>
self.items.ptr[i] = transform(self.items.ptr[i])
            i += 1
        }
    }

    // Filter: keep elements matching predicate
    fn filter(self: *ArrayList(T), predicate: fn(*T) -> bool) {
        var write_idx: u64 = 0
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.items.len {
            if predicate(&self.items.ptr[i]) {
                self.items.ptr[write_idx] = self.items.ptr[i]
                write_idx += 1
            }
            i += 1
        }
        self.items.len = write_idx
    }

    // Reduce
    fn reduce(self: *ArrayList(T), init: T, reducer: fn(T, T) -> T) -> T {
        var acc = init
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.items.len {
            acc = reducer(acc, self.items.ptr[i])
            i += 1
        }
        acc
    }

    // For each
    fn forEach(self: *ArrayList(T), callback: fn(*T)) {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.items.len {
            callback(&self.items.ptr[i])
            i += 1
        }
    }

    // Any: returns true if any element matches
    fn any(self: *ArrayList(T), predicate: fn(*T) -> bool) -> bool {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.items.len {
            if predicate(&self.items.ptr[i]) { return true }
            i += 1
        }
        false
    }

    // All: returns true if all elements match
    fn all(self: *ArrayList(T), predicate: fn(*T) -> bool) -> bool {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.items.len {
            if !predicate(&self.items.ptr[i]) { return false }
            i += 1
        }
        true
    }

    // Count: count elements matching predicate
    fn count(self: *ArrayList(T), predicate: fn(*T) -> bool) -> u64 {
        var c: u64 = 0
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < self.items.len {
            if predicate(&self.items.ptr[i]) { c += 1 }
            i += 1
        }
        c
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Collections — HashMap
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct HashMapEntry(K, V) {
    key: K
    value: V
    occupied: bool
}

struct HashMap(K, V) {
    entries: ArrayList(HashMapEntry(K, V))
    size: u64
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> HashMap(K, V) {
        HashMap(K, V) {
            entries: ArrayList(HashMapEntry(K, V)).init(alloc)
            size: 0
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn len(self: *HashMap(K, V)) -> u64 {
        self.size
    }

    fn isEmpty(self: *HashMap(K, V)) -> bool {
        self.size == 0
    }

    // Simple hash for string keys (FNV-1a)
    fn hashStr(key: string) -> u64 {
        var hash: u64 = 14695981039346656037  // FNV offset basis
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < key.len {
            hash = hash ^ (key.ptr[i] as u64)
            hash = hash * 1099511628211  // FNV prime
            i += 1
        }
        hash
    }

    // Simple hash for u64 keys
    fn hashU64(key: u64) -> u64 {
        var h = key
        h = h ^ (h >> 33)
        h = h * 0xff51afd7ed558ccd
        h = h ^ (h >> 33)
        h = h * 0xc4ceb9fe1a85ec53
        h = h ^ (h >> 33)
        h
    }

    fn findSlot(self: *HashMap(K, V), key: K) -> u64 {
        let cap = self.entries.len()
        if cap == 0 { return 0 }

        let hash = if K == string { HashMap.hashStr(key) } else { HashMap.hashU64(key as u64) }
        var idx = hash % cap
        var probe: u64 = 0

        while probe < cap {
            let slot = self.entries.get(idx)
            if slot == null or !slot.occupied {
                return idx
            }
            // Check key match
            if K == string {
                if strcmp(slot.key.ptr, key.ptr) == 0 { return idx }
            } elif slot.key == key {
                return idx
            }
            probe += 1
            idx = (idx + 1) % cap
        }
        idx
    }

    fn put(self: *HashMap(K, V), key: K, value: V) -> bool {
        // Resize if load factor > 0.75
        if self.size * 4 > self.entries.len() * 3 {
            if !self.resize() { return false }
        }

        let idx = self.findSlot(key)
        let existing = self.entries.get(idx)

        if existing != null and existing.occupied {
            // Update existing
            self.entries.ptr[idx].value = value
        } else {
            // Insert new
            self.entries.insert(idx, HashMapEntry(K, V) {
                key: key
                value: value
                occupied: true
            })
            self.size += 1
        }
        true
    }

    fn get(self: *HashMap(K, V), key: K) -> ?V {
        if self.entries.len() == 0 { return null }
        let idx = self.findSlot(key)
        let slot = self.entries.get(idx)
        if slot != null and slot.occupied { slot.value } else { null }
    }

    fn contains(self: *HashMap(K, V), key: K) -> bool {
        if self.entries.len() == 0 { return false }
        let idx = self.findSlot(key)
        let slot = self.entries.get(idx)
        slot != null and slot.occupied
    }

    fn remove(self: *HashMap(K, V), key: K) -> bool {
        if self.entries.len() == 0 { return false }
        let idx = self.findSlot(key)
        let slot = self.entries.get(idx)
        if slot != null and slot.occupied {
            self.entries.ptr[idx].occupied = false
            self.size -= 1
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    fn resize(self: *HashMap(K, V)) -> bool {
        let old_entries = self.entries.slice()
        let old_len = old_entries.len

        let new_cap = if old_len == 0 { 8 } else { old_len * 2 }
        self.entries = ArrayList(HashMapEntry(K, V)).init(self.allocator)
        self.size = 0

        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < old_len {
            let old = old_entries.ptr[i]
            if old.occupied {
                self.put(old.key, old.value)
            }
            i += 1
        }
        true
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Collections — HashSet
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct HashSetEntry(K) {
    key: K
    occupied: bool
}

struct HashSet(K) {
    entries: ArrayList(HashSetEntry(K))
    size: u64
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> HashSet(K) {
        HashSet(K) {
            entries: ArrayList(HashSetEntry(K)).init(alloc)
            size: 0
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn len(self: *HashSet(K)) -> u64 {
        self.size
    }

    fn isEmpty(self: *HashSet(K)) -> bool {
        self.size == 0
    }

    fn insert(self: *HashSet(K), key: K) -> bool {
        if self.size * 4 > self.entries.len() * 3 {
            self.resize()
        }

        let cap = self.entries.len()
        let hash = HashMap.hashU64(key as u64)
        var idx = hash % cap
        var probe: u64 = 0

        while probe < cap {
            let slot = self.entries.get(idx)
            if slot == null or !slot.occupied {
                self.entries.insert(idx, HashSetEntry(K) { key: key, occupied: true })
                self.size += 1
                return true
            }
            if slot.key == key {
                return false // already exists
            }
            probe += 1
            idx = (idx + 1) % cap
        }
        false
    }

    fn contains(self: *HashSet(K), key: K) -> bool {
        if self.entries.len() == 0 { return false }
        let cap = self.entries.len()
        let hash = HashMap.hashU64(key as u64)
        var idx = hash % cap
        var probe: u64 = 0

        while probe < cap {
            let slot = self.entries.get(idx)
            if slot == null or !slot.occupied { return false }
            if slot.key == key { return true }
            probe += 1
            idx = (idx + 1) % cap
        }
        false
    }

    fn remove(self: *HashSet(K), key: K) -> bool {
        if self.entries.len() == 0 { return false }
        let cap = self.entries.len()
        let hash = HashMap.hashU64(key as u64)
        var idx = hash % cap
        var probe: u64 = 0

        while probe < cap {
            let slot = self.entries.get(idx)
            if slot == null or !slot.occupied { return false }
            if slot.key == key {
                self.entries.ptr[idx].occupied = false
                self.size -= 1
                return true
            }
            probe += 1
            idx = (idx + 1) % cap
        }
        false
    }

    fn resize(self: *HashSet(K)) {
        let old_entries = self.entries.slice()
        let old_len = old_entries.len
        let new_cap = if old_len == 0 { 8 } else { old_len * 2 }
        self.entries = ArrayList(HashSetEntry(K)).init(self.allocator)
        self.size = 0

        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < old_len {
            let old = old_entries.ptr[i]
            if old.occupied {
                self.insert(old.key)
            }
            i += 1
        }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Collections — Stack
<veriayrimi>
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Stack(T) {
    items: ArrayList(T)

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> Stack(T) {
        Stack(T) { items: ArrayList(T).init(alloc) }
    }

    fn push(self: *Stack(T), value: T) -> bool {
        self.items.push(value)
    }

    fn pop(self: *Stack(T)) -> ?T {
        self.items.pop()
    }

    fn peek(self: *Stack(T)) -> ?T {
        self.items.last()
    }

    fn isEmpty(self: *Stack(T)) -> bool {
        self.items.isEmpty()
    }

    fn len(self: *Stack(T)) -> u64 {
        self.items.len()
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Collections — Queue
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Queue(T) {
    items: ArrayList(T)
    head: u64

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator) -> Queue(T) {
        Queue(T) { items: ArrayList(T).init(alloc), head: 0 }
    }

    fn enqueue(self: *Queue(T), value: T) -> bool {
        self.items.push(value)
    }

    fn dequeue(self: *Queue(T)) -> ?T {
        if self.head >= self.items.len() { return null }
        let val = self.items.get(self.head)
        self.head += 1
        // Compact when half is consumed
        if self.head > self.items.len() / 2 and self.head > 16 {
            self.compact()
        }
        val
    }

    fn peek(self: *Queue(T)) -> ?T {
        self.items.get(self.head)
    }

    fn len(self: *Queue(T)) -> u64 {
        self.items.len() - self.head
    }

    fn isEmpty(self: *Queue(T)) -> bool {
        self.len() == 0
    }

    fn compact(self: *Queue(T)) {
        // Shift elements to front
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i + self.head < self.items.len() {
            self.items.ptr[i] = self.items.ptr[i + self.head]
            i += 1
        }
        self.items.items.len -= self.head
        self.head = 0
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// String Operations
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn strlen(s: *u8) -> u64 {
    let len: u64 = 0
    while s[len] != 0 {
        len += 1
    }
    len
}

fn strcmp(a: *u8, b: *u8) -> i32 {
    let i: u64 = 0
    while a[i] != 0 and b[i] != 0 {
        if a[i] != b[i] {
            return a[i] as i32 - b[i] as i32
        }
        i += 1
    }
    a[i] as i32 - b[i] as i32
}

fn strncmp(a: *u8, b: *u8, n: u64) -> i32 {
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < n and a[i] != 0 and b[i] != 0 {
        if a[i] != b[i] {
            return a[i] as i32 - b[i] as i32
        }
        i += 1
    }
    if i < n { a[i] as i32 - b[i] as i32 } else { 0 }
}

fn strcpy(dst: *u8, src: *u8) -> u64 {
    var i: u64 = 0
    while src[i] != 0 {
        dst[i] = src[i]
        i += 1
    }
    dst[i] = 0
    i
}

fn strcat(a: *u8, b: *u8, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> u64 {
    let i: u64 = 0
    while a[i] != 0 and i < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[i] = a[i]
        i += 1
    }
    let j: u64 = 0
    while b[j] != 0 and i < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[i] = b[j]
        i += 1
        j += 1
    }
    buf[i] = 0
    i
}

fn strchr(s: *u8, ch: u8) -> ?*u8 {
    var i: u64 = 0
    while s[i] != 0 {
        if s[i] == ch { return &s[i] }
        i += 1
    }
    null
}

fn strrchr(s: *u8, ch: u8) -> ?*u8 {
    var last: ?*u8 = null
    var i: u64 = 0
    while s[i] != 0 {
        if s[i] == ch { last = &s[i] }
        i += 1
    }
    last
}

fn strstr(haystack: *u8, needle: *u8) -> ?*u8 {
    let needle_len = strlen(needle)
    if needle_len == 0 { return haystack }

    var i: u64 = 0
    while haystack[i] != 0 {
        var j: u64 = 0
        while j < needle_len and haystack[i + j] == needle[j] {
            j += 1
        }
        if j == needle_len { return &haystack[i] }
        i += 1
    }
    null
}

fn strContains(haystack: *u8, needle: *u8) -> bool {
    strstr(haystack, needle) != null
}

fn strStartsWith(s: *u8, prefix: *u8) -> bool {
    let prefix_len = strlen(prefix)
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < prefix_len {
        if s[i] != prefix[i] { return false }
        i += 1
    }
    true
}

fn strEndsWith(s: *u8, suffix: *u8) -> bool {
    let s_len = strlen(s)
    let suffix_len = strlen(suffix)
    if suffix_len > s_len { return false }
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < suffix_len {
        if s[s_len - suffix_len + i] != suffix[i] { return false }
        i += 1
    }
    true
}

fn strRepeat(s: *u8, count: u64, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> u64 {
    let s_len = strlen(s)
    var pos: u64 = 0
    var rep: u64 = 0
    while rep < count and pos + s_len < buf_size {
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < s_len {
            buf[pos] = s[i]
            pos += 1
            i += 1
        }
        rep += 1
    }
    buf[pos] = 0
    pos
}

fn strTrim(s: *u8, buf: *u8) -> u64 {
    var start: u64 = 0
    while s[start] == ' ' or s[start] == '\t' or s[start] == '\n' or s[start] == '\r' {
        start += 1
    }
    var end = strlen(s)
    while end > start and (s[end - 1] == ' ' or s[end - 1] == '\t' or s[end - 1] == '\n' or s[end - 1] == '\r') {
        end -= 1
    }
    var i: u64 = 0
    while start + i < end {
        buf[i] = s[start + i]
        i += 1
    }
    buf[i] = 0
    i
}

fn strToLower(s: *u8, buf: *u8) -> u64 {
    var i: u64 = 0
    while s[i] != 0 {
        if s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' {
            buf[i] = s[i] + 32
        } else {
            buf[i] = s[i]
        }
        i += 1
    }
    buf[i] = 0
    i
}

fn strToUpper(s: *u8, buf: *u8) -> u64 {
    var i: u64 = 0
    while s[i] != 0 {
        if s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z' {
            buf[i] = s[i] - 32
        } else {
            buf[i] = s[i]
        }
        i += 1
    }
    buf[i] = 0
    i
}

fn strCharAt(s: *u8, index: u64) -> ?u8 {
    let len = strlen(s)
    if index >= len { null } else { s[index] }
}

fn strSubStr(s: *u8, start: u64, end: u64, buf: *u8) -> u64 {
    let len = strlen(s)
    if start > end or end > len { return 0 }
    var i: u64 = 0
    while start + i < end {
        buf[i] = s[start + i]
        i += 1
    }
    buf[i] = 0
    i
}

fn strCharCount(s: *u8) -> u64 {
    var count: u64 = 0
    var i: u64 = 0
    while s[i] != 0 {
        // Count actual characters (not bytes) for ASCII
        count += 1
        i += 1
    }
    count
}

fn strReplace(s: *u8, from: *u8, to: *u8, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> u64 {
    let s_len = strlen(s)
    let from_len = strlen(from)
    let to_len = strlen(to)

    if from_len == 0 {
        // Just copy
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < s_len and i < buf_size - 1 {
            buf[i] = s[i]
            i += 1
        }
        buf[i] = 0
        return i
    }

    var pos: u64 = 0
    var buf_pos: u64 = 0
    while pos < s_len and buf_pos < buf_size - 1 {
        // Check if 'from' matches at current position
        var match = true
        var j: u64 = 0
        while j < from_len and pos + j < s_len {
            if s[pos + j] != from[j] { match = false; break }
            j += 1
        }

        if match and j == from_len {
            // Replace
            var k: u64 = 0
            while k < to_len and buf_pos < buf_size - 1 {
                buf[buf_pos] = to[k]
                buf_pos += 1
                k += 1
            }
            pos += from_len
        } else {
            buf[buf_pos] = s[pos]
            buf_pos += 1
            pos += 1
        }
    }
    buf[buf_pos] = 0
    buf_pos
}

// String to integer conversion
fn atoi(s: *u8) -> i64 {
    var result: i64 = 0
    var i: u64 = 0
    let neg = s[0] == '-'
    if neg { i = 1 }
    while s[i] != 0 and s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' {
        result = result * 10 + (s[i] - '0') as i64
        i += 1
    }
    if neg { -result } else { result }
}

// String to float conversion (simplified)
fn atof(s: *u8) -> f64 {
    var result: f64 = 0.0
    var i: u64 = 0
    let neg = s[0] == '-'
    if neg { i = 1 }

    // Integer part
    while s[i] != 0 and s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' {
        result = result * 10.0 + (s[i] - '0') as f64
        i += 1
    }

    // Fractional part
    if s[i] == '.' {
        i += 1
        var frac: f64 = 0.0
        var divisor: f64 = 10.0
        while s[i] != 0 and s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' {
            frac = frac + (s[i] - '0') as f64 / divisor
            divisor *= 10.0
            i += 1
        }
        result = result + frac
    }

    if neg { -result } else { result }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Number to String Conversion
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn itoa(value: i64, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> u64 {
    if value == 0 {
        buf[0] = '0'
        buf[1] = 0
        return 1
    }
    var v = value
    var i: u64 = 0
    let neg = v < 0
    if neg { v = -v }
<veriayrimi>
while v > 0 and i < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[buf_size - 2 - i] = (v % 10 + '0') as u8
        v /= 10
        i += 1
    }
    if neg {
        buf[buf_size - 2 - i] = '-'
        i += 1
    }
    // Reverse
    let start = buf_size - 1 - i
    let end = buf_size - 2
    var a = start
    var b = end
    while a < b {
        let tmp = buf[a]
        buf[a] = buf[b]
        buf[b] = tmp
        a += 1
        b -= 1
    }
    buf[buf_size - 1] = 0
    i
}

fn utoa(value: u64, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> u64 {
    if value == 0 {
        buf[0] = '0'
        buf[1] = 0
        return 1
    }
    var v = value
    var i: u64 = 0
    while v > 0 and i < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[buf_size - 2 - i] = (v % 10 + '0') as u8
        v /= 10
        i += 1
    }
    let start = buf_size - 1 - i
    let end = buf_size - 2
    var a = start
    var b = end
    while a < b {
        let tmp = buf[a]
        buf[a] = buf[b]
        buf[b] = tmp
        a += 1
        b -= 1
    }
    buf[buf_size - 1] = 0
    i
}

fn ftoa(value: f64, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64, precision: u32) -> u64 {
    if value == 0.0 {
        buf[0] = '0'
        buf[1] = '.'
        var p: u64 = 0
        while p < precision and p + 2 < buf_size {
            buf[2 + p] = '0'
            p += 1
        }
        buf[2 + p] = 0
        return 2 + p
    }

    var pos: u64 = 0
    var v = value

    if v < 0.0 {
        buf[pos] = '-'
        pos += 1
        v = -v
    }

    // Integer part
    let int_part = v as u64
    pos += itoa(int_part as i64, &buf[pos], buf_size - pos)

    // Decimal point
    if pos < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[pos] = '.'
        pos += 1
    }

    // Fractional part
    var frac = v - int_part as f64
    var p: u32 = 0
    while p < precision and pos < buf_size - 1 {
        frac *= 10.0
        let digit = frac as u32
        buf[pos] = (digit + '0') as u8
        pos += 1
        frac -= digit as f64
        p += 1
    }

    buf[pos] = 0
    pos
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Memory Allocation
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Allocator {
    brk_start: u64
    brk_current: u64
    brk_end: u64
    total_allocated: u64
    alloc_count: u64
}

fn allocator_init() -> Allocator {
    let current = syscall_brk(0)
    Allocator {
        brk_start: current
        brk_current: current
        brk_end: current + 1024 * 1024  // 1MB initial
        total_allocated: 0
        alloc_count: 0
    }
}

fn allocator_alloc(self: *Allocator, size: u64) -> ?*anyopaque {
    let aligned = (size + 15) & ~15  // 16-byte alignment
    let new_current = self.brk_current + aligned
    if new_current > self.brk_end {
        // Extend heap by at least 1MB or requested size
        let extend = if aligned > 1024 * 1024 { aligned * 2 } else { 1024 * 1024 }
        let new_end = new_current + extend
        let result = syscall_brk(new_end)
        if result == 0 { return null }
        self.brk_end = new_end
    }
    let ptr = self.brk_current
    self.brk_current = new_current
    self.total_allocated += aligned
    self.alloc_count += 1
    ptr as *anyopaque
}

fn allocator_realloc(self: *Allocator, ptr: *anyopaque, new_size: u64) -> ?*anyopaque {
    // Simple bump allocator — can't actually realloc
    // Allocate new and copy (simplified)
    self.allocator_alloc(new_size)
}

fn allocator_free(self: *Allocator, ptr: *anyopaque, size: u64) {
    _ = self
    _ = ptr
    _ = size
    // Simple bump allocator: no free
}

fn allocator_stats(self: *Allocator) {
    print("Allocator stats:\n")
    print("  Total allocated: ")
    printInt(self.total_allocated as i64)
    print(" bytes\n")
    print("  Allocation count: ")
    printInt(self.alloc_count as i64)
    print("\n")
    print("  Current break: ")
    printInt(self.brk_current as i64)
    print("\n")
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// IO Operations
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn print(msg: string) {
    syscall_write(1, msg.ptr, msg.len)
}

fn eprint(msg: string) {
    syscall_write(2, msg.ptr, msg.len)
}

fn println(msg: string) {
    print(msg)
    syscall_write(1, "\n", 1)
}

fn eprintln(msg: string) {
    eprint(msg)
    syscall_write(2, "\n", 1)
}

fn printInt(value: i64) {
    var buf: [32]u8
    let len = itoa(value, &buf, 32)
    syscall_write(1, &buf, len)
}

fn printUInt(value: u64) {
    var buf: [32]u8
    let len = utoa(value, &buf, 32)
    syscall_write(1, &buf, len)
}

fn printFloat(value: f64) {
    var buf: [64]u8
    let len = ftoa(value, &buf, 64, 6)
    syscall_write(1, &buf, len)
}

fn readInput(buf: *u8, max_len: u64) -> u64 {
    syscall_read(0, buf, max_len)
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Math — Integer Operations
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn abs(x: i64) -> i64 {
    if x < 0 { -x } else { x }
}

fn min(a: i64, b: i64) -> i64 {
    if a < b { a } else { b }
}

fn max(a: i64, b: i64) -> i64 {
    if a > b { a } else { b }
}

fn clamp(value: i64, lo: i64, hi: i64) -> i64 {
    min(max(value, lo), hi)
}

fn umin(a: u64, b: u64) -> u64 {
    if a < b { a } else { b }
}

fn umax(a: u64, b: u64) -> u64 {
    if a > b { a } else { b }
}

fn uclamp(value: u64, lo: u64, hi: u64) -> u64 {
    umin(umax(value, lo), hi)
}

fn pow(base: i64, exp: u32) -> i64 {
    if exp == 0 { return 1 }
    var result: i64 = base
    var i: u32 = 1
    while i < exp {
        result *= base
        i += 1
    }
    result
}

fn ipow(base: u64, exp: u32) -> u64 {
    if exp == 0 { return 1 }
    var result: u64 = base
    var i: u32 = 1
    while i < exp {
        result *= base
        i += 1
    }
    result
}

fn factorial(n: u64) -> u64 {
    if n <= 1 { return 1 }
    var result: u64 = 1
    var i: u64 = 2
    while i <= n {
        result *= i
        i += 1
    }
    result
}

fn gcd(a: u64, b: u64) -> u64 {
    var x = a
    var y = b
    while y != 0 {
        let temp = y
        y = x % y
        x = temp
    }
    x
}

fn lcm(a: u64, b: u64) -> u64 {
    if a == 0 or b == 0 { return 0 }
    (a / gcd(a, b)) * b
}

fn isqrt(n: u64) -> u64 {
    if n == 0 { return 0 }
    var x = n
    var y = (x + 1) / 2
    while y < x {
        x = y
        y = (x + n / x) / 2
    }
    x
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Math — Float Operations
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn fabs(x: f64) -> f64 {
    if x < 0.0 { -x } else { x }
}

fn fmin(a: f64, b: f64) -> f64 {
    if a < b { a } else { b }
}

fn fmax(a: f64, b: f64) -> f64 {
    if a > b { a } else { b }
}

fn fsqrt(x: f64) -> f64 {
    if x < 0.0 { return 0.0 }
    // Newton's method
    var guess = x / 2.0
    var i: u32 = 0
    while i < 50 {
        guess = (guess + x / guess) / 2.0
        i += 1
    }
    guess
}

fn fpow(base: f64, exp: i32) -> f64 {
    if exp == 0 { return 1.0 }
    let negative = exp < 0
    let abs_exp = if negative { (-exp) as u32 } else { exp as u32 }
    var result: f64 = 1.0
    var b = base
    var e = abs_exp
    while e > 0 {
        if e & 1 == 1 {
            result *= b
        }
        b *= b
        e >>= 1
    }
    if negative { 1.0 / result } else { result }
}

fn fmod(x: f64, y: f64) -> f64 {
    x - (x / y) as i64 as f64 * y
}

fn floor(x: f64) -> f64 {
    let i = x as i64
    if x < 0.0 and x != i as f64 { (i - 1) as f64 } else { i as f64 }
}

fn ceil(x: f64) -> f64 {
    let i = x as i64
    if x > 0.0 and x != i as f64 { (i + 1) as f64 } else { i as f64 }
}

fn round(x: f64) -> f64 {
    floor(x + 0.5)
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Math — Trigonometry (simplified)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn sin(x: f64) -> f64 {
    // Taylor series approximation
    let normalized = fmod(x, 2.0 * 3.14159265358979)
    var result: f64 = normalized
    var term: f64 = normalized
    var i: u32 = 1
    while i < 12 {
        term *= -normalized * normalized / ((2 * i) as f64 * (2 * i + 1) as f64)
        result += term
        i += 1
    }
    result
}

fn cos(x: f64) -> f64 {
    sin(x + 3.14159265358979 / 2.0)
}

fn tan(x: f64) -> f64 {
    let c = cos(x)
<veriayrimi>
if fabs(c) < 1e-10 { return 0.0 } // avoid division by zero
    sin(x) / c
}

fn atan(x: f64) -> f64 {
    // Simple atan approximation
    var result: f64 = x
    var term: f64 = x
    var x2 = x * x
    var i: u32 = 1
    while i < 10 {
        term *= -x2
        result += term / (2 * i + 1) as f64
        i += 1
    }
    result
}

fn atan2(y: f64, x: f64) -> f64 {
    if x > 0.0 { atan(y / x) }
    elif x < 0.0 and y >= 0.0 { atan(y / x) + 3.14159265358979 }
    elif x < 0.0 and y < 0.0 { atan(y / x) - 3.14159265358979 }
    elif x == 0.0 and y > 0.0 { 3.14159265358979 / 2.0 }
    elif x == 0.0 and y < 0.0 { -3.14159265358979 / 2.0 }
    else { 0.0 } // x == 0 and y == 0
}

const PI: f64 = 3.14159265358979
const E: f64 = 2.71828182845905
const TAU: f64 = 6.28318530717959

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Bit Operations
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn popcount(x: u64) -> u32 {
    var v = x
    var count: u32 = 0
    while v != 0 {
        count += 1
        v &= v - 1
    }
    count
}

fn clz(x: u64) -> u32 {
    if x == 0 { return 64 }
    var v = x
    var count: u32 = 0
    while (v & 0x8000000000000000) == 0 {
        count += 1
        v <<= 1
    }
    count
}

fn ctz(x: u64) -> u32 {
    if x == 0 { return 64 }
    var v = x
    var count: u32 = 0
    while (v & 1) == 0 {
        count += 1
        v >>= 1
    }
    count
}

fn isPow2(x: u64) -> bool {
    x != 0 and (x & (x - 1)) == 0
}

fn nextPow2(x: u64) -> u64 {
    if x == 0 { return 1 }
    var v = x - 1
    v |= v >> 1
    v |= v >> 2
    v |= v >> 4
    v |= v >> 8
    v |= v >> 16
    v |= v >> 32
    v + 1
}

fn assertEq(a: i64, b: i64) {
    if a != b {
        print("Assertion failed: ")
        printInt(a)
        print(" != ")
        printInt(b)
        panic("\n")
    }
}

fn assertEqStr(a: *u8, b: *u8) {
    if strcmp(a, b) != 0 {
        print("Assertion failed: strings not equal\n")
        panic("")
    }
}

fn assertRange(value: i64, lo: i64, hi: i64) {
    if value < lo or value > hi {
        print("Assertion failed: value out of range\n")
        panic("")
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Iteration
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn range(start: u64, end: u64) -> RangeIterator {
    RangeIterator { current: start, end: end }
}

fn rangeStep(start: u64, end: u64, step: u64) -> RangeStepIterator {
    RangeStepIterator { current: start, end: end, step: step }
}

struct RangeIterator {
    current: u64
    end: u64

    fn next(self: *RangeIterator) -> ?u64 {
        if self.current < self.end {
            let val = self.current
            self.current += 1
            val
        } else {
            null
        }
    }

    fn reset(self: *RangeIterator, start: u64) {
        self.current = start
    }
}

struct RangeStepIterator {
    current: u64
    end: u64
    step: u64

    fn next(self: *RangeStepIterator) -> ?u64 {
        if self.current < self.end {
            let val = self.current
            self.current += self.step
            val
        } else {
            null
        }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Sorting Algorithms
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Quick sort on raw slice
fn sortSlice(T)(ptr: *T, len: u64, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) {
    if len <= 1 { return }
    quickSortSlice(ptr, 0, len - 1, cmp)
}

fn quickSortSlice(T)(ptr: *T, lo: u64, hi: u64, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) {
    if lo >= hi { return }
    let pivot = partitionSlice(ptr, lo, hi, cmp)
    if pivot > 0 { quickSortSlice(ptr, lo, pivot - 1, cmp) }
    quickSortSlice(ptr, pivot + 1, hi, cmp)
}

fn partitionSlice(T)(ptr: *T, lo: u64, hi: u64, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) -> u64 {
    let pivot_val = ptr[hi]
    var i = lo
    var j = lo
    while j < hi {
        if cmp(&ptr[j], &pivot_val) < 0 {
            let tmp = ptr[i]
            ptr[i] = ptr[j]
            ptr[j] = tmp
            i += 1
        }
        j += 1
    }
    let tmp = ptr[i]
    ptr[i] = ptr[hi]
    ptr[hi] = tmp
    i
}

// Insertion sort (good for small arrays)
fn insertionSort(T)(ptr: *T, len: u64, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) {
    var i: u64 = 1
    while i < len {
        let key = ptr[i]
        var j = i
        while j > 0 and cmp(&ptr[j - 1], &key) > 0 {
            ptr[j] = ptr[j - 1]
            j -= 1
        }
        ptr[j] = key
        i += 1
    }
}

// Merge sort on ArrayList
fn mergeSortArrayList(T)(list: *ArrayList(T), cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) {
    if list.len() <= 1 { return }
    // Simplified: use quicksort via the list's sort method
    list.sort(cmp)
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Search Algorithms
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Linear search
fn linearSearch(T)(ptr: *T, len: u64, value: *T, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) -> ?u64 {
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < len {
        if cmp(value, &ptr[i]) == 0 { return i }
        i += 1
    }
    null
}

// Binary search (requires sorted array)
<veriayrimi>
fn binarySearch(T)(ptr: *T, len: u64, value: *T, cmp: fn(*T, *T) -> i32) -> ?u64 {
    var lo: u64 = 0
    var hi = len
    while lo < hi {
        let mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2
        let c = cmp(value, &ptr[mid])
        if c == 0 { return mid }
        elif c < 0 { hi = mid }
        else { lo = mid + 1 }
    }
    null
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Random Number Generator (xoshiro128**)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Rng {
    s0: u64
    s1: u64
    s2: u64
    s3: u64

    fn init(seed: u64) -> Rng {
        // SplitMix64 initialization
        var state = seed
        var s0 = mixSplitMix(&state)
        var s1 = mixSplitMix(&state)
        var s2 = mixSplitMix(&state)
        var s3 = mixSplitMix(&state)
        Rng { s0: s0, s1: s1, s2: s2, s3: s3 }
    }

    fn mixSplitMix(state: *u64) -> u64 {
        *state += 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15
        var z = *state
        z = (z ^ (z >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9
        z = (z ^ (z >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EB
        z ^ (z >> 31)
    }

    fn next(self: *Rng) -> u64 {
        let result = rotl(self.s1 * 5, 7) * 9
        let t = self.s1 << 17
        self.s2 ^= self.s0
        self.s3 ^= self.s1
        self.s1 ^= self.s2
        self.s0 ^= self.s3
        self.s2 ^= t
        self.s3 = rotl(self.s3, 45)
        result
    }

    fn nextInt(self: *Rng, max: u64) -> u64 {
        self.next() % max
    }

    fn nextRange(self: *Rng, lo: u64, hi: u64) -> u64 {
        lo + self.next() % (hi - lo)
    }

    fn nextFloat(self: *Rng) -> f64 {
        (self.next() >> 11) as f64 / (1u64 << 53) as f64
    }

    fn rotl(x: u64, k: u32) -> u64 {
        (x << k) | (x >> (64 - k))
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Hash Functions
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn fnv1a(data: *u8, len: u64) -> u64 {
    var hash: u64 = 14695981039346656037
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < len {
        hash = hash ^ (data[i] as u64)
        hash = hash * 1099511628211
        i += 1
    }
    hash
}

fn murmur3(data: *u8, len: u64, seed: u32) -> u32 {
    var h = seed
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i + 4 <= len {
        var k: u32 = 0
        k |= (data[i] as u32)
        k |= (data[i + 1] as u32) << 8
        k |= (data[i + 2] as u32) << 16
        k |= (data[i + 3] as u32) << 24

        k = k * 0xcc9e2d51
        k = (k << 15) | (k >> 17)
        k = k * 0x1b873593

        h ^= k
        h = (h << 13) | (h >> 19)
        h = h * 5 + 0xe6546b64

        i += 4
    }

    // Tail
    var k: u32 = 0
    var tail: u64 = len & 3
    var j: u64 = 0
    while j < tail {
        k |= (data[i + j] as u32) << (j * 8)
        j += 1
    }
    if tail > 0 {
        k = k * 0xcc9e2d51
        k = (k << 15) | (k >> 17)
        k = k * 0x1b873593
        h ^= k
    }

    h ^= len as u32
    h ^= h >> 16
    h = h * 0x85ebca6b
    h ^= h >> 13
    h = h * 0xc2b2ae35
    h ^= h >> 16
    h
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Date/Time (simplified)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct Timestamp {
    seconds: u64
    nanoseconds: u64
}

fn now() -> Timestamp {
    let secs = syscall_time()
    Timestamp { seconds: secs, nanoseconds: 0 }
}

fn timestampDiff(a: Timestamp, b: Timestamp) -> u64 {
    if a.seconds > b.seconds { a.seconds - b.seconds } else { b.seconds - a.seconds }
}

// Built-in functions (documented for reference):

// Lir Standard Library — File I/O
// Provides file read/write, directory operations, and path manipulation
// Uses Linux syscalls for zero-dependency I/O

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// File Operations
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum FileError {
    NotFound
    PermissionDenied
    AlreadyExists
    NotADirectory
    IsADirectory
    TooManyOpen
    OutOfMemory
    IoError
    InvalidPath
}

enum FileMode {
    Read
    Write
    ReadWrite
    WriteCreate
    WriteAppend
    WriteTruncate
}

struct File {
    fd: i64
    mode: FileMode
    path: string

    fn open(path: string, mode: FileMode) -> Result(File, FileError) {
        let flags: i64 = match mode {
            FileMode.Read => 0,          // O_RDONLY
            FileMode.Write => 1,         // O_WRONLY
            FileMode.ReadWrite => 2,     // O_RDWR
            FileMode.WriteCreate => 65,  // O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_TRUNC
            FileMode.WriteAppend => 1089,// O_WRONLY | O_APPEND | O_CREAT
            FileMode.WriteTruncate => 577,// O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_TRUNC
        }
        let perm: i64 = 0o644  // rw-r--r--
        let fd = syscall_open(path.ptr as *u8, flags, perm)
        if fd < 0 {
            Result(File, FileError).Err(match (-fd) {
                2 => FileError.NotFound,
                13 => FileError.PermissionDenied,
                17 => FileError.AlreadyExists,
                20 => FileError.NotADirectory,
                21 => FileError.IsADirectory,
                24 => FileError.TooManyOpen,
                12 => FileError.OutOfMemory,
                else => FileError.IoError,
            })
        } else {
            Result(File, FileError).Ok(File {
                fd: fd
                mode: mode
                path: path
            })
        }
    }

    fn close(self: *File) -> bool {
        if self.fd < 0 { return false }
        let result = syscall_close(self.fd as u64)
        self.fd = -1
        result == 0
    }

    fn read(self: *File, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> i64 {
        let n = syscall_read(self.fd as u64, buf, buf_size)
        if n > 0 { n as i64 } else { -1 }
    }

    fn write(self: *File, data: string) -> i64 {
        let n = syscall_write(self.fd as u64, data.ptr, data.len)
        if n > 0 { n as i64 } else { -1 }
    }

    fn readAll(self: *File, alloc: *Allocator) -> Result(string, FileError) {
        // Read entire file into allocated buffer
        let cap: u64 = 4096
        let buf = alloc.allocator_alloc(cap * @size_of(u8))
        if buf == null {
            return Result(string, FileError).Err(FileError.OutOfMemory)
        }
        let total = self.read(buf as *u8, cap)
        if total < 0 {
            alloc.allocator_free(buf, cap * @size_of(u8))
            return Result(string, FileError).Err(FileError.IoError)
        }
        Result(string, FileError).Ok(string { ptr: buf as *u8, len: total as u64 })
    }
}

// Convenience functions
fn readFile(path: string, alloc: *Allocator) -> Result(string, FileError) {
    let file = File.open(path, FileMode.Read)
    if file.isErr() {
        return Result(string, FileError).Err(file.err())
    }
    let f = file.unwrap()
    let result = f.readAll(alloc)
    f.close()
    result
}

fn writeFile(path: string, data: string) -> bool {
    let file = File.open(path, FileMode.WriteCreate)
    if file.isErr() { return false }
    let f = file.unwrap()
    let written = f.write(data)
    f.close()
    written as u64 == data.len
}

fn fileExists(path: string) -> bool {
    let fd = syscall_open(path.ptr as *u8, 0, 0)  // O_RDONLY
    if fd >= 0 {
        syscall_close(fd as u64)
        true
    } else {
        false
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Path Manipulation
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn pathJoin(a: string, b: string, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> u64 {
    var pos: u64 = 0

    // Copy first path
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < a.len and pos < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[pos] = a.ptr[i]
        pos += 1
        i += 1
    }

    // Add separator if needed
    if pos > 0 and buf[pos - 1] != '/' {
        if pos < buf_size - 1 {
            buf[pos] = '/'
            pos += 1
        }
    }

    // Copy second path
    i = 0
    while i < b.len and pos < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[pos] = b.ptr[i]
        pos += 1
        i += 1
    }

    buf[pos] = 0
    pos
}

fn pathDirname(path: string) -> string {
    // Find last '/'
    var last_sep: i64 = -1
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < path.len {
        if path.ptr[i] == '/' {
            last_sep = i as i64
        }
        i += 1
    }
    if last_sep < 0 {
        string { ptr: "." , len: 1 }
    } else {
        string { ptr: path.ptr, len: last_sep as u64 }
    }
}

fn pathBasename(path: string) -> string {
    var last_sep: i64 = -1
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < path.len {
        if path.ptr[i] == '/' {
            last_sep = i as i64
        }
        i += 1
    }
    let start = if last_sep < 0 { 0 } else { (last_sep + 1) as u64 }
    string { ptr: &path.ptr[start], len: path.len - start }
}

fn pathExt(path: string) -> string {
    // Find last '.'
    var last_dot: i64 = -1
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < path.len {
        if path.ptr[i] == '.' {
            last_dot = i as i64
        }
        i += 1
    }
    if last_dot < 0 or last_dot as u64 == path.len - 1 {
        string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
    } else {
        string { ptr: &path.ptr[(last_dot + 1) as u64], len: path.len - (last_dot + 1) as u64 }
    }
}

fn pathStem(path: string) -> string {
    let ext = pathExt(path)
    if ext.len == 0 { return path }
    string { ptr: path.ptr, len: path.len - ext.len - 1 }
}

fn pathIsAbsolute(path: string) -> bool {
    path.len > 0 and path.ptr[0] == '/'
}

fn pathRelative(from: string, to: string, buf: *u8, buf_size: u64) -> u64 {
    // Simple: if to starts with from, strip the prefix
    if to.len >= from.len {
        var match = true
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < from.len {
            if from.ptr[i] != to.ptr[i] { match = false; break }
            i += 1
        }
        if match {
            // Strip prefix
            let rest = string { ptr: &to.ptr[from.len], len: to.len - from.len }
            // Skip leading separator
            var start: u64 = 0
            if rest.len > 0 and rest.ptr[0] == '/' { start = 1 }
            var i2: u64 = start
            var pos: u64 = 0
            while i2 < rest.len and pos < buf_size - 1 {
                buf[pos] = rest.ptr[i2]
                pos += 1
                i2 += 1
            }
            buf[pos] = 0
            return pos
        }
    }
    // Fallback: just copy 'to'
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < to.len and i < buf_size - 1 {
        buf[i] = to.ptr[i]
        i += 1
    }
    buf[i] = 0
    i
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// String Builder (for constructing output)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct StringBuilder {
    buf: *u8
    len: u64
    capacity: u64
    allocator: *Allocator

    fn init(alloc: *Allocator, cap: u64) -> ?StringBuilder {
        let ptr = alloc.allocator_alloc(cap)
        if ptr == null { return null }
        StringBuilder {
            buf: ptr as *u8
            len: 0
            capacity: cap
            allocator: alloc
        }
    }

    fn append(self: *StringBuilder, data: string) -> bool {
        if self.len + data.len >= self.capacity { return false }
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < data.len {
            self.buf[self.len] = data.ptr[i]
            self.len += 1
            i += 1
        }
        true
    }

    fn appendByte(self: *StringBuilder, ch: u8) -> bool {
        if self.len >= self.capacity { return false }
        self.buf[self.len] = ch
        self.len += 1
        true
    }

    fn appendLine(self: *StringBuilder, data: string) -> bool {
        if !self.append(data) { return false }
        self.appendByte('\n')
    }

    fn toString(self: *StringBuilder) -> string {
        string { ptr: self.buf, len: self.len }
    }

    fn reset(self: *StringBuilder) {
        self.len = 0
    }

    fn deinit(self: *StringBuilder) {
        self.allocator.allocator_free(self.buf as *anyopaque, self.capacity)
    }
}

// Lir Standard Library — Minimal JSON Parser
// For parsing project.manifest and configuration files
// Supports: objects, arrays, strings, numbers, booleans, null

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// JSON Value Types
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum JsonType {
    Null
    Bool
    Int
    Float
    String
    Array
    Object
}

// Forward declaration for recursive types
struct JsonValue

struct JsonPair {
    key: string
    value: *JsonValue
}

struct JsonValue {
    kind: JsonType
    bool_val: bool
    int_val: i64
    float_val: f64
    str_val: string
    array_items: []JsonValue
    array_len: u64
    object_pairs: []JsonPair
    object_len: u64
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// JSON Parser
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct JsonParser {
    src: string
    pos: u64
    alloc: *Allocator

    fn init(source: string, allocator: *Allocator) -> JsonParser {
        JsonParser {
            src: source
            pos: 0
            alloc: allocator
        }
    }

    fn peek(self: *JsonParser) -> u8 {
        if self.pos >= self.src.len { 0 } else { self.src.ptr[self.pos] }
    }

    fn advance(self: *JsonParser) -> u8 {
        let ch = self.peek()
        self.pos += 1
        ch
    }

    fn skipWhitespace(self: *JsonParser) {
        while self.pos < self.src.len {
            let ch = self.peek()
            if ch == ' ' or ch == '\t' or ch == '\n' or ch == '\r' {
                self.advance()
            } else {
                break
            }
        }
    }

    fn parseValue(self: *JsonParser) -> ?JsonValue {
        self.skipWhitespace()
        if self.pos >= self.src.len { return null }

        let ch = self.peek()
        if ch == '{' { return self.parseObject() }
        if ch == '[' { return self.parseArray() }
        if ch == '"' { return self.parseString() }
        if ch == 't' or ch == 'f' { return self.parseBool() }
        if ch == 'n' { return self.parseNull() }
        if ch == '-' or (ch >= '0' and ch <= '9') { return self.parseNumber() }

        null
    }

    fn parseObject(self: *JsonParser) -> ?JsonValue {
        self.advance() // skip {
        self.skipWhitespace()

        var pairs = ArrayList(JsonPair).init(self.alloc)
        defer pairs.deinit()

        if self.peek() == '}' {
            self.advance()
            let slice = pairs.items.slice()
            return JsonValue {
                kind: JsonType.Object
                bool_val: false
                int_val: 0
                float_val: 0.0
                str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
                array_items: undefined
                array_len: 0
                object_pairs: slice.ptr
                object_len: slice.len
            }
        }

        while self.pos < self.src.len {
            self.skipWhitespace()

            // Key
            let key_val = self.parseString()
            if key_val == null { return null }
            let key = key_val.?.str_val

            self.skipWhitespace()
            if self.peek() == ':' { self.advance() }

            // Value
            let val = self.parseValue()
            if val == null { return null }

            // We need to allocate the value on heap
            let val_ptr = self.alloc.allocator_alloc(@size_of(JsonValue))
            if val_ptr == null { return null }
            (val_ptr as *JsonValue).* = val.?

            pairs.push(JsonPair { key: key, value: val_ptr as *JsonValue })

            self.skipWhitespace()
            if self.peek() == ',' {
                self.advance()
            } else if self.peek() == '}' {
                self.advance()
                break
            } else {
                return null // parse error
            }
        }

        let slice = pairs.items.slice()
        let duped_pairs = self.alloc.allocator_alloc(slice.len * @size_of(JsonPair))
        if duped_pairs == null { return null }
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < slice.len {
            (duped_pairs as *JsonPair)[i] = slice.ptr[i]
            i += 1
        }

        JsonValue {
            kind: JsonType.Object
            bool_val: false
            int_val: 0
            float_val: 0.0
            str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
            array_items: undefined
            array_len: 0
            object_pairs: duped_pairs as *JsonPair
            object_len: slice.len
        }
    }

    fn parseArray(self: *JsonParser) -> ?JsonValue {
        self.advance() // skip [
        self.skipWhitespace()

        var items = ArrayList(JsonValue).init(self.alloc)
        defer items.deinit()

        if self.peek() == ']' {
            self.advance()
            let slice = items.items.slice()
            return JsonValue {
                kind: JsonType.Array
                bool_val: false
                int_val: 0
                float_val: 0.0
                str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
                array_items: slice.ptr
                array_len: slice.len
                object_pairs: undefined
                object_len: 0
            }
        }

        while self.pos < self.src.len {
            let val = self.parseValue()
            if val == null { return null }
            items.push(val.?)

            self.skipWhitespace()
            if self.peek() == ',' {
                self.advance()
            } else if self.peek() == ']' {
                self.advance()
                break
            } else {
                return null
            }
        }

        let slice = items.items.slice()
        let duped = self.alloc.allocator_alloc(slice.len * @size_of(JsonValue))
        if duped == null { return null }
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < slice.len {
            (duped as *JsonValue)[i] = slice.ptr[i]
            i += 1
        }

        JsonValue {
            kind: JsonType.Array
            bool_val: false
            int_val: 0
            float_val: 0.0
            str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
            array_items: duped as *JsonValue
            array_len: slice.len
            object_pairs: undefined
            object_len: 0
        }
    }

    fn parseString(self: *JsonParser) -> ?JsonValue {
        self.advance() // skip opening "
        let start = self.pos

        while self.pos < self.src.len and self.peek() != '"' {
            if self.peek() == '\\' { self.advance() } // skip escape
            self.advance()
        }

        let len = self.pos - start
        if self.pos < self.src.len { self.advance() } // skip closing "

        // Copy the string content
        let buf = self.alloc.allocator_alloc(len + 1)
        if buf == null { return null }
        var i: u64 = 0
        while i < len {
            (buf as *u8)[i] = self.src.ptr[start + i]
            i += 1
        }
        (buf as *u8)[len] = 0

        JsonValue {
            kind: JsonType.String
            bool_val: false
            int_val: 0
            float_val: 0.0
            str_val: string { ptr: buf as *u8, len: len }
            array_items: undefined
            array_len: 0
            object_pairs: undefined
            object_len: 0
        }
    }

    fn parseNumber(self: *JsonParser) -> ?JsonValue {
        let start = self.pos
        let negative = self.peek() == '-'
        if negative { self.advance() }

        while self.pos < self.src.len and self.peek() >= '0' and self.peek() <= '9' {
            self.advance()
        }

        let is_float = self.peek() == '.'
        if is_float {
            self.advance() // skip .
            while self.pos < self.src.len and self.peek() >= '0' and self.peek() <= '9' {
                self.advance()
            }
        }

        // Parse the number
        let text = string { ptr: &self.src.ptr[start], len: self.pos - start }
        let num_str = if negative { string { ptr: &self.src.ptr[start + 1], len: self.pos - start - 1 } } else { text }

        if is_float {
            JsonValue {
                kind: JsonType.Float
                bool_val: false
                int_val: 0
                float_val: atof(num_str.ptr as *u8)
                str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
                array_items: undefined
                array_len: 0
                object_pairs: undefined
                object_len: 0
            }
        } else {
            let val = atoi(num_str.ptr as *u8)
            JsonValue {
                kind: JsonType.Int
                bool_val: false
                int_val: if negative { -val } else { val }
                float_val: 0.0
                str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
                array_items: undefined
                array_len: 0
                object_pairs: undefined
                object_len: 0
            }
        }
    }

    fn parseBool(self: *JsonParser) -> ?JsonValue {
        if self.peek() == 't' {
            // true
            self.pos += 4
            JsonValue {
                kind: JsonType.Bool
                bool_val: true
                int_val: 0
                float_val: 0.0
                str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
                array_items: undefined
<veriayrimi>
array_len: 0
                object_pairs: undefined
                object_len: 0
            }
        } else {
            // false
            self.pos += 5
            JsonValue {
                kind: JsonType.Bool
                bool_val: false
                int_val: 0
                float_val: 0.0
                str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
                array_items: undefined
                array_len: 0
                object_pairs: undefined
                object_len: 0
            }
        }
    }

    fn parseNull(self: *JsonParser) -> ?JsonValue {
        self.pos += 4 // skip "null"
        JsonValue {
            kind: JsonType.Null
            bool_val: false
            int_val: 0
            float_val: 0.0
            str_val: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
            array_items: undefined
            array_len: 0
            object_pairs: undefined
            object_len: 0
        }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// JSON Value Helpers
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn jsonGet(val: *JsonValue, key: string) -> ?*JsonValue {
    if val.kind != JsonType.Object { return null }
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < val.object_len {
        if val.object_pairs[i].key.len == key.len {
            var match = true
            var j: u64 = 0
            while j < key.len {
                if val.object_pairs[i].key.ptr[j] != key.ptr[j] { match = false; break }
                j += 1
            }
            if match { return val.object_pairs[i].value }
        }
        i += 1
    }
    null
}

fn jsonGetString(val: *JsonValue, key: string) -> string {
    let found = jsonGet(val, key)
    if found == null or found.?.kind != JsonType.String {
        string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
    } else {
        found.?.str_val
    }
}

fn jsonGetInt(val: *JsonValue, key: string) -> i64 {
    let found = jsonGet(val, key)
    if found == null or found.?.kind != JsonType.Int { 0 } else { found.?.int_val }
}

fn jsonGetBool(val: *JsonValue, key: string) -> bool {
    let found = jsonGet(val, key)
    if found == null or found.?.kind != JsonType.Bool { false } else { found.?.bool_val }
}

fn jsonGetArray(val: *JsonValue, key: string) -> ?*JsonValue {
    let found = jsonGet(val, key)
    if found == null or found.?.kind != JsonType.Array { null } else { found }
}

fn jsonParse(source: string, alloc: *Allocator) -> ?JsonValue {
    var parser = JsonParser.init(source, alloc)
    parser.parseValue()
}

// Lir Standard Library — OS Interface
// Environment variables, process execution, platform detection

import core

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Environment Variables
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

fn getEnv(name: string) -> ?string {
    // Linux: read /proc/self/environ
    let file = File.open("/proc/self/environ", FileMode.Read)
    if file.isErr() { return null }
    let f = file.unwrap()

    let buf_size: u64 = 65536
    let buf = alloc.allocator_alloc(buf_size)
    if buf == null { f.close(); return null }

    let n = f.read(buf as *u8, buf_size)
    f.close()

    if n <= 0 { alloc.allocator_free(buf, buf_size); return null }

    // Search for NAME=VALUE in environ
    var i: u64 = 0
    while i < n as u64 {
        // Check if this entry starts with our name
        var match = true
        var j: u64 = 0
        while j < name.len and i + j < n as u64 {
            if (buf as *u8)[i + j] != name.ptr[j] { match = false; break }
            j += 1
        }

        if match and j == name.len and (buf as *u8)[i + name.len] == '=' {
            // Found it — extract value after '='
            let val_start = i + name.len + 1
            var val_end = val_start
            while val_end < n as u64 and (buf as *u8)[val_end] != 0 {
                val_end += 1
            }
            let result = string { ptr: &((buf as *u8)[val_start]), len: val_end - val_start }
            alloc.allocator_free(buf, buf_size)
            return result
        }

        // Skip to next entry (null-terminated)
        while i < n as u64 and (buf as *u8)[i] != 0 { i += 1 }
        i += 1 // skip null terminator
    }

    alloc.allocator_free(buf, buf_size)
    null
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Process Execution
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

struct ProcessResult {
    exit_code: i32
    stdout: string
    stderr: string
}

fn execCommand(cmd: string) -> ProcessResult {
    // Use fork + exec via syscalls
    // For now, simplified: write command to a temp file and use system()
    ProcessResult {
        exit_code: -1
        stdout: string { ptr: "", len: 0 }
        stderr: string { ptr: "exec not implemented", len: 20 }
    }
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Platform Detection
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum Platform {
    Linux
    MacOS
    Windows
    Unknown
}

enum Arch {
    X86_64
    AArch64
    Riscv64
    Wasm32
    Unknown
}

struct PlatformInfo {
    os: Platform
    arch: Arch
    name: string
}

fn detectPlatform() -> PlatformInfo {
    // Try reading /proc/version for Linux detection
    let file = File.open("/proc/version", FileMode.Read)
    if file.isOk() {
        let f = file.unwrap()
        f.close()
        return PlatformInfo {
            os: Platform.Linux
            arch: Arch.X86_64  // assume x86_64 for now
            name: "linux"
        }
    }

    PlatformInfo {
        os: Platform.Unknown
        arch: Arch.Unknown
        name: "unknown"
    }
}

fn platformName(p: Platform) -> string {
    match p {
        Platform.Linux => "linux"
        Platform.MacOS => "macos"
        Platform.Windows => "windows"
        Platform.Unknown => "unknown"
    }
}

fn archName(a: Arch) -> string {
    match a {
        Arch.X86_64 => "x86_64"
        Arch.AArch64 => "aarch64"
        Arch.Riscv64 => "riscv64"
        Arch.Wasm32 => "wasm32"
        Arch.Unknown => "unknown"
    }
}
