// LEGACY / ISOLATED (NOEMA v3 Phase 0): exemplar-retrieval pretrain prototype.
// This file is NOT NOEMA v3 weight-generation. New work belongs under ai/src/noema_v3/.
// Do not report this path as noema_v3_weight_generation; output_mode must remain
// legacy_retrieval_exemplar when served through legacy backends.
//
// NOEMA-Phi pretraining and inference prototype, LIR-only model execution.
// Raw UTF-8 byte streams; no tokenizer, fixed vocabulary, attention/softmax,
// MLP, backprop tape, or external optimizer. The online core is bounded field
// evolution over four sparse cavities and eight phase streams.
import "noema_phi_atlas.lir"
import "noema_phi_etd.lir"
const NP_C_MIN: i64 = 64
const NP_MAX_STATE_FLOATS: i64 = 1152921504606846975
const NP_K: i64 = 4
const NP_R: i64 = 4
const NP_B: i64 = 8
const NP_SIG: i64 = 8
const NP_MODES: i64 = 5
const NP_DO_EVERY: i64 = 8
const NP_KONS: i64 = 16
const NP_RING: i64 = 256
const NP_DT: f64 = 0.15
const NP_DT_COARSE: f64 = 0.30
const NP_SIG_MIN: f64 = 0.0-2.5
const NP_SIG_MAX: f64 = 2.5
const NP_U_MAX: f64 = 3.0
const NP_B_MAX: f64 = 0.85
const NP_ALPHA_S: f64 = 0.001
const NP_ALPHA_U: f64 = 0.0005
const NP_ALPHA_B: f64 = 0.0008
const NP_MARGIN: f64 = 1.25
const NP_M: f64 = 1.5
const NP_FLOOR: f64 = 0.000000000001
const NP_THETA_SNR: f64 = 0.0001
const NP_MEM_SLOTS: i64 = 256
const NP_MEM_BYTES: i64 = 256
const NP_MEM_FEATURES: i64 = 512
const NP_MEM_MIN_SCORE: f64 = 0.22
const NP_META: i64 = 32
const NP_C: i64 = 0
const NP_D: i64 = 1
const NP_N: i64 = 2
const NP_DOCS: i64 = 3
const NP_STAGE: i64 = 4
const NP_PRNG: i64 = 5
const NP_DO: i64 = 6
const NP_GSUM: i64 = 7
const NP_GN: i64 = 8
const NP_KCOUNT: i64 = 9
const NP_FBAR: i64 = 10
const NP_EARLY: i64 = 11
const NP_TRAIN_BYTES: i64 = 12
const NP_DOMAIN: i64 = 13
const NP_LAST_F: i64 = 14
const NP_LAST_GATE: i64 = 15
const NP_LAST_INPUT: i64 = 16
const NP_LAST_OUTPUT: i64 = 17
const NP_RINGHEAD: i64 = 18
const NP_RINGCOUNT: i64 = 19
const NP_RESET_COUNT: i64 = 20
const NP_LEARN_COUNT: i64 = 21
const NP_STAGE_DOCS: i64 = 22
const NP_PLATEAU: i64 = 23
const NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES: i64 = 24
const NP_MEM_COUNT: i64 = 25
const NP_MEM_NEXT: i64 = 26
const NP_LAST_MATCH: i64 = 27
const NP_MEM_CAP: i64 = 28
const NP_AUTO_LAST_MACROS: i64 = 29
const NP_AUTO_MACRO_SUM: i64 = 30
const NP_AUTO_MACRO_COUNT: i64 = 31
const NP_ETA0: f64 = 0.2
const NP_LAMBDA0: f64 = 0.001
const NP_PI2: f64 = 6.283185307179586

fn np_c(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_C] as i64 }
fn np_d(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_D] as i64 }
fn np_n(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_N] as i64 }
fn np_sig_off() -> i64 { return NP_META }
fn np_sigma_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return NP_META+np_c(st)*NP_SIG }
fn np_bias_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_sigma_off(st)+np_c(st) }
fn np_u_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_bias_off(st)+np_c(st)*np_d(st) }
fn np_field_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_u_off(st)+np_c(st)*np_d(st)*NP_R }
fn np_field_len(st: *f64) -> i64 { return NP_K*NP_B*np_n(st) }
fn np_array(st: *f64, a: i64) -> i64 { return np_field_off(st)+a*np_field_len(st) }
fn np_orta(st: *f64, i: i64) -> i64 { return np_field_off(st)+8*np_field_len(st)+i }
fn np_ring(st: *f64, i: i64, j: i64) -> i64 { return np_field_off(st)+8*np_field_len(st)+2*np_n(st)+i*4+j }
fn np_scratch_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_field_off(st)+8*np_field_len(st)+2*np_n(st)+NP_RING*4 }
fn np_mem_capacity(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_MEM_CAP] as i64 }
fn np_mem_len_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_scratch_off(st)+64 }
fn np_mem_feat_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_mem_len_off(st)+np_mem_capacity(st) }
fn np_mem_bytes_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return (np_mem_feat_off(st)+np_mem_capacity(st)*NP_MEM_FEATURES)*8 }
fn np_mem_query_off(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_mem_bytes_off(st)+np_mem_capacity(st)*NP_MEM_BYTES }
fn np_mem_bytes_ptr(st: *f64,slot: i64) -> *u8 { return @ptr_add(st as *u8,np_mem_bytes_off(st)+slot*NP_MEM_BYTES) }
fn np_at(slot: i64, stream: i64, cell: i64, n: i64) -> i64 { return (slot*NP_B+stream)*n+cell }
fn np_sig(_st: *f64, card: i64, j: i64) -> i64 { return np_sig_off()+card*NP_SIG+j }
fn np_sigma(st: *f64, card: i64) -> i64 { return np_sigma_off(st)+card }
fn np_bias(st: *f64, card: i64, j: i64) -> i64 { return np_bias_off(st)+card*np_d(st)+j }
fn np_metric(st: *f64, card: i64, r: i64, j: i64) -> i64 { return np_u_off(st)+(card*NP_R+r)*np_d(st)+j }
fn np_size_add(a:i64,b:i64) -> i64 {
    if a<0 or b<0 or a>NP_MAX_STATE_FLOATS-b { return -1 }
    return a+b
}
fn np_size_mul(a:i64,b:i64) -> i64 {
    if a<0 or b<0 { return -1 }
    if a==0 or b==0 { return 0 }
    if a>NP_MAX_STATE_FLOATS/b { return -1 }
    return a*b
}
fn np_state_floats_legacy(cards: i64,dim_d: i64,dim_n: i64) -> i64 {
    if cards<=0 or dim_d<=0 or dim_n<=0 { return -1 }
    let card_dim=np_size_mul(cards,dim_d)
    if card_dim<0 { return -1 }
    let card_rank=np_size_mul(card_dim,NP_R)
    let field=np_size_mul(NP_K*NP_B*8,dim_n)
    let twice_dim=np_size_mul(2,dim_n)
    if card_rank<0 or field<0 or twice_dim<0 { return -1 }
    var total=NP_META
    total=np_size_add(total,np_size_mul(cards,NP_SIG))
    total=np_size_add(total,cards)
    total=np_size_add(total,card_dim)
    total=np_size_add(total,card_rank)
    total=np_size_add(total,field)
    total=np_size_add(total,twice_dim)
    total=np_size_add(total,NP_RING*4+64)
    return total
}
fn np_state_floats_cap(cards: i64,dim_d: i64,dim_n: i64,memory_slots: i64) -> i64 {
    if memory_slots<=0 { return -1 }
    var total=np_state_floats_legacy(cards,dim_d,dim_n)
    if total<0 { return -1 }
    total=np_size_add(total,memory_slots)
    total=np_size_add(total,np_size_mul(memory_slots,NP_MEM_FEATURES))
    total=np_size_add(total,np_size_mul(memory_slots,NP_MEM_BYTES/8))
    total=np_size_add(total,NP_MEM_FEATURES)
    return total
}
fn np_state_floats(cards: i64,dim_d: i64,dim_n: i64) -> i64 { return np_state_floats_cap(cards,dim_d,dim_n,NP_MEM_SLOTS) }

fn np_rand(st: *f64) -> f64 {
    let x = @wrapping_add(@wrapping_mul(st[NP_PRNG] as i64,1103515245),12345) & 2147483647
    st[NP_PRNG] = x as f64
    return x as f64/2147483648.0
}
fn np_finite(x: f64) -> i64 {
    if x!=x { return 0 }
    if @fabs(x)>1.0e300 { return 0 }
    return 1
}
fn np_exp(x: f64) -> f64 {
    let y = @clamp(x,0.0-30.0,30.0)
    let k = @floor(y/0.6931471805599453) as i64
    let r = y-k as f64*0.6931471805599453
    let r2=r*r
    let r3=r2*r
    let r4=r3*r
    return (1.0+r+r2/2.0+r3/6.0+r4/24.0)*np_pow2(k)
}
fn np_pow2(k: i64) -> f64 {
    var v: f64 = 1.0
    var i: i64 = 0
    if k >= 0 { while i < k { v=v*2.0 i=i+1 } }
    else { while i < 0-k { v=v*0.5 i=i+1 } }
    return v
}
fn np_phase_real(q: i64) -> f64 { if q==0 { return 1.0 } if q==2 { return 0.0-1.0 } return 0.0 }
fn np_phase_imag(q: i64) -> f64 { if q==1 { return 1.0 } if q==3 { return 0.0-1.0 } return 0.0 }
fn np_phase_quadrant(x: f64,y: f64) -> i64 {
    if x>=0.0 and y>=0.0 { return if x>=y { 0 } else { 1 } }
    if x<0.0 and y>=0.0 { return if y>=0.0-x { 1 } else { 2 } }
    if x<0.0 and y<0.0 { return if 0.0-x>=0.0-y { 2 } else { 3 } }
    return if 0.0-y>=x { 3 } else { 0 }
}

fn noema_pretrain_create(cards: i64,dim_d: i64,dim_n: i64,seed: i64) -> *f64 {
    return noema_pretrain_create_with_memory(cards,dim_d,dim_n,seed,NP_MEM_SLOTS)
}
fn noema_pretrain_create_with_memory(cards: i64,dim_d: i64,dim_n: i64,seed: i64,memory_slots: i64) -> *f64 {
    if cards<NP_C_MIN or dim_d<2 or dim_n<8 or memory_slots<=0 { exit(2) }
    let count=np_state_floats_cap(cards,dim_d,dim_n,memory_slots)
    if count<=0 or count>NP_MAX_STATE_FLOATS { exit(2) }
    let st=@malloc(count*8) as *f64
    if st==0 as *f64 { exit(2) }
    var i: i64=0
    while i<count { st[i]=0.0 i=i+1 }
    st[NP_D]=dim_d as f64
    st[NP_N]=dim_n as f64
    st[NP_MEM_CAP]=memory_slots as f64
    st[NP_PRNG]=(@fabs(seed as f64)%2147483647.0)+1.0
    i=0
    while i<cards {
        st[np_sigma(st,i)]=0.0
        var j: i64=0
        while j<NP_SIG { st[np_sig(st,i,j)]=(np_rand(st)*2.0-1.0)*0.2 j=j+1 }
        j=0
        while j<dim_d {
            st[np_bias(st,i,j)]=0.0
            var r: i64=0
            while r<NP_R { st[np_metric(st,i,r,j)]=(np_rand(st)*2.0-1.0)*0.05 r=r+1 }
            j=j+1
        }
        i=i+1
    }
    st[NP_C]=cards as f64
    return st
}
fn noema_pretrain_expand_memory(st: *f64,new_capacity: i64) -> *f64 {
    let old_capacity=np_mem_capacity(st)
    if new_capacity<=old_capacity { return st }
    let expanded=noema_pretrain_create_with_memory(np_c(st),np_d(st),np_n(st),1,new_capacity)
    if expanded==0 as *f64 { return 0 as *f64 }
    let legacy=np_state_floats_legacy(np_c(st),np_d(st),np_n(st))
    var i:i64=0
    while i<legacy { expanded[i]=st[i] i=i+1 }
    expanded[NP_MEM_CAP]=new_capacity as f64
    let count=st[NP_MEM_COUNT] as i64
    expanded[NP_MEM_COUNT]=st[NP_MEM_COUNT]
    expanded[NP_MEM_NEXT]=st[NP_MEM_NEXT]
    var slot:i64=0
    while slot<count {
        expanded[np_mem_len_off(expanded)+slot]=st[np_mem_len_off(st)+slot]
        i=0
        while i<NP_MEM_FEATURES {
            expanded[np_mem_feat_off(expanded)+slot*NP_MEM_FEATURES+i]=st[np_mem_feat_off(st)+slot*NP_MEM_FEATURES+i]
            i=i+1
        }
        let src=np_mem_bytes_ptr(st,slot)
        let dst=np_mem_bytes_ptr(expanded,slot)
        i=0
        while i<NP_MEM_BYTES { @write_u8(@ptr_add(dst,i),@read_u8(@ptr_add(src,i))) i=i+1 }
        slot=slot+1
    }
    noema_pretrain_destroy(st)
    return expanded
}
fn noema_pretrain_destroy(st: *f64) { @free(st as *u8) }

// reset kind 0 clears cross-document context; kind 1 retains the context ring.
fn noema_pretrain_reset(st: *f64, kind: i64) {
    var i: i64=0
    while i<8*np_field_len(st) { st[np_field_off(st)+i]=0.0 i=i+1 }
    if kind==0 {
        i=0
        while i<2*np_n(st) { st[np_orta(st,0)+i]=0.0 i=i+1 }
        i=0
        while i<NP_RING*4 { st[np_ring(st,i/4,i%4)]=0.0 i=i+1 }
        st[NP_RINGHEAD]=0.0 st[NP_RINGCOUNT]=0.0
<veriayrimi>
st[NP_RESET_COUNT]=st[NP_RESET_COUNT]+1.0
    }
}

// Four damped sinusoidal projections; large records are sampled evenly so
// preprocessing cost is bounded instead of scaling trig calls with file size.
const NP_SIG_MAX_SAMPLES: i64 = 8192
fn np_signature(bytes: *u8,length: i64,out: *f64) {
    var k: i64=0
    while k<NP_SIG { out[k]=0.0 k=k+1 }
    if length<=0 { return }
    let samples=if length<NP_SIG_MAX_SAMPLES { length } else { NP_SIG_MAX_SAMPLES }
    let scale=1.0/@sqrt(samples as f64)
    // Harmonic oscillator recurrence computes the same sampled Fourier
    // projections without per-byte trigonometric calls or a token table.
    k=0
    while k<4 {
        let w=NP_PI2*(k+1) as f64/(samples+1) as f64
        let cw=@cos(w)
        let sw=@sin(w)
        var phase_re:f64=1.0
        var phase_im:f64=0.0
        var sum_re:f64=0.0
        var sum_im:f64=0.0
        var i: i64=0
        while i<samples {
            let source=(i*length)/samples
            let x=(@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,source)) as i64) as f64/255.0
            sum_re=sum_re+x*phase_re
            sum_im=sum_im+x*phase_im
            let next_re=phase_re*cw-phase_im*sw
            let next_im=phase_re*sw+phase_im*cw
            phase_re=next_re phase_im=next_im
            i=i+1
        }
        out[k*2]=sum_re*scale
        out[k*2+1]=sum_im*scale
        k=k+1
    }
}

// Hash raw UTF-8 byte 5-grams into a normalized resonance signature.
// This is a fixed internal-memory index, not an external embedding model.
fn np_text_features(bytes: *u8,length: i64,out: *f64) {
    var k:i64=0
    while k<NP_MEM_FEATURES { out[k]=0.0 k=k+1 }
    var i:i64=0
    while i+4<length {
        var hash:i64=0
        var j:i64=0
        while j<5 { hash=(hash*257+@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,i+j)) as i64+1)&2147483647 j=j+1 }
        let bucket=(hash%NP_MEM_FEATURES) as i64
        out[bucket]=out[bucket]+1.0
        i=i+1
    }
    var norm:f64=0.0
    k=0
    while k<NP_MEM_FEATURES { norm=norm+out[k]*out[k] k=k+1 }
    if norm>0.0 {
        norm=@sqrt(norm)
        k=0
        while k<NP_MEM_FEATURES { out[k]=out[k]/norm k=k+1 }
    }
}

fn np_memory_has_eligible_line(bytes: *u8,length: i64) -> i64 {
    var pos:i64=0
    while pos<length {
        let line_start=pos
        while pos<length and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,pos)) as i64)!=10 { pos=pos+1 }
        var start=line_start
        var end=pos
        if end>start and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,end-1)) as i64)==13 { end=end-1 }
        while start<end {
            let c=@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,start)) as i64
            if c==32 or c==9 { start=start+1 } else { break }
        }
        while end>start {
            let c=@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,end-1)) as i64
            if c==32 or c==9 { end=end-1 } else { break }
        }
        if end>start and end-start<=NP_MEM_BYTES { return 1 }
        if pos<length and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,pos)) as i64)==10 { pos=pos+1 }
    }
    return 0
}

// Store the first whole, short source line per successful training record.
// This deterministic anchor keeps training examples reproducible and lets the
// generator emit an exact per-record validation fixture.
fn np_memory_add(st: *f64,bytes: *u8,length: i64) {
    if length<=0 or st[NP_MEM_COUNT]>=np_mem_capacity(st) as f64 { return }
    var pos:i64=0
    var found:i64=0
    while pos<length and found==0 {
        let line_start=pos
        while pos<length and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,pos)) as i64)!=10 { pos=pos+1 }
        var line_end=pos
        if line_end>line_start and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,line_end-1)) as i64)==13 { line_end=line_end-1 }
        var start=line_start
        var end=line_end
        while start<end {
            let c=@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,start)) as i64
            if c==32 or c==9 { start=start+1 } else { break }
        }
        while end>start {
            let c=@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,end-1)) as i64
            if c==32 or c==9 { end=end-1 } else { break }
        }
        if end>start and end-start<=NP_MEM_BYTES {
            var slot=st[NP_MEM_NEXT] as i64
            if st[NP_MEM_COUNT]<np_mem_capacity(st) as f64 {
                slot=st[NP_MEM_COUNT] as i64
                st[NP_MEM_COUNT]=st[NP_MEM_COUNT]+1.0
            }
            st[np_mem_len_off(st)+slot]= (end-start) as f64
            let feat=@ptr_add(st as *u8,(np_mem_feat_off(st)+slot*NP_MEM_FEATURES)*8) as *f64
            np_text_features(@ptr_add(bytes,start),end-start,feat)
            let dst=np_mem_bytes_ptr(st,slot)
            var j:i64=0
            while j<NP_MEM_BYTES { @write_u8(@ptr_add(dst,j),0) j=j+1 }
            j=0
            while j<end-start { @write_u8(@ptr_add(dst,j),@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,start+j))) j=j+1 }
            if st[NP_MEM_COUNT]>=np_mem_capacity(st) as f64 { st[NP_MEM_NEXT]=((slot+1)%np_mem_capacity(st)) as f64 }
            found=1
        }
        if pos<length and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,pos)) as i64)==10 { pos=pos+1 }
    }
}

fn np_memory_word_byte(value:i64) -> i64 {
    if (value>=48 and value<=57) or (value>=65 and value<=90) or (value>=97 and value<=122) or value>=128 { return 1 }
    return 0
}
fn np_memory_contains(source: *u8,source_len: i64,query: *u8,query_len: i64) -> i64 {
    if query_len<8 or query_len>source_len { return 0 }
    let first=@read_u8(query) as i64
    var at:i64=0
    while at+query_len<=source_len {
        if (@read_u8(@ptr_add(source,at)) as i64)==first {
            var j:i64=1
            while j<query_len and @read_u8(@ptr_add(source,at+j))==@read_u8(@ptr_add(query,j)) { j=j+1 }
            if j==query_len {
                let left_ok=if at==0 { 1 } else { 1-np_memory_word_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(source,at-1)) as i64) }
                let end=at+query_len
                let right_ok=if end>=source_len { 1 } else { 1-np_memory_word_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(source,end)) as i64) }
                if left_ok==1 and right_ok==1 { return 1 }
            }
        }
        at=at+1
    }
    return 0
}
fn np_memory_exact_slot(st: *f64,prompt: *u8,prompt_len: i64) -> i64 {
    var slot:i64=0
    while slot<st[NP_MEM_COUNT] as i64 {
        let length=st[np_mem_len_off(st)+slot] as i64
        if np_memory_contains(np_mem_bytes_ptr(st,slot),length,prompt,prompt_len)==1 { return slot }
        slot=slot+1
    }
    return -1
}
fn np_memory_lower_byte(value:i64) -> i64 { return if value>=65 and value<=90 { value+32 } else { value } }
fn np_memory_contains_token(source: *u8,source_len: i64,query: *u8,query_len: i64) -> i64 {
    if query_len<4 or query_len>source_len { return 0 }
    let first=np_memory_lower_byte(@read_u8(query) as i64)
    var at:i64=0
    while at+query_len<=source_len {
        if np_memory_lower_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(source,at)) as i64)==first {
            var j:i64=1
            while j<query_len and np_memory_lower_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(source,at+j)) as i64)==np_memory_lower_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(query,j)) as i64) { j=j+1 }
            if j==query_len {
                let left_ok=if at==0 { 1 } else { 1-np_memory_word_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(source,at-1)) as i64) }
                let end=at+query_len
                let right_ok=if end>=source_len { 1 } else { 1-np_memory_word_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(source,end)) as i64) }
                if left_ok==1 and right_ok==1 { return 1 }
            }
        }
        at=at+1
    }
    return 0
}
fn np_memory_token_overlap(source: *u8,source_len: i64,query: *u8,query_len: i64) -> i64 {
    var at:i64=0
    while at<query_len {
        while at<query_len and np_memory_word_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(query,at)) as i64)==0 { at=at+1 }
        let start=at
        while at<query_len and np_memory_word_byte(@read_u8(@ptr_add(query,at)) as i64)==1 { at=at+1 }
        let token_len=at-start
        if token_len>=4 and np_memory_contains_token(source,source_len,@ptr_add(query,start),token_len)==1 { return 1 }
    }
    return 0
}
fn np_memory_retrieve(st: *f64,prompt: *u8,prompt_len: i64,output: *u8,capacity: i64) -> i64 {
    st[NP_LAST_MATCH]=0.0
    if prompt_len<5 or capacity<=0 or st[NP_MEM_COUNT]<=0.0 { return 0 }
    let exact_slot=np_memory_exact_slot(st,prompt,prompt_len)
    if exact_slot>=0 {
        var exact_count=st[np_mem_len_off(st)+exact_slot] as i64
        if exact_count>capacity { exact_count=capacity }
        st[NP_LAST_MATCH]=1.0
        let exact_src=np_mem_bytes_ptr(st,exact_slot)
        var exact_i:i64=0
        while exact_i<exact_count { @write_u8(@ptr_add(output,exact_i),@read_u8(@ptr_add(exact_src,exact_i))) exact_i=exact_i+1 }
        return exact_count
    }
    let query=@ptr_add(st as *u8,np_mem_query_off(st)) as *f64
    np_text_features(prompt,prompt_len,query)
    var best_score=0.0-1.0
    var best_slot:i64=0
    var slot:i64=0
    while slot<st[NP_MEM_COUNT] as i64 {
        let text_len=st[np_mem_len_off(st)+slot] as i64
        if np_memory_token_overlap(np_mem_bytes_ptr(st,slot),text_len,prompt,prompt_len)==1 {
            let feat=@ptr_add(st as *u8,(np_mem_feat_off(st)+slot*NP_MEM_FEATURES)*8) as *f64
            var score:f64=0.0
            var j:i64=0
            while j<NP_MEM_FEATURES { score=score+query[j]*feat[j] j=j+1 }
            if score>best_score { best_score=score best_slot=slot }
        }
        slot=slot+1
    }
    st[NP_LAST_MATCH]=if best_score<0.0 { 0.0 } else { best_score }
    if best_score<NP_MEM_MIN_SCORE { return 0 }
    var count=st[np_mem_len_off(st)+best_slot] as i64
    if count>capacity { count=capacity }
    let src=np_mem_bytes_ptr(st,best_slot)
    var i:i64=0
    while i<count { @write_u8(@ptr_add(output,i),@read_u8(@ptr_add(src,i))) i=i+1 }
    return count
}

fn noema_pretrain_memory_count(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_MEM_COUNT] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_memory_capacity(st: *f64) -> i64 { return np_mem_capacity(st) }
fn noema_pretrain_last_match(st: *f64) -> f64 { return st[NP_LAST_MATCH] }

// Select exactly four sparse cards through the shared hierarchical resonance
<veriayrimi>
// atlas. Temporary bounds are built from the persisted card signatures; the
// exact branch-and-bound selector is deterministic with a direct-scan fallback.
fn np_select(st: *f64,sig: *f64,ids: *f64) {
    let cards=np_c(st)
    var base:i64=1
    while base<cards { base=base*2 }
    let signatures=@malloc(cards*NP_SIG*8) as *f64
    let bounds=@malloc(base*2*8) as *f64
    let meta=@malloc(4*8) as *f64
    let scores=@malloc(NP_K*8) as *f64
    let stack=@malloc(64*8) as *f64
    var card:i64=0
    while card<cards {
        var j:i64=0
        while j<NP_SIG { signatures[card*NP_SIG+j]=st[np_sig(st,card,j)] j=j+1 }
        card=card+1
    }
    meta[0]=cards as f64 meta[1]=NP_SIG as f64 meta[2]=base as f64 meta[3]=NP_K as f64
    let built=noema_phi_atlas_build_bounds(signatures,cards,NP_SIG,base,bounds)
    var selected:i64=0
    if built==1 { selected=noema_phi_atlas_select(sig,signatures,bounds,meta,ids,scores,stack) }
    if selected!=NP_K {
        var pick:i64=0
        while pick<NP_K {
            var best=-1.0e300
            var best_card:i64=0
            card=0
            while card<cards {
                var used:i64=0
                var t:i64=0
                while t<pick { if ids[t] as i64==card { used=1 } t=t+1 }
                if used==0 {
                    var score:f64=0.0
                    var j2:i64=0
                    while j2<NP_SIG { score=score+sig[j2]*st[np_sig(st,card,j2)] j2=j2+1 }
                    score=score*score
                    if score>best { best=score best_card=card }
                }
                card=card+1
            }
            ids[pick]=best_card as f64
            pick=pick+1
        }
    }
    @free(signatures as *u8) @free(bounds as *u8) @free(meta as *u8)
    @free(scores as *u8) @free(stack as *u8)
}

fn np_inject(st: *f64,bytes: *u8,length: i64,ids: *f64,sig: *f64,do_mode: i64,learn: i64) {
    let n=np_n(st)
    var slot: i64=0
    while slot<NP_K {
        let card=ids[slot] as i64
        var stream: i64=0
        while stream<NP_B {
            var i: i64=0
            while i<n {
                var source: i64=0
                var b: i64=0
                if length>0 { source=(slot*31+stream*17+i*7)%length b=@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,source)) as i64 }
                let feature=sig[(i+stream+slot)%NP_SIG]
                var chart: f64=0.0
                var d: i64=0
                while d<np_d(st) {
                    chart=chart+st[np_bias(st,card,d)]*sig[d%NP_SIG]
                    var r: i64=0
                    while r<NP_R { chart=chart+st[np_metric(st,card,r,d)]*sig[(d+r)%NP_SIG]/NP_R as f64 r=r+1 }
                    d=d+1
                }
                let energy=(feature/@sqrt(length as f64+1.0)+chart)*np_exp(0.0-2.0*st[np_sigma(st,card)])
                let q=(b>>((stream%4)*2))&3
                let amp=0.35+b as f64/255.0*0.25
                let at=np_at(slot,stream,i,n)
                let x=energy+amp*np_phase_real(q)+0.05*st[np_orta(st,i)]
                let y=amp*np_phase_imag(q)+0.05*st[np_orta(st,n+i)]
                st[np_array(st,0)+at]=x st[np_array(st,1)+at]=y
                st[np_array(st,2)+at]=0.0 st[np_array(st,3)+at]=0.0
                // A deterministic one-in-eight-document counterfactual row
                // reverses the encoded phase variable in the alternate branch.
                let qcf=if do_mode==1 { (q+2)%4 } else { q }
                st[np_array(st,4)+at]=energy+amp*np_phase_real(qcf)+0.05*st[np_orta(st,i)]
                st[np_array(st,5)+at]=amp*np_phase_imag(qcf)+0.05*st[np_orta(st,n+i)]
                st[np_array(st,6)+at]=0.0 st[np_array(st,7)+at]=0.0
                i=i+1
            }
            stream=stream+1
        }
        if learn==1 { st[np_sigma(st,card)]=@clamp(st[np_sigma(st,card)],NP_SIG_MIN,NP_SIG_MAX) }
        slot=slot+1
    }
}

fn np_energy(st: *f64,branch: i64) -> f64 {
    let n=np_n(st)
    var e: f64=0.0
    var slot: i64=0
    while slot<NP_K {
        var stream: i64=0
        while stream<NP_B {
            var i: i64=0
            while i<n {
                let at=np_at(slot,stream,i,n)
                let x=st[np_array(st,branch)+at]
                let y=st[np_array(st,branch+1)+at]
                let p=st[np_array(st,branch+2)+at]
                let q=st[np_array(st,branch+3)+at]
                let mag=x*x+y*y
                let omega=1.0+0.25*i as f64/n as f64
                e=e+0.5*(p*p+q*q)+0.5*omega*mag+0.125*(mag-1.0)*(mag-1.0)
                i=i+1
            }
            stream=stream+1
        }
        slot=slot+1
    }
    return e
}

// Nonlinear half-kick / analytic linear ETD / nonlinear half-kick.
// The 25/50/75/100% spectral curriculum remains the active-mode cutoff.
// For this prototype, tau2=.5 and gamma=.06 reproduce the legacy linear
// damping rate .12; the radial quartic force is derived from np_energy.
fn np_step(st: *f64,branch: i64,dt: f64,stage: i64) {
    let n=np_n(st)
    let cutoff=((stage+1)*n)/4
    let fields=@malloc(4*8) as *f64
    let cfg=@malloc(4*8) as *f64
    cfg[0]=0.5 cfg[1]=0.06 cfg[3]=dt
    var slot:i64=0
    while slot<NP_K {
        var stream:i64=0
        while stream<NP_B {
            var i:i64=0
            while i<cutoff {
                let at=np_at(slot,stream,i,n)
                fields[0]=st[np_array(st,branch)+at]
                fields[1]=st[np_array(st,branch+1)+at]
                fields[2]=st[np_array(st,branch+2)+at]
                fields[3]=st[np_array(st,branch+3)+at]
                let mag=fields[0]*fields[0]+fields[1]*fields[1]
                fields[2]=fields[2]-0.25*dt*(mag-1.0)*fields[0]
                fields[3]=fields[3]-0.25*dt*(mag-1.0)*fields[1]
                cfg[2]=1.0+0.25*i as f64/n as f64
                if noema_phi_etd_step(fields,cfg)==1 {
                    let mag2=fields[0]*fields[0]+fields[1]*fields[1]
                    fields[2]=fields[2]-0.25*dt*(mag2-1.0)*fields[0]
                    fields[3]=fields[3]-0.25*dt*(mag2-1.0)*fields[1]
                    st[np_array(st,branch)+at]=fields[0]
                    st[np_array(st,branch+1)+at]=fields[1]
                    st[np_array(st,branch+2)+at]=fields[2]
                    st[np_array(st,branch+3)+at]=fields[3]
                }
                i=i+1
            }
            stream=stream+1
        }
        slot=slot+1
    }
    @free(fields as *u8) @free(cfg as *u8)
}

fn np_gate(delta: f64,ref: f64) -> f64 { return @clamp((0.0-delta-0.001)/(ref+NP_FLOOR),0.0,1.0) }
fn np_commit_document(st: *f64,length: i64,before: f64,after: f64,gate: f64,do_mode: i64) {
    st[NP_DOCS]=st[NP_DOCS]+1.0
    st[NP_TRAIN_BYTES]=st[NP_TRAIN_BYTES]+length as f64
    st[NP_FBAR]=after
    st[NP_GSUM]=st[NP_GSUM]+gate
    st[NP_GN]=st[NP_GN]+1.0
    if do_mode==1 { st[NP_DO]=st[NP_DO]+1.0 }
    st[NP_STAGE_DOCS]=st[NP_STAGE_DOCS]+1.0
    let relative_change=@fabs(after-before)/(@fabs(before)+NP_FLOOR)
    if relative_change<0.005 { st[NP_PLATEAU]=st[NP_PLATEAU]+1.0 } else { st[NP_PLATEAU]=0.0 }
    // Unlock the next 25% of spectral modes only after eight plateau records.
    if st[NP_STAGE]<3.0 and st[NP_STAGE_DOCS]>=8.0 and st[NP_PLATEAU]>=8.0 {
        st[NP_STAGE]=st[NP_STAGE]+1.0
        st[NP_STAGE_DOCS]=0.0
        st[NP_PLATEAU]=0.0
    }
}
/// Deterministic resource planner for automatic corpus training. The spectral
/// stage sets the base budget; long records and recent useful learning add work,
/// while recent plateau/early-exit evidence removes it. The cap is caller-owned.
fn np_auto_macros(st: *f64,length: i64,cap: i64) -> i64 {
    if length<=0 or cap<=0 or cap>16 or st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]>0.0 { return 0 }
    var budget=2+st[NP_STAGE] as i64
    if length>65536 { budget=budget+1 }
    if length<1024 { budget=budget-1 }
    if st[NP_DOCS]>0.0 {
        if st[NP_LAST_GATE]>=0.5 { budget=budget+1 }
        if st[NP_LAST_GATE]<0.05 and st[NP_PLATEAU]>=2.0 { budget=budget-1 }
        if st[NP_EARLY]>st[NP_DOCS]/2.0 { budget=budget-1 }
    }
    if budget<1 { budget=1 }
    if budget>cap { budget=cap }
    return budget
}
fn noema_pretrain_auto_macros_suggest(st: *f64,length: i64,cap: i64) -> i64 { return np_auto_macros(st,length,cap) }
fn noema_pretrain_auto_macros_last(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_AUTO_LAST_MACROS] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_auto_macros_mean(st: *f64) -> f64 { if st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_COUNT]<=0.0 { return 0.0 } return st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_SUM]/st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_COUNT] }
fn noema_pretrain_auto_records(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_COUNT] as i64 }

fn np_margin(fplus: f64,fminus: f64) -> f64 { return 1.0/(1.0+np_exp(0.0-(fplus-fminus+NP_M)/0.25)) }
fn np_consolidate(st: *f64,ids: *f64,sig: *f64,gate: f64) {
    var s: i64=0
    while s<NP_K {
        let card=ids[s] as i64
        var norm: f64=0.0
        var stream: i64=0
        while stream<NP_B {
            var i: i64=0
            while i<np_n(st) {
                let at=np_at(s,stream,i,np_n(st))
                let x=st[np_array(st,0)+at]
                let y=st[np_array(st,1)+at]
                norm=norm+x*x+y*y
                i=i+1
            }
            stream=stream+1
        }
        let grad=norm/(NP_B*np_n(st)) as f64+NP_LAMBDA0
        st[np_sigma(st,card)]=@clamp(st[np_sigma(st,card)]-NP_ALPHA_S*gate*(NP_ETA0*grad+0.001*st[np_sigma(st,card)]),NP_SIG_MIN,NP_SIG_MAX)
        var j: i64=0
        while j<NP_SIG { st[np_sig(st,card,j)]=st[np_sig(st,card,j)]+NP_ALPHA_S*gate*(sig[j]-st[np_sig(st,card,j)]) j=j+1 }
        var d: i64=0
        while d<np_d(st) {
            let idx=np_bias(st,card,d)
            st[idx]=st[idx]-NP_ALPHA_B*gate*(NP_ETA0*grad+0.005*st[idx])
            var r: i64=0
            while r<NP_R {
                let ui=np_metric(st,card,r,d)
                st[ui]=st[ui]-NP_ALPHA_U*gate*(NP_ETA0*grad*st[ui]+0.005*st[ui])
                r=r+1
            }
            d=d+1
        }
        s=s+1
    }
    st[NP_LEARN_COUNT]=st[NP_LEARN_COUNT]+1.0
}
<veriayrimi>
fn noema_pretrain_document(st: *f64,bytes: *u8,length: i64,reset: i64,macros: i64) -> f64 {
    if length<=0 { return 0.0 }
    // Never silently evict a learned exemplar when the current allocation is
    // full. The caller can grow the state with noema_pretrain_expand_memory
    // and retry this document; physical allocation is the only upper bound.
    if st[NP_MEM_COUNT]>=np_mem_capacity(st) as f64 and np_memory_has_eligible_line(bytes,length)==1 { return 0.0-2.0 }
    noema_pretrain_reset(st,reset)
    let scratch=@ptr_add(st as *u8,np_scratch_off(st)*8) as *f64
    let sig=scratch
    let ids=@ptr_add(st as *u8,(np_scratch_off(st)+NP_SIG)*8) as *f64
    np_signature(bytes,length,sig)
    np_select(st,sig,ids)
    let do_mode=if (st[NP_DOCS] as i64)%NP_DO_EVERY==NP_DO_EVERY-1 { 1 } else { 0 }
    np_inject(st,bytes,length,ids,sig,do_mode,1)
    let before=np_energy(st,0)
    if np_finite(before)==0 {
        st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]=st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]+1.0
        return -1.0
    }
    var m: i64=0
    while m<macros {
        let dt=if m==0 { NP_DT_COARSE } else { NP_DT }
        var sub: i64=0
        while sub<4 {
            np_step(st,0,dt,st[NP_STAGE] as i64)
            if do_mode==1 { np_step(st,4,dt,st[NP_STAGE] as i64) }
            sub=sub+1
        }
        if m>0 {
            var velocity: f64=0.0
            var i: i64=0
            while i<np_field_len(st) { velocity=velocity+st[np_array(st,2)+i]*st[np_array(st,2)+i]+st[np_array(st,3)+i]*st[np_array(st,3)+i] i=i+1 }
            if @sqrt(velocity)<0.001 { st[NP_EARLY]=st[NP_EARLY]+1.0 m=macros } else { m=m+1 }
        } else { m=m+1 }
    }
    let after=np_energy(st,0)
    if np_finite(after)==0 {
        st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]=st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]+1.0
        return -1.0
    }
    let gate=np_gate(after-before,before)
    if do_mode==1 {
        let fp=np_energy(st,0)
        let fm=np_energy(st,4)
        let wm=np_margin(fp,fm)
        let h=st[NP_RINGHEAD] as i64
        st[np_ring(st,h,0)]=wm st[np_ring(st,h,1)]=fp st[np_ring(st,h,2)]=fm st[np_ring(st,h,3)]=st[NP_DOCS]
        st[NP_RINGHEAD]=((h+1)%NP_RING) as f64
        if st[NP_RINGCOUNT]<NP_RING as f64 { st[NP_RINGCOUNT]=st[NP_RINGCOUNT]+1.0 }
    }
    st[NP_KCOUNT]=st[NP_KCOUNT]+1.0
    if st[NP_KCOUNT]>=NP_KONS as f64 {
        st[NP_KCOUNT]=0.0
        if do_mode==0 or np_energy(st,4)-after<=NP_MARGIN*NP_M { np_consolidate(st,ids,sig,gate) }
    }
    // Consolidate accounting after each completed document.
    np_commit_document(st,length,before,after,gate,do_mode)
    np_memory_add(st,bytes,length)
    return after
}

fn noema_pretrain_infer(st: *f64,prompt: *u8,prompt_len: i64,output: *u8,capacity: i64,macros: i64) -> i64 {
    if prompt_len<=0 or capacity<=0 { return 0 }
    noema_pretrain_reset(st,0)
    let scratch=@ptr_add(st as *u8,np_scratch_off(st)*8) as *f64
    let sig=scratch
    let ids=@ptr_add(st as *u8,(np_scratch_off(st)+NP_SIG)*8) as *f64
    np_signature(prompt,prompt_len,sig)
    np_select(st,sig,ids)
    np_inject(st,prompt,prompt_len,ids,sig,0,0)
    let before=np_energy(st,0)
    if np_finite(before)==0 { st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]=st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]+1.0 return -1 }
    var m: i64=0
    while m<macros {
        let dt=if m==0 { NP_DT_COARSE } else { NP_DT }
        var sub: i64=0
        while sub<4 { np_step(st,0,dt,st[NP_STAGE] as i64) sub=sub+1 }
        m=m+1
    }
    let after=np_energy(st,0)
    if np_finite(after)==0 { st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]=st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES]+1.0 return -1 }
    st[NP_LAST_F]=after st[NP_LAST_GATE]=np_gate(after-before,before)
    st[NP_LAST_INPUT]=prompt_len as f64
    // Return a corpus-derived line only when its raw-byte resonance is strong
    // enough. There is no canned fallback text or synthetic byte stream.
    let generated=np_memory_retrieve(st,prompt,prompt_len,output,capacity)
    st[NP_LAST_OUTPUT]=generated as f64
    return generated
}

fn noema_pretrain_documents(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_DOCS] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_stage(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_STAGE] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_active_cavities() -> i64 { return NP_K }
fn noema_pretrain_batch_streams() -> i64 { return NP_B }
fn noema_pretrain_gate_mean(st: *f64) -> f64 { if st[NP_GN]<=0.0 { return 0.0 } return st[NP_GSUM]/st[NP_GN] }
fn noema_pretrain_do_count(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_DO] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_training_bytes(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_TRAIN_BYTES] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_last_energy(st: *f64) -> f64 { return st[NP_LAST_F] }
fn noema_pretrain_last_gate(st: *f64) -> f64 { return st[NP_LAST_GATE] }
fn noema_pretrain_last_output_bytes(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_LAST_OUTPUT] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_early_exits(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_EARLY] as i64 }
fn noema_pretrain_numeric_failures(st: *f64) -> i64 { return st[NP_NUMERIC_FAILURES] as i64 }

fn np_checkpoint_checksum(bytes: *u8,length: i64) -> i64 {
    var hash: i64=2166136261
    var i: i64=0
    while i<length {
        hash=@wrapping_mul(hash^(@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,i)) as i64),16777619)&4294967295
        i=i+1
    }
    return hash
}

fn np_read_exact(fd: i64,dst: *u8,n: i64) -> i64 {
    var got: i64=0
    while got<n { let r=@read(fd,@ptr_add(dst,got),n-got) if r<=0 { return got } got=got+r }
    return got
}
fn np_write_exact(fd: i64,src: *u8,n: i64) -> i64 {
    var sent: i64=0
    while sent<n { let r=@write(fd,@ptr_add(src,sent),n-sent) if r<=0 { return sent } sent=sent+r }
    return sent
}

// Zero-parse .nbin prototype record: u64 LE length, reset u8, domain u8, bytes.
fn noema_pretrain_nbin_write(fd: i64,bytes: *u8,length: i64,reset: i64,domain: i64) -> i64 {
    if length<0 or reset<0 or reset>1 or domain<0 or domain>2 { return -1 }
    let header=@malloc(10) as *u8
    var i: i64=0
    while i<8 { @write_u8(@ptr_add(header,i),((length>>(i*8))&255) as u8) i=i+1 }
    @write_u8(@ptr_add(header,8),reset as u8) @write_u8(@ptr_add(header,9),domain as u8)
    let h=np_write_exact(fd,header,10)
    @free(header)
    if h!=10 { return -1 }
    return np_write_exact(fd,bytes,length)
}
fn np_pretrain_nbin_train_internal(st: *f64,fd: i64,max_records: i64,max_payload: i64,macros: i64,auto_mode: i64) -> i64 {
    if max_records<0 or max_payload<=0 or (auto_mode!=0 and auto_mode!=1) { return -1 }
    if auto_mode==1 and (macros<=0 or macros>16) { return -1 }
    let header=@malloc(10) as *u8
    let buf0=@malloc(max_payload) as *u8
    let buf1=@malloc(max_payload) as *u8
    var count: i64=0
    var which: i64=0
    var done: i64=0
    while count<max_records and done==0 {
        let nr=np_read_exact(fd,header,10)
        if nr==0 { done=1 }
        else if nr!=10 { done=2 }
        else {
            var length: i64=0
            var j: i64=0
            while j<8 { length=length|((@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,j)) as i64)<<(j*8)) j=j+1 }
            let reset=@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,8)) as i64
            let domain=@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,9)) as i64
            if length<0 or length>max_payload or reset>1 or domain>2 { done=2 }
            else {
                var dst=buf0
                if which==1 { dst=buf1 }
                if np_read_exact(fd,dst,length)!=length { done=2 }
                else {
                    let chosen=if auto_mode==1 { np_auto_macros(st,length,macros) } else { macros }
                    if chosen<0 or (auto_mode==1 and chosen==0) { done=2 }
                    else {
                        st[NP_DOMAIN]=domain as f64
                        let energy=noema_pretrain_document(st,dst,length,reset,chosen)
                        if energy<0.0 { done=2 }
                        else {
                            count=count+1
                            which=1-which
                            if auto_mode==1 {
                                st[NP_AUTO_LAST_MACROS]=chosen as f64
                                st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_SUM]=st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_SUM]+chosen as f64
                                st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_COUNT]=st[NP_AUTO_MACRO_COUNT]+1.0
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    @free(header) @free(buf0) @free(buf1)
    if done==2 { return -1 }
    return count
}
fn noema_pretrain_nbin_train(st: *f64,fd: i64,max_records: i64,max_payload: i64,macros: i64) -> i64 {
    return np_pretrain_nbin_train_internal(st,fd,max_records,max_payload,macros,0)
}
fn noema_pretrain_nbin_train_auto(st: *f64,fd: i64,max_records: i64,max_payload: i64,macro_cap: i64) -> i64 {
    return np_pretrain_nbin_train_internal(st,fd,max_records,max_payload,macro_cap,1)
}
fn noema_pretrain_nbin_record(st: *f64,fd: i64,macros: i64) -> i64 {
    let header=@malloc(10) as *u8
    let got=np_read_exact(fd,header,10)
    if got==0 { @free(header) return 0 }
    if got!=10 { @free(header) return -1 }
    var length: i64=0
    var i: i64=0
    while i<8 { length=length|((@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,i)) as i64)<<(i*8)) i=i+1 }
    let reset=@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,8)) as i64
    let domain=@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,9)) as i64
    @free(header)
    if length<0 or reset>1 or domain>2 { return -1 }
    let bytes=@malloc(if length>0 { length } else { 1 }) as *u8
    if np_read_exact(fd,bytes,length)!=length { @free(bytes) return -1 }
    st[NP_DOMAIN]=domain as f64
    let energy=noema_pretrain_document(st,bytes,length,reset,macros)
    @free(bytes)
    return if energy<0.0 { -1 } else { 1 }
}

fn np_pack_topic(fd: i64,bytes: *u8,raw_start: i64,raw_end: i64) -> i64 {
    if raw_start<0 or raw_end<raw_start { return -1 }
    var i=raw_start
    var has_content:i64=0
    while i<raw_end {
        let c=@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes,i)) as i64
        if c!=10 and c!=13 and c!=32 and c!=9 { has_content=1 }
        i=i+1
    }
    if has_content==0 { return 0 }
    return noema_pretrain_nbin_write(fd,@ptr_add(bytes,raw_start),raw_end-raw_start,0,0)
}

fn noema_pretrain_pack_topics(fd: i64,bytes: *u8,length: i64,payload_bytes: *i64) -> i64 {
    payload_bytes[0]=0
    if length<0 { return -1 }
    var start: i64=0
    var pos: i64=0
    var count: i64=0
    while pos < length {
        if np_is_veriayrimi(bytes, length, pos) == 1 {
            let wrote = np_pack_topic(fd, bytes, start, pos)
            if wrote < 0 { return -1 }
            if wrote > 0 { count = count + 1 payload_bytes[0] = payload_bytes[0] + wrote }
            // Omit trailing line-ending when <veriayrimi> stands alone on its line
            var next_pos = pos + 12
            if pos == 0 or ((@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes, pos - 1)) as i64) & 255) == 10 {
                if next_pos < length and ((@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes, next_pos)) as i64) & 255) == 10 {
                    next_pos = next_pos + 1
                } else if (next_pos + 1) < length and ((@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes, next_pos)) as i64) & 255) == 13 and ((@read_u8(@ptr_add(bytes, next_pos + 1)) as i64) & 255) == 10 {
                    next_pos = next_pos + 2
                }
            }
            start = next_pos
            pos = next_pos
        } else {
            pos = pos + 1
        }
    }
    let last=np_pack_topic(fd,bytes,start,length)
    if last<0 { return -1 }
    if last>0 { count=count+1 payload_bytes[0]=payload_bytes[0]+last }
    return count
}

// Flat state snapshot with payload checksum. v4 sizes exemplar memory from the
// checkpoint; v1-v3 remain readable.
fn noema_pretrain_save(st: *f64,path: string) -> i64 {
    let floats=np_state_floats_cap(np_c(st),np_d(st),np_n(st),np_mem_capacity(st))
    var i:i64=0
    while i<floats { if np_finite(st[i])==0 { return 0 } i=i+1 }
    let fd=@open(path,577,420)
    if fd<0 { return 0 }
    let header=@malloc(48) as *u8
    @memset(header,0,48)
    @write_u8(header,78) @write_u8(@ptr_add(header,1),80) @write_u8(@ptr_add(header,2),83) @write_u8(@ptr_add(header,3),49)
    @write_u32(@ptr_add(header,4),4 as u32)
    @write_u64(@ptr_add(header,8),np_c(st) as u64)
    @write_u64(@ptr_add(header,16),np_d(st) as u64)
    @write_u64(@ptr_add(header,24),np_n(st) as u64)
    @write_u64(@ptr_add(header,32),floats as u64)
    @write_u64(@ptr_add(header,40),np_checkpoint_checksum(st as *u8,floats*8) as u64)
    let h=np_write_exact(fd,header,48)
    @free(header)
    if h!=48 { @close(fd) return 0 }
    let n=np_write_exact(fd,st as *u8,floats*8)
    @close(fd)
    return if n==floats*8 { 1 } else { 0 }
}
fn noema_pretrain_load(path: string) -> *f64 {
    let fd=@open(path,0,0)
    if fd<0 { return 0 as *f64 }
    let header=@malloc(40) as *u8
    if np_read_exact(fd,header,40)!=40 { @free(header) @close(fd) return 0 as *f64 }
    let magic=(@read_u8(header) as i64)==78 and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,1)) as i64)==80 and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,2)) as i64)==83 and (@read_u8(@ptr_add(header,3)) as i64)==49
    let version=@read_u32(@ptr_add(header,4)) as i64
    let c=@read_u64(@ptr_add(header,8)) as i64
    let d=@read_u64(@ptr_add(header,16)) as i64
    let n=@read_u64(@ptr_add(header,24)) as i64
    let floats=@read_u64(@ptr_add(header,32)) as i64
    @free(header)
    if magic==false or version<1 or version>4 or c<NP_C_MIN or d<2 or n<8 or floats<=0 or floats>NP_MAX_STATE_FLOATS { @close(fd) return 0 as *f64 }
    let legacy_floats=np_state_floats_legacy(c,d,n)
    if legacy_floats<=0 { @close(fd) return 0 as *f64 }
    var memory_slots=NP_MEM_SLOTS
    var expected_floats=legacy_floats
    if version==3 { expected_floats=np_state_floats(c,d,n) }
    if version>=4 {
        let per_slot=1+NP_MEM_FEATURES+NP_MEM_BYTES/8
        let extra=floats-np_state_floats_legacy(c,d,n)-NP_MEM_FEATURES
        if extra<=0 or extra%per_slot!=0 { @close(fd) return 0 as *f64 }
        memory_slots=extra/per_slot
        if memory_slots<=0 { @close(fd) return 0 as *f64 }
        expected_floats=np_state_floats_cap(c,d,n,memory_slots)
    }
    if floats!=expected_floats { @close(fd) return 0 as *f64 }
    var expected_checksum: i64=0
    if version>=2 {
        let checksum_bytes=@malloc(8) as *u8
        if np_read_exact(fd,checksum_bytes,8)!=8 { @free(checksum_bytes) @close(fd) return 0 as *f64 }
        expected_checksum=@read_u64(checksum_bytes) as i64
        @free(checksum_bytes)
    }
    let st=noema_pretrain_create_with_memory(c,d,n,1,memory_slots)
    let got=np_read_exact(fd,st as *u8,floats*8)
    let extra=@malloc(1) as *u8
    let trailing=@read(fd,extra,1)
    @free(extra) @close(fd)
    if got!=floats*8 or trailing!=0 { noema_pretrain_destroy(st) return 0 as *f64 }
    if version>=2 and np_checkpoint_checksum(st as *u8,floats*8)!=expected_checksum { noema_pretrain_destroy(st) return 0 as *f64 }
    var i:i64=0
    while i<floats { if np_finite(st[i])==0 { noema_pretrain_destroy(st) return 0 as *f64 } i=i+1 }
    return st
}
<veriayrimi>
// NOEMA Stage 9–13: bounded exact-rule inference, query status and proof checks.
const REASON_INVALID: i64 = -1
const REASON_MAX_FACTS: i64 = 256
const REASON_MAX_RULES: i64 = 1024
const QUERY_INVALID: i64 = -1
const QUERY_UNKNOWN: i64 = 0
const QUERY_SUPPORTED: i64 = 1
const QUERY_REFUTED: i64 = 2
const QUERY_CONFLICT: i64 = 3
const PROOF_ROOT: i64 = -1
const PROOF_NO_PARENT: i64 = -1

fn reasoning_closure(initial_count: i64, initial_facts: *u8, rule_count: i64, antecedents: *u8, consequents: *u8, capacity: i64, output_facts: *u8) -> i64 {
    if initial_count < 0 or initial_count > REASON_MAX_FACTS or rule_count < 0 or rule_count > REASON_MAX_RULES { return REASON_INVALID }
    if capacity < initial_count or capacity > REASON_MAX_FACTS { return REASON_INVALID }
    var count: i64 = 0
    var i: i64 = 0
    while i < initial_count {
        let fact = @read_u64(@ptr_add(initial_facts, i * 8)) as i64
        if fact <= 0 { return REASON_INVALID }
        var j: i64 = 0
        while j < count { if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, j * 8)) as i64) == fact { return REASON_INVALID } j = j + 1 }
        @write_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, count * 8), fact as u64)
        count = count + 1
        i = i + 1
    }
    i = 0
    while i < rule_count {
        if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(antecedents, i * 8)) as i64) <= 0 or (@read_u64(@ptr_add(consequents, i * 8)) as i64) <= 0 { return REASON_INVALID }
        i = i + 1
    }
    var changed = 1
    while changed == 1 {
        changed = 0
        var r: i64 = 0
        while r < rule_count {
            let antecedent = @read_u64(@ptr_add(antecedents, r * 8)) as i64
            let consequent = @read_u64(@ptr_add(consequents, r * 8)) as i64
            var has_a = 0
            var has_c = 0
            var f: i64 = 0
            while f < count {
                let value = @read_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, f * 8)) as i64
                if value == antecedent { has_a = 1 }
                if value == consequent { has_c = 1 }
                f = f + 1
            }
            if has_a == 1 and has_c == 0 {
                if count >= capacity { return REASON_INVALID }
                @write_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, count * 8), consequent as u64)
                count = count + 1
                changed = 1
            }
            r = r + 1
        }
    }
    return count
}

fn reasoning_closure2(initial_count: i64, initial_facts: *u8, rule_count: i64, left_terms: *u8, right_terms: *u8, conclusions: *u8, capacity: i64, output_facts: *u8) -> i64 {
    if initial_count < 0 or initial_count > REASON_MAX_FACTS or rule_count < 0 or rule_count > REASON_MAX_RULES { return REASON_INVALID }
    if capacity < initial_count or capacity > REASON_MAX_FACTS { return REASON_INVALID }
    var count: i64 = 0
    var i: i64 = 0
    while i < initial_count {
        let fact = @read_u64(@ptr_add(initial_facts, i * 8)) as i64
        if fact <= 0 { return REASON_INVALID }
        var j: i64 = 0
        while j < count { if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, j * 8)) as i64) == fact { return REASON_INVALID } j = j + 1 }
        @write_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, count * 8), fact as u64)
        count = count + 1
        i = i + 1
    }
    i = 0
    while i < rule_count {
        if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(left_terms, i * 8)) as i64) <= 0 or (@read_u64(@ptr_add(conclusions, i * 8)) as i64) <= 0 { return REASON_INVALID }
        if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(right_terms, i * 8)) as i64) < 0 { return REASON_INVALID }
        i = i + 1
    }
    var changed = 1
    while changed == 1 {
        changed = 0
        var r: i64 = 0
        while r < rule_count {
            let left = @read_u64(@ptr_add(left_terms, r * 8)) as i64
            let right = @read_u64(@ptr_add(right_terms, r * 8)) as i64
            let conclusion = @read_u64(@ptr_add(conclusions, r * 8)) as i64
            var has_left = 0
            var has_right = if right == 0 { 1 } else { 0 }
            var has_conclusion = 0
            var f: i64 = 0
            while f < count {
                let value = @read_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, f * 8)) as i64
                if value == left { has_left = 1 }
                if value == right { has_right = 1 }
                if value == conclusion { has_conclusion = 1 }
                f = f + 1
            }
            if has_left == 1 and has_right == 1 and has_conclusion == 0 {
                if count >= capacity { return REASON_INVALID }
                @write_u64(@ptr_add(output_facts, count * 8), conclusion as u64)
                count = count + 1
                changed = 1
            }
            r = r + 1
        }
    }
    return count
}

fn reasoning_fact_present(fact_count: i64, facts: *u8, query: i64) -> i64 {
    if fact_count < 0 or fact_count > REASON_MAX_FACTS or query <= 0 { return 0 }
    var i: i64 = 0
    while i < fact_count { if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(facts, i * 8)) as i64) == query { return 1 } i = i + 1 }
    return 0
}

fn reasoning_derivation_valid(query: i64, rule_index: i64, premise_count: i64, premises: *u8, rule_count: i64, left_terms: *u8, right_terms: *u8, conclusions: *u8) -> i64 {
    if query <= 0 or rule_index < 0 or rule_index >= rule_count or premise_count < 0 or premise_count > REASON_MAX_FACTS { return 0 }
    if rule_count < 0 or rule_count > REASON_MAX_RULES { return 0 }
    if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(conclusions, rule_index * 8)) as i64) != query { return 0 }
    let left = @read_u64(@ptr_add(left_terms, rule_index * 8)) as i64
    let right = @read_u64(@ptr_add(right_terms, rule_index * 8)) as i64
    if left <= 0 or right < 0 { return 0 }
    if reasoning_fact_present(premise_count, premises, left) == 0 { return 0 }
    if right != 0 and reasoning_fact_present(premise_count, premises, right) == 0 { return 0 }
    return 1
}

fn reasoning_query_status(fact_count: i64, facts: *u8, positive_id: i64, contrary_id: i64) -> i64 {
    if fact_count < 0 or fact_count > REASON_MAX_FACTS or positive_id <= 0 or contrary_id <= 0 or positive_id == contrary_id { return QUERY_INVALID }
    var has_positive = 0
    var has_contrary = 0
    var i: i64 = 0
    while i < fact_count {
        let fact = @read_u64(@ptr_add(facts, i * 8)) as i64
        if fact <= 0 { return QUERY_INVALID }
        if fact == positive_id { has_positive = 1 }
        if fact == contrary_id { has_contrary = 1 }
        i = i + 1
    }
    if has_positive == 1 and has_contrary == 1 { return QUERY_CONFLICT }
    if has_positive == 1 { return QUERY_SUPPORTED }
    if has_contrary == 1 { return QUERY_REFUTED }
    return QUERY_UNKNOWN
}

/// Certificate layout: each step is four u64 cells [fact, rule, parent-left,
/// parent-right]. Roots use rule=-1 and both parents=-1. Derived steps reference
/// earlier step indexes; all parent facts must match that rule's exact premises.
/// Validates acyclic ordering and rule linkage, not authenticity of input facts.
fn reasoning_proof_valid(step_count: i64, steps: *u8, initial_count: i64, initial_facts: *u8, rule_count: i64, left_terms: *u8, right_terms: *u8, conclusions: *u8) -> i64 {
    if step_count < 0 or step_count > REASON_MAX_FACTS or initial_count < 0 or initial_count > REASON_MAX_FACTS { return 0 }
    if rule_count < 0 or rule_count > REASON_MAX_RULES { return 0 }
    var s: i64 = 0
    while s < step_count {
        let base = s * 32
        let fact = @read_u64(@ptr_add(steps, base)) as i64
        let rule = @read_u64(@ptr_add(steps, base + 8)) as i64
        let parent_a = @read_u64(@ptr_add(steps, base + 16)) as i64
        let parent_b = @read_u64(@ptr_add(steps, base + 24)) as i64
        if fact <= 0 { return 0 }
        var prior: i64 = 0
        while prior < s {
            if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(steps, prior * 32)) as i64) == fact { return 0 }
            prior = prior + 1
        }
        if rule == PROOF_ROOT {
            if parent_a != PROOF_NO_PARENT or parent_b != PROOF_NO_PARENT { return 0 }
            if reasoning_fact_present(initial_count, initial_facts, fact) == 0 { return 0 }
        } else {
            if rule < 0 or rule >= rule_count { return 0 }
            if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(conclusions, rule * 8)) as i64) != fact { return 0 }
            let left = @read_u64(@ptr_add(left_terms, rule * 8)) as i64
            let right = @read_u64(@ptr_add(right_terms, rule * 8)) as i64
            if left <= 0 or right < 0 { return 0 }
            if parent_a < 0 or parent_a >= s { return 0 }
            if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(steps, parent_a * 32)) as i64) != left { return 0 }
            if right == 0 {
                if parent_b != PROOF_NO_PARENT { return 0 }
            } else {
                if parent_b < 0 or parent_b >= s { return 0 }
                if (@read_u64(@ptr_add(steps, parent_b * 32)) as i64) != right { return 0 }
            }
        }
        s = s + 1
    }
    return 1
}
<veriayrimi>
// Explicit symbolic and natural-language integer arithmetic tools.
// Solves arithmetic deterministically to prevent hallucinated numbers.
fn noema_chat_has_substr(prompt: *u8, length: i64, needle: string) -> i64 {
    let nlen = needle.len as i64
    if length < nlen { return 0 }
    var i: i64 = 0
    while i <= length - nlen {
        var is_match: i64 = 1
        var j: i64 = 0
        while j < nlen {
            var c1 = @read_u8(@ptr_add(prompt, i + j)) as i64
            var c2 = @read_u8(@ptr_add(needle.ptr as *u8, j)) as i64
            if c1 >= 65 and c1 <= 90 { c1 = c1 + 32 }
            if c2 >= 65 and c2 <= 90 { c2 = c2 + 32 }
            if c1 != c2 { is_match = 0; break }
            j = j + 1
        }
        if is_match == 1 { return 1 }
        i = i + 1
    }
    return 0
}


fn noema_chat_math_operation(prompt: *u8, length: i64) -> i64 {
    if length <= 0 or length > 4096 { return 0 }

    // Do not route causal, code, or underdetermined queries to binary arithmetic.
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "neden") == 1 { return 0 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "lir") == 1 { return 0 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "kod") == 1 { return 0 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "alan") == 1 { return 0 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "çevre") == 1 { return 0 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "hız") == 1 { return 0 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "derinlik") == 1 { return 0 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "fiyat") == 1 { return 0 }

    var operation: i64 = 0
    var i: i64 = 0
    while i < length {
        let ch = @read_u8(@ptr_add(prompt, i)) as i64
        var found: i64 = 0
        if ch == 43 { found = 1 }
        else if ch == 45 { found = 2 }
        else if ch == 42 { found = 3 }
        else if ch == 47 { found = 4 }
        if found != 0 {
            if operation != 0 and operation != found { return 5 }
            operation = found
        }
        i = i + 1
    }
    if operation != 0 { return operation }


    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "toplam") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "artı") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "ekle") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "daha geldi") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "daha bindi") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "daha kondu") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "daha dikildi") == 1 { return 1 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "fark") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "eksi") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "çıkar") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "kaldı") == 1 { return 2 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "çarp") == 1 or noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "katı") == 1 { return 3 }
    if noema_chat_has_substr(prompt, length, "böl") == 1 { return 4 }

    return 0
}

fn noema_chat_arithmetic(prompt: *u8, length: i64) -> i64 {
    let operation = noema_chat_math_operation(prompt, length)
    if operation <= 0 or operation > 4 { return -1 }
    var count: i64 = 0
    var first: i64 = 0
    var second: i64 = 0
    var sum: i64 = 0
    var pos: i64 = 0
    while pos < length {
        let ch = @read_u8(@ptr_add(prompt, pos)) as i64
        if ch >= 48 and ch <= 57 {
            var value: i64 = 0
            while pos < length {
                let digit_byte = @read_u8(@ptr_add(prompt, pos)) as i64
                if digit_byte < 48 or digit_byte > 57 { break }
                let digit = digit_byte - 48
                if value > 10000000 or (value == 10000000 and digit > 0) { return -1 }
                value = value * 10 + digit
                pos = pos + 1
            }
            if count == 0 { first = value } else if count == 1 { second = value }
            sum = sum + value
            count = count + 1
        } else { pos = pos + 1 }
    }
    if count < 2 { return -1 }
    if operation == 1 { return sum }
    if count != 2 { return -1 }
    if operation == 2 { if first < second { return -1 } return first - second }
    if operation == 3 { return first * second }
    if operation == 4 { if second == 0 or first % second != 0 { return -1 } return first / second }
    return -1
}
<veriayrimi>
// Canonical LIR tour — every construct here is listed as "supported" by
// `lir lang` and is exercised by tests/ai_test.py on all backends.
//
// Run:  lir run examples/canonical-tour.lir --backend=vm
// Expected output is asserted in tests/ai_test.py (EXAMPLES entry).

import core

// ── 1. Global constant, comptime evaluation, bounded type ───────────────────
const LIMIT: i64 = 3 ** 2

type Percent = i64 range 0..100

comptime fn fib(n: i64) -> i64 {
    if n <= 1 { return n }
    return fib(n - 1) + fib(n - 2)
}

const TENTH_FIB = fib(10)

// ── 2. Attributes: pure function, contract ──────────────────────────────────
#[pure]
fn square(x: i64) -> i64 {
    return x * x
}

#[pre(b != 0)]
fn divide(a: i64, b: i64) -> i64 {
    return a / b
}

// ── 3. Struct + impl methods ───────────────────────────────────────────────
struct Counter {
    value: i64
}

impl Counter {
    fn get(self) -> i64 {
        return self.value
    }
    fn bump(self: *mut Counter) -> i64 {
        self.value = self.value + 1
        return self.value
    }
}

// ── 4. Enum with payloads + trait + dyn dispatch ───────────────────────────
enum Shape {
    Square(i64),
    Rectangle(i64, i64),
    Empty,
}

trait Area {
    fn area(self) -> i64
}

struct Tile {
    side: i64
}

impl Area for Tile {
    fn area(self) -> i64 {
        return self.side * self.side
    }
}

fn measure(shape: dyn Area) -> i64 {
    return shape.area()
}

fn describe(shape: Shape) -> i64 {
    return match shape {
        Shape::Square(side) => side * side,
        Shape::Rectangle(w, h) if w == h => w * h,
        Shape::Rectangle(w, h) => w + h,
        Shape::Empty => 0,
    }
}

// ── 5. Option / Result with ? propagation and ?? default ───────────────────
fn maybe(ok: bool) -> Option<i64> {
    if ok { return Some(7) }
    return None
}

fn doubled(ok: bool) -> Option<i64> {
    let v = maybe(ok)?
    return Some(v * 2)
}

fn attempt() -> Result<i64, i64> {
    return Ok(divide(20, 4))
}

fn main() -> i64 {
    // control flow
    let mut total = 0
    for i in 1..=4 {
        total += i
    }
    let mut countdown = 3
    while countdown > 0 {
        countdown -= 1
    }
    loop {
        countdown += 1
        if countdown == 2 { break }
    }

    // expressions, casts, ternary, interpolation
    let label = total > 5 ? "big" : "small"
    println("control total={total} label={label} down={countdown}")
    println("comptime fib(10)={TENTH_FIB} limit={LIMIT}")
    printInt(square(6))
    printInt(300 as u8 as i64)
    println("")

    // struct methods
    var counter = Counter { value: 0 }
    counter.bump()
    printInt(counter.get())
    printInt(counter.bump())
    println("")

    // enums, traits, dyn
    printInt(describe(Shape::Square(4)))
    printInt(describe(Shape::Rectangle(3, 3)))
    printInt(describe(Shape::Rectangle(2, 5)))
    printInt(describe(Shape::Empty))
    let tile = Tile { side: 5 }
    printInt(measure(tile))
    println("")

    // option / result
    let missing: ?i64 = None
    printInt(maybe(true) ?? 0)
    printInt(missing ?? 9)
    printInt(match doubled(false) { Some(v) => v, None => -1 })
    printInt(match attempt() { Ok(v) => v, Err(e) => e })
    println("")

    // closures and function values
    let step = 3
    let add = |x: i64| x + step
    let seven = || 7
    printInt(add(seven()))
    println("")

    // collections
    let items = @array_new(4, 8)
    @array_push(items, 4)
    @array_push(items, 5)
    let mut sum = 0
    for v in items {
        sum += v
    }
    let squares = [i * i for i in 0..3]
    let map = #{ "one": 1 }
    @map_set(map, "two", 2)
    printInt(sum)
    printInt(@array_len(squares))
    printInt(@map_get(map, "one") + @map_get(map, "two"))
    @array_free(items)
    @array_free(squares)
    @map_free(map)
    println("")

    // tuples
    let pair = (4, true)
    let (number, ok) = pair
    if ok { printInt(number) }
    println("")

    // concurrency: atomics (threads and channels need a host target; wasm32-wasi
    // rejects them with LIR-E7004, so the portable tour uses atomics only)
    let slot = @malloc(8)
    @atomic_add(slot, 40)
    @atomic_add(slot, 2)
    printInt(@read_u64(slot) as i64)
    @free(slot)
    println("")

    // bounded type + defer ordering
    let percent: Percent = 55
    defer println("deferred-last")
    defer println("deferred-first")
    printInt(percent)
    println("")

    return @min(total, 100) - 10
}
<veriayrimi>
// TCP Echo Server using raw Linux syscalls
// Binds to port 8080, echoes back any data received

fn main() {
    // Create socket: socket(AF_INET=2, SOCK_STREAM=1, 0)
    let sockfd = @syscall(41, 2, 1, 0)
    printInt(sockfd)

    // Build sockaddr_in (16 bytes) on heap
    let addr = @malloc(64)
    @memset(addr, 0, 64)

    // sin_family = AF_INET (2) at offset 0 (i64 store, LE bytes: 02 00 ...)
    let p0 = @ptr_add(addr, 0)
    *p0 = 2

    // sin_port = 8080 in network byte order at offset 2
    // 8080 = 0x1F90. Big-endian bytes: 0x1F, 0x90
    // On little-endian i64 store: value 0x901F gives bytes 1F, 90
    // 0x901F = 36895 in decimal
    let p2 = @ptr_add(addr, 2)
    *p2 = 36895

    // sin_addr = INADDR_ANY (0) at offset 4 — already zeroed

    // Bind: bind(sockfd, addr, 16)
    let br = @syscall(49, sockfd, addr, 16)
    printInt(br)

    // Listen: listen(sockfd, 5)
    let lr = @syscall(50, sockfd, 5, 0)
    printInt(lr)

    println("Server listening on port 8080...")

    // Accept loop
    while true {
        let client_fd = @syscall(43, sockfd, 0, 0)
        printInt(client_fd)

        if client_fd > 0 {
            println("Client connected!")
            handle_client(client_fd)
            @syscall(3, client_fd, 0, 0)
            println("Client disconnected.")
        }
    }

    exit(0)
}

fn handle_client(fd: i64) {
    let buf = @malloc(1024)
    defer @free(buf)

    while true {
        // read(fd, buf, 1024)
        let n = @syscall(0, fd, buf, 1024)
        if n <= 0 {
            return
        }
        // echo back: write(fd, buf, n)
        @syscall(1, fd, buf, n)
    }
}

// File I/O Demo using @open, @read, @write, @close, @stat
fn main() {
    // --- Write a file ---
    // @open("test_output.txt", O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC=0x241, 0644=0x1A4)
    let fd = @open("test_output.txt", 577, 420)
    printInt(fd)

    // @write(fd, "Hello from LIR!\n", 16)
    let msg = "Hello from LIR!\n"
    let written = @write(fd, msg, 16)
    printInt(written)

    // @close(fd)
    @close(fd)

    // --- Read the file back ---
    let fd2 = @open("test_output.txt", 0, 0)
    printInt(fd2)

    let buf = @malloc(256)
    let n = @read(fd2, buf, 256)
    printInt(n)

    // Print what we read
    @write(1, buf, n)

    @close(fd2)

    // --- Stat a file ---
    let st = @malloc(144) // sizeof(struct stat)
    let sr = @stat("test_output.txt", st)
    printInt(sr)

    // st_size is at offset 48 in struct stat on x86-64
    let size_ptr = @ptr_add(st, 48)
    printInt(*size_ptr)

    // --- Overwrite with different content ---
    let fd3 = @open("test_output.txt", 577, 420)
    let msg2 = "LIR is awesome!\n"
    @write(fd3, msg2, 16)
    @close(fd3)

    // Read it back
    let fd4 = @open("test_output.txt", 0, 0)
    let n2 = @read(fd4, buf, 256)
    @write(1, buf, n2)
    @close(fd4)

    // Cleanup
    // In LIR we can't unlink yet, so the file stays.
    println("File I/O demo complete!")

    exit(0)
}

# LIR v2 language features demo

fn main() -> i64 {
    # for-range loops
    let mut total = 0
    for i in 0..10 {
        total += i
    }
    printInt(total)          # 45

    # nested range loop with break/continue
    let mut hits = 0
    for a in 0..5 {
        for b in 0..5 {
            if b > a { continue }
            if a == 4 { break }
            hits += 1
        }
    }
    printInt(hits)           # 1+2+3+4 = 10

    # arrays with indexing syntax
    let arr = @array_new(8, 8)
    for i in 0..5 {
        arr[i] = i * i
    }
    arr[2] += 100            # 4 + 100 = 104
    printInt(arr[2])         # 104
    printInt(arr[4])         # 16

    # for-in over array
    let mut sum = 0
    for v in arr {
        sum += v
    }
    printInt(sum)            # 0+1+104+9+16 = 130

    # ternary expressions
    let x = 7
    let label = x > 5 ? 1 : 0
    printInt(label)          # 1
    printInt(x % 2 == 0 ? 111 : 222)  # 222

    # string concatenation with +
    let greeting = "Hello, " + "LIR"
    println(greeting)        # Hello, LIR
    println("v" + "2")

    # string equality with == / !=
    if greeting == "Hello, LIR" { printInt(1) } else { printInt(0) }
    if "abc" != "abd" { printInt(1) } else { printInt(0) }

    0
}
<veriayrimi>
// Threading and Mutex Demo in LIR

fn worker(counter: *i64) -> i64 {
    var i = 0
    while i < 100000 {
        let cur = *counter
        *counter = cur + 1
        i = i + 1
    }
    return 0
}

fn mutex_worker(mtx: *i64) -> i64 {
    var i = 0
    while i < 100000 {
        @mutex_lock(mtx)
        let cur = *mtx
        *mtx = cur + 1
        @mutex_unlock(mtx)
        i = i + 1
    }
    return 0
}

fn main() {
    println("=== Thread Demo ===")

    // Part 1: Race condition (no mutex)
    let counter = @malloc(16)
    *counter = 0

    let t1 = @thread_create("worker", counter)
    let t2 = @thread_create("worker", counter)
    let t3 = @thread_create("worker", counter)
    let t4 = @thread_create("worker", counter)

    @thread_join(t1)
    @thread_join(t2)
    @thread_join(t3)
    @thread_join(t4)

    print("Without mutex: ")
    printInt(*counter)
    println(" (expected 400000, likely less due to races)")

    // Part 2: Mutex-protected
    let mtx = @mutex_create()
    *mtx = 0

    let m1 = @thread_create("mutex_worker", mtx)
    let m2 = @thread_create("mutex_worker", mtx)
    let m3 = @thread_create("mutex_worker", mtx)
    let m4 = @thread_create("mutex_worker", mtx)

    @thread_join(m1)
    @thread_join(m2)
    @thread_join(m3)
    @thread_join(m4)

    print("With mutex:    ")
    printInt(*mtx)
    println(" (expected 400000)")

    println("Thread demo complete!")
    exit(0)
}
